import { Cell, Address } from "@ton/core";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";

// ============================================================
// CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================
//
// IMPORTANT:
// rebuildFreshV22 saves rooster_v22_generated.boc as RAW binary
// BOC, NOT as Base64 text.
//
// This decoder is specifically aligned with the STON.fi v2.2
// SDK structure found in @ston-fi/sdk 2.7.0.
//
// READ ONLY
// NO TRANSACTION
// NO SIGNATURE
//

const BOC_PATH = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "rooster_v22_generated.boc"
);

// ============================================================
// EXPECTED VALUES
// ============================================================

const EXPECTED = {
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Jetton Transfer
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  jettonTransferOpcode: 0x0f8a7ea5,

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // STON.fi v2.2 PROVIDE_LP
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  provideLpOpcode: 0x37c096df,

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Fresh build
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  roosterAmount: 100000000000n,

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Actual STON.fi v2.2 Router
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  routerAddress:
    "EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3",

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Connected wallet / response / refund / excesses
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  userAddress:
    "EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix",

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // pTON jetton wallet owned by STON.fi Router
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  routerPtonWallet:
    "EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3",

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Fresh build
  // 95% minimum LP
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  minLpOut: 150109265n,

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // STON.fi v2.2 SDK 2.7.0:
  //
  // provideLpJetton.forwardGasAmount = 0.235 TON
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  forwardTonAmount: 235000000n,

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Fresh build deadline
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  deadline: 1790697420n,

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // v2.2 flags
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  bothPositive: true,

  // No custom DEX payload in fresh build
  dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount: 0n,

  hasDexCustomPayload: false,
};

// ============================================================
// HELPERS
// ============================================================

function check(
  label: string,
  actual: unknown,
  expected: unknown
): boolean {
  if (actual === expected) {
    console.log(
      `PASS | ${label} | actual=${String(actual)} | expected=${String(expected)}`
    );

    return true;
  }

  console.log(
    `FAIL | ${label} | actual=${String(actual)} | expected=${String(expected)}`
  );

  return false;
}

// ============================================================
// ADDRESS HELPERS
// ============================================================

function addressString(
  address: Address | null
): string {
  if (!address) {
    return "null";
  }

  return address.toString({
    bounceable: true,
    urlSafe: true,
  });
}

function checkAddress(
  label: string,
  actual: Address | null,
  expected: string
): boolean {
  console.log(label);

  if (!actual) {
    console.log(
      `       actual:   null`
    );

    console.log(
      `       expected: ${expected}`
    );

    console.log(
      `FAIL | ${label}`
    );

    return false;
  }

  const actualString =
    addressString(actual);

  console.log(
    `       actual:   ${actualString}`
  );

  console.log(
    `       expected: ${expected}`
  );

  const actualRaw =
    actual.toRawString();

  const expectedRaw =
    Address.parse(expected).toRawString();

  if (actualRaw === expectedRaw) {
    console.log(
      `PASS | ${label}`
    );

    return true;
  }

  console.log(
    `       actual RAW:   ${actualRaw}`
  );

  console.log(
    `       expected RAW: ${expectedRaw}`
  );

  console.log(
    `FAIL | ${label}`
  );

  return false;
}

// ============================================================
// INNER PROVIDE_LP DATA DECODER
// ============================================================
//
// STON.fi v2.2 SDK:
//
// storeRef(
//   beginCell()
//     .storeCoins(minLpOut)
//     .storeAddress(receiverAddress)
//     .storeUint(bothPositive ? 1 : 0, 1)
//     .storeCoins(dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount ?? 0)
//     .storeMaybeRef(dexCustomPayload)
//     .endCell()
// )
//
// ============================================================

function decodeProvideLpInnerCell(
  cell: Cell
): boolean {
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "===== PROVIDE_LP INNER REF ====="
  );

  console.log(
    `Inner bits: ${cell.bits.length}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Inner refs: ${cell.refs.length}`
  );

  const s =
    cell.beginParse();

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // minLpOut
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const minLpOut =
    s.loadCoins();

  const minLpOk =
    check(
      "PROVIDE_LP minLpOut",
      minLpOut.toString(),
      EXPECTED.minLpOut.toString()
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // receiverAddress
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const receiverAddress =
    s.loadAddress();

  const receiverOk =
    checkAddress(
      "PROVIDE_LP receiver address",
      receiverAddress,
      EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // bothPositive
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const bothPositive =
    s.loadBit();

  const bothPositiveOk =
    check(
      "PROVIDE_LP bothPositive",
      bothPositive,
      EXPECTED.bothPositive
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // DEX custom payload forward gas
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
    s.loadCoins();

  const dexGasOk =
    check(
      "DEX custom payload forward gas",
      dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString(),
      EXPECTED.dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Optional custom payload
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const hasDexCustomPayload =
    s.loadBit();

  const customPayloadFlagOk =
    check(
      "DEX custom payload flag",
      hasDexCustomPayload,
      EXPECTED.hasDexCustomPayload
    );

  if (hasDexCustomPayload) {
    console.log(
      "WARNING | Unexpected DEX custom payload reference"
    );

    if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
      s.loadRef();
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Remaining
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const remainingBits =
    s.remainingBits;

  const remainingRefs =
    s.remainingRefs;

  console.log(
    `Inner remaining bits: ${remainingBits}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Inner remaining refs: ${remainingRefs}`
  );

  const fullyConsumed =
    remainingBits === 0 &&
    remainingRefs === 0;

  if (fullyConsumed) {
    console.log(
      "PASS | PROVIDE_LP inner cell fully consumed"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `FAIL | PROVIDE_LP inner cell trailing data | bits=${remainingBits}, refs=${remainingRefs}`
    );
  }

  return (
    minLpOk &&
    receiverOk &&
    bothPositiveOk &&
    dexGasOk &&
    customPayloadFlagOk &&
    fullyConsumed
  );
}

// ============================================================
// OUTER PROVIDE_LP DECODER
// ============================================================
//
// STON.fi v2.2:
//
//   uint32  PROVIDE_LP
//   address routerWalletAddress
//   address refundAddress
//   address excessesAddress
//   uint64  deadline
//   ref     inner payload
//
// ============================================================

function decodeProvideLpPayload(
  cell: Cell
): boolean {
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "===== PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD ====="
  );

  console.log(
    `Outer PROVIDE_LP bits: ${cell.bits.length}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Outer PROVIDE_LP refs: ${cell.refs.length}`
  );

  const s =
    cell.beginParse();

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Opcode
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const opcode =
    s.loadUint(32);

  const opcodeOk =
    check(
      "PROVIDE_LP opcode",
      opcode,
      EXPECTED.provideLpOpcode
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Router pTON wallet
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const routerWalletAddress =
    s.loadAddress();

  const routerWalletOk =
    checkAddress(
      "PROVIDE_LP router wallet",
      routerWalletAddress,
      EXPECTED.routerPtonWallet
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Refund address
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const refundAddress =
    s.loadAddress();

  const refundOk =
    checkAddress(
      "PROVIDE_LP refund address",
      refundAddress,
      EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Excesses address
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const excessesAddress =
    s.loadAddress();

  const excessesOk =
    checkAddress(
      "PROVIDE_LP excesses address",
      excessesAddress,
      EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Deadline
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const deadline =
    s.loadUintBig(64);

  const deadlineOk =
    check(
      "PROVIDE_LP deadline",
      deadline.toString(),
      EXPECTED.deadline.toString()
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Inner reference
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  let innerCell: Cell | null = null;

  if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
    innerCell =
      s.loadRef();

    console.log(
      "PASS | PROVIDE_LP inner payload reference present"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "FAIL | PROVIDE_LP inner payload reference missing"
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Outer cell remaining
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const remainingBits =
    s.remainingBits;

  const remainingRefs =
    s.remainingRefs;

  console.log(
    `PROVIDE_LP outer remaining bits: ${remainingBits}`
  );

  console.log(
    `PROVIDE_LP outer remaining refs: ${remainingRefs}`
  );

  const outerFullyConsumed =
    remainingBits === 0 &&
    remainingRefs === 0;

  if (outerFullyConsumed) {
    console.log(
      "PASS | PROVIDE_LP outer cell fully consumed"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `FAIL | PROVIDE_LP outer cell trailing data | bits=${remainingBits}, refs=${remainingRefs}`
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Decode inner reference
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  let innerOk = false;

  if (innerCell) {
    innerOk =
      decodeProvideLpInnerCell(
        innerCell
      );
  }

  return (
    opcodeOk &&
    routerWalletOk &&
    refundOk &&
    excessesOk &&
    deadlineOk &&
    outerFullyConsumed &&
    innerOk
  );
}

// ============================================================
// JETTON TRANSFER DECODER
// ============================================================

function decodeJettonTransfer(
  root: Cell
): boolean {
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "===== JETTON TRANSFER ====="
  );

  const s =
    root.beginParse();

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Jetton Transfer opcode
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const opcode =
    s.loadUint(32);

  const opcodeOk =
    check(
      "Jetton Transfer opcode",
      opcode,
      EXPECTED.jettonTransferOpcode
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Query ID
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const queryId =
    s.loadUintBig(64);

  console.log(
    `query_id: ${queryId.toString()}`
  );

  const queryIdOk =
    check(
      "query_id",
      queryId.toString(),
      "0"
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Jetton amount
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const amount =
    s.loadCoins();

  const amountOk =
    check(
      "Jetton transfer amount",
      amount.toString(),
      EXPECTED.roosterAmount.toString()
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Destination
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const destination =
    s.loadAddress();

  const destinationOk =
    checkAddress(
      "Jetton transfer destination",
      destination,
      EXPECTED.routerAddress
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Response destination
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const responseDestination =
    s.loadAddress();

  const responseOk =
    checkAddress(
      "Jetton response destination",
      responseDestination,
      EXPECTED.userAddress
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Custom payload
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const hasCustomPayload =
    s.loadBit();

  const customPayloadOk =
    check(
      "custom payload flag",
      hasCustomPayload,
      false
    );

  if (hasCustomPayload) {
    console.log(
      "WARNING | Unexpected custom payload reference"
    );

    s.loadRef();
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Forward TON amount
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const forwardTonAmount =
    s.loadCoins();

  const forwardTonOk =
    check(
      "forward TON amount",
      forwardTonAmount.toString(),
      EXPECTED.forwardTonAmount.toString()
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Forward payload
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const forwardPayloadIsRef =
    s.loadBit();

  const forwardPayloadFlagOk =
    check(
      "forward payload reference flag",
      forwardPayloadIsRef,
      true
    );

  let provideLpCell: Cell | null = null;

  if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {
    provideLpCell =
      s.loadRef();

    console.log(
      "PASS | Forward payload stored as reference"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "FAIL | Forward payload is not a reference"
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Forward payload information
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (provideLpCell) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "===== FORWARD PAYLOAD ====="
    );

    console.log(
      `Forward payload bits: ${provideLpCell.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
      `Forward payload refs: ${provideLpCell.refs.length}`
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Decode PROVIDE_LP
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  let provideLpOk = false;

  if (provideLpCell) {
    provideLpOk =
      decodeProvideLpPayload(
        provideLpCell
      );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Root remaining data
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const remainingBits =
    s.remainingBits;

  const remainingRefs =
    s.remainingRefs;

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "===== OUTER CELL CHECK ====="
  );

  console.log(
    `Remaining root bits: ${remainingBits}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Remaining root refs: ${remainingRefs}`
  );

  const outerFullyConsumed =
    remainingBits === 0 &&
    remainingRefs === 0;

  if (outerFullyConsumed) {
    console.log(
      "PASS | Jetton Transfer payload fully consumed"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `FAIL | Jetton Transfer contains unexpected trailing data | bits=${remainingBits}, refs=${remainingRefs}`
    );
  }

  return (
    opcodeOk &&
    queryIdOk &&
    amountOk &&
    destinationOk &&
    responseOk &&
    customPayloadOk &&
    forwardTonOk &&
    forwardPayloadFlagOk &&
    provideLpOk &&
    outerFullyConsumed
  );
}

// ============================================================
// LOAD RAW BOC
// ============================================================

function loadBocFile(): Buffer {
  const boc =
    fs.readFileSync(
      BOC_PATH
    );

  if (boc.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(
      "BOC file is empty."
    );
  }

  console.log(
    `Raw BOC bytes: ${boc.length}`
  );

  return boc;
}

// ============================================================
// MAIN
// ============================================================

export async function run() {
  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  console.log(
    " STON.fi v2.2 INDEPENDENT LIQUIDITY BOC DECODE"
  );

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  console.log(
    "READ ONLY"
  );

  console.log(
    "NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT."
  );

  console.log(
    "NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED."
  );

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    `BOC file: ${BOC_PATH}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // File existence
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (!fs.existsSync(BOC_PATH)) {
    throw new Error(
      `BOC file not found: ${BOC_PATH}`
    );
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Load RAW binary BOC
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  const boc =
    loadBocFile();

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // BOC size
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  console.log(
    `Decoded BOC bytes: ${boc.length}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // BOC magic
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  if (boc.length < 4) {
    throw new Error(
      "Decoded BOC is shorter than 4 bytes."
    );
  }

  const magic =
    boc
      .subarray(0, 4)
      .toString("hex");

  console.log(
    `BOC magic: 0x${magic}`
  );

  if (magic !== "b5ee9c72") {
    throw new Error(
      `Invalid BOC magic: 0x${magic}`
    );
  }

  console.log(
    "PASS | BOC magic | 0xb5ee9c72"
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Decode TON cells
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  let cells: Cell[];

  try {
    cells =
      Cell.fromBoc(boc);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unable to decode binary BOC: ${String(error)}`
    );
  }

  if (cells.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(
      "BOC contains no cells."
    );
  }

  console.log(
    `Root cells: ${cells.length}`
  );

  const root =
    cells[0];

  console.log(
    `Root bits: ${root.bits.length}`
  );

  console.log(
    `Root refs: ${root.refs.length}`
  );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Independent structural decode
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  console.log(
    " INDEPENDENT STRUCTURAL DECODE"
  );

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  const result =
    decodeJettonTransfer(
      root
    );

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // Final result
  // ----------------------------------------------------------

  console.log("");

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  console.log(
    " FINAL RESULT"
  );

  console.log(
    "=============================================="
  );

  if (result) {
    console.log("");

    console.log(
      "STATUS: PASS"
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
      "The fresh rooster_v22_generated.boc matches the expected STON.fi v2.2 SDK-style structure."
    );

    console.log(
      "No transaction was sent."
    );

    console.log(
      "No wallet signature was requested."
    );
  } else {
    console.log("");

    console.log(
      "STATUS: FAIL"
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
      "The fresh BOC does not match one or more expected values."
    );

    console.log(
      "No transaction was sent."
    );

    console.log(
      "No wallet signature was requested."
    );

    throw new Error(
      "Independent BOC validation failed."
    );
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFreshV22Bocs.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const ROOSTER_BOC = './rooster_v22_current_ratio.boc';
const PTON_BOC = './pton_v22_current_ratio.boc';

function printAddress(slice: any, name: string) {
    try {
        const address = slice.loadAddress();

        console.log(`${name}: ${address ? address.toString() : 'null'}`);

        return address;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(`${name}: ERROR`);
        console.log(String(e));
        return null;
    }
}

function decodeProvideLpPayload(cell: Cell, label: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(` ${label}`);
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log(`BITS: ${s.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`REFS: ${s.remainingRefs}`);

    const op = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        `OPCODE: 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    if (op !== 0x37c096df) {
        console.log('ERROR: not provide_lp');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- PROVIDE_LP HEADER ---');

    printAddress(s, 'OTHER_TOKEN_WALLET');
    printAddress(s, 'REFUND_ADDRESS');
    printAddress(s, 'EXCESSES_ADDRESS');

    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
        const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log(`DEADLINE: ${deadline}`);

        const deadlineNumber = Number(deadline);

        if (
            Number.isSafeInteger(deadlineNumber) &&
            deadlineNumber > 1000000000 &&
            deadlineNumber < 5000000000
        ) {
            console.log(
                `DEADLINE DATE: ${new Date(
                    deadlineNumber * 1000
                ).toISOString()}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- PROVIDE_LP ADDITIONAL_DATA ---');

    if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {
        console.log('ERROR: missing additional_data reference');
        return;
    }

    const additional = s.loadRef();
    const a = additional.beginParse();

    console.log(`ADDITIONAL BITS: ${a.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`ADDITIONAL REFS: ${a.remainingRefs}`);

    const minLpOut = a.loadCoins();

    console.log(`MIN_LP_OUT: ${minLpOut}`);

    printAddress(a, 'RECEIVER_ADDRESS');

    if (a.remainingBits >= 1) {
        const bothPositive = a.loadBit();

        console.log(`BOTH_POSITIVE: ${bothPositive}`);
    }

    if (a.remainingBits > 0) {
        const fwdAmount = a.loadCoins();

        console.log(`FWD_AMOUNT: ${fwdAmount}`);
        console.log(
            `FWD_AMOUNT TON: ${Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9}`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- CUSTOM_PAYLOAD ---');

    if (a.remainingBits >= 1) {
        const hasPayload = a.loadBit();

        console.log(`CUSTOM_PAYLOAD PRESENT: ${hasPayload}`);

        if (hasPayload && a.remainingRefs > 0) {
            const customPayload = a.loadRef();

            console.log(
                `CUSTOM_PAYLOAD BITS: ${customPayload.bits.length}`
            );

            console.log(
                `CUSTOM_PAYLOAD REFS: ${customPayload.refs.length}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(`ADDITIONAL REMAINING BITS: ${a.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`ADDITIONAL REMAINING REFS: ${a.remainingRefs}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`PAYLOAD REMAINING BITS: ${s.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`PAYLOAD REMAINING REFS: ${s.remainingRefs}`);
}

function decodeRoosterTransfer(root: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' ROOSTER JETTON TRANSFER');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const op = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        `OPCODE: 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(`QUERY_ID: ${queryId}`);

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log(`JETTON_AMOUNT: ${amount}`);
    console.log(
        `JETTON_AMOUNT ROOSTER: ${Number(amount) / 1e9}`
    );

    printAddress(s, 'DESTINATION');
    printAddress(s, 'RESPONSE_DESTINATION');

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- CUSTOM PAYLOAD ---');

    const customPresent = s.loadBit();

    console.log(`CUSTOM_PAYLOAD PRESENT: ${customPresent}`);

    if (customPresent && s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log(`FORWARD_TON_AMOUNT: ${forwardAmount}`);
    console.log(
        `FORWARD_TON_AMOUNT TON: ${Number(forwardAmount) / 1e9}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('--- FORWARD PAYLOAD ---');

    const isRef = s.loadBit();

    console.log(`FORWARD_PAYLOAD IS REF: ${isRef}`);

    let payload: Cell | null = null;

    if (isRef && s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        payload = s.loadRef();
    }

    if (payload) {
        decodeProvideLpPayload(
            payload,
            'ROOSTER -> ROUTER PROVIDE_LP'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(`ROOT REMAINING BITS: ${s.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`ROOT REMAINING REFS: ${s.remainingRefs}`);
}

function decodePtonRaw(root: Cell) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' pTON ROOT STRUCTURE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const s = root.beginParse();

    const op = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        `OPCODE: 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    console.log(`REMAINING BITS: ${s.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`REMAINING REFS: ${s.remainingRefs}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < s.remainingRefs; i++) {
        const ref = s.loadRef();

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `pTON REF ${i}: bits=${ref.bits.length}, refs=${ref.refs.length}`
        );

        const rs = ref.beginParse();

        if (rs.remainingBits >= 32) {
            const refOp = rs.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                `pTON REF ${i} OPCODE: 0x${refOp
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, '0')}`
            );

            if (refOp === 0x37c096df) {
                decodeProvideLpPayload(
                    ref,
                    `pTON -> PROVIDE_LP REF ${i}`
                );
            }
        }
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');

    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log(' NO RPC');
    console.log(' NO WALLET');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('======================================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC:', ROOSTER_BOC);
    console.log('pTON BOC   :', PTON_BOC);

    const roosterRoot = Cell.fromBoc(
        readFileSync(ROOSTER_BOC)
    )[0];

    const ptonRoot = Cell.fromBoc(
        readFileSync(PTON_BOC)
    )[0];

    decodeRoosterTransfer(roosterRoot);

    decodePtonRaw(ptonRoot);

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' DECODING COMPLETE');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('======================================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunc107CallsExact.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x0000d339;
const END   = 0x0000d49e;

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb32(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let x;

  do {
    x = b[pos++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value, next: pos };
}

function readInstruction(p) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let text = `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  `;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      const tab = uleb(p);
      p = tab.next;
      text += `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      text += `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      text += `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb32(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `I32.CONST ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x6a:
      text += "I32.ADD";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text += "I32.SUB";
      break;

    case 0x45:
      text += "I32.EQZ";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text += "I32.NE";
      break;

    case 0x04:
      text += `IF type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x02:
      text += `BLOCK type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      text += `LOOP type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text += "END";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `BR ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text += `BR_IF ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text += "DROP";
      break;

    case 0x0f:
      text += "RETURN";
      break;

    case 0x41:
      break;

    default:
      text += `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
      break;
  }

  return { next: p, text };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 107 EXACT CALL DECODER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = START;
const calls = [];

while (p < END) {

  const x = readInstruction(p);

  console.log(x.text);

  if (x.text.includes("CALL FUNC")) {
    calls.push({
      addr: p,
      text: x.text
    });
  }

  p = x.next;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT DIRECT CALLS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const c of calls)
  console.log(c.text);

console.log("");
console.log(`CALL COUNT : ${calls.length}`);

const f106 = calls.filter(x => x.text.includes("CALL FUNC 106"));

console.log("");
console.log(`FUNC106 CALL COUNT : ${f106.length}`);

if (f106.length === 0) {
  console.log("NO REAL FUNC106 CALL FOUND IN FUNC107");
} else {
  for (const x of f106)
    console.log(`REAL FUNC106 @ 0x${x.addr.toString(16)}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

for (let func = 0; func <= 122; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  if (func !== 122) {
    q = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const localCount = leb(q);
  q = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = leb(q);
    q = n.next;
    q += 1;
  }

  const start = q;
  const end = bodyEnd - 1;

  console.log("FUNC 122 CALL 87 STACK TRACE");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (wasm[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const c = leb(x + 1);
    if (c.value !== 87) continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`CALL 87 @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`);

    const from = Math.max(start, x - 32);

    let i = from;

    while (i < x) {
      const op = wasm[i];

      if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = leb(i + 1);
        console.log(`  local.get ${r.value}`);
        i = r.next;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = leb(i + 1);
        console.log(`  local.tee ${r.value}`);
        i = r.next;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = leb(i + 1);
        console.log(`  i32.const ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`);
        i = r.next;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = leb(i + 1);
        console.log(`  i64.const ${r.value} (0x${r.value.toString(16)})`);
        i = r.next;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x29) {
        const align = wasm[i + 1];
        const off = leb(i + 2);
        console.log(`  i64.load align=${align} offset=${off.value}`);
        i = off.next;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x6a) {
        console.log("  i32.add");
        i++;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x86) {
        console.log("  i64.extend_i32_u");
        i++;
        continue;
      }

      if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = leb(i + 1);
        console.log(`  call ${r.value}`);
        i = r.next;
        continue;
      }

      console.log(`  opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
      i++;
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("DONE");
  break;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x0000eec5;
const END   = 0x0000ef44;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: v, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                v |= (~0 << shift);

            return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
        }
    }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 122 CONTROL-FLOW DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

let p = START;
let depth = 0;

function indent() {
    return "  ".repeat(depth);
}

while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x02: { // block
            const x = wasm[p++];
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}BLOCK type=${hex(x,2)}`
            );
            depth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x03: { // loop
            const x = wasm[p++];
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}LOOP type=${hex(x,2)}`
            );
            depth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x04: { // if
            const x = wasm[p++];
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}IF type=${hex(x,2)}`
            );
            depth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x05: // else
            depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
            console.log(`${hex(at)} ${indent()}ELSE`);
            depth++;
            break;

        case 0x0b: // end
            depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
            console.log(`${hex(at)} ${indent()}END`);
            break;

        case 0x0c: { // br
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}BR depth=${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: { // br_if
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}BR_IF depth=${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} ${indent()}RETURN`);
            break;

        case 0x20: { // local.get
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}local.get ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: { // local.set
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}local.set ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: { // local.tee
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}local.tee ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: { // i32.const
            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: { // i32.load
            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = uleb(p);
            p = offset.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: { // i32.store
            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = uleb(p);
            p = offset.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x45:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} ${indent()}i32.eqz`);
            break;

        case 0x46:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} ${indent()}i32.eq`);
            break;

        case 0x10: { // call
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}CALL FUNC ${x.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = uleb(p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        default:
            // Keep unknown opcodes visible, but don't stop.
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} ${indent()}OPCODE ${hex(op,2)}`
            );
            break;
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const BODY_START = 0; // auto-find below
const FUNC = 1533;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return [v >>> 0, pos];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  let code = null;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const [size, q] = uleb(p);
    p = q;

    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
      const [count, q2] = uleb(p);
      let x = q2;

      const bodies = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const [bodySize, q3] = uleb(x);
        x = q3;

        const start = x;
        const stop = x + bodySize;

        bodies.push({ start, stop });
        x = stop;
      }

      code = bodies;
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return code;
}

const bodies = readSections();

if (!bodies || !bodies[FUNC - 83]) {
  throw new Error("FUNC1533 body not found");
}

const f = bodies[FUNC - 83];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 1533 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

console.log("BODY END   :", hex(f.stop));
console.log("SIZE       :", f.stop - f.start);

let p = f.start;

// locals vector
const [localGroups, q] = uleb(p);
p = q;

console.log("LOCAL GROUPS:", localGroups);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
  const [n, q1] = uleb(p);
  p = q1;

  const type = b[p++];

  console.log(`locals[${i}] count=${n} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const opNames = {
  0x0b: "END",
  0x0f: "RETURN",
  0x1a: "DROP",
  0x20: "LOCAL.GET",
  0x21: "LOCAL.SET",
  0x22: "LOCAL.TEE",
  0x23: "GLOBAL.GET",
  0x24: "GLOBAL.SET",

  0x28: "I32.LOAD",
  0x2d: "I32.LOAD8_U",
  0x36: "I32.STORE",
  0x3a: "I32.STORE8",

  0x41: "I32.CONST",

  0x45: "I32.EQZ",
  0x46: "I32.EQ",
  0x47: "I32.NE",

  0x6a: "I32.ADD",
  0x6b: "I32.SUB",
  0x6c: "I32.MUL",
  0x6d: "I32.DIV_S",
  0x71: "I32.AND",
  0x72: "I32.OR",
  0x73: "I32.XOR",

  0x04: "IF",
  0x05: "ELSE",
  0x02: "BLOCK",
  0x03: "LOOP",
  0x0c: "BR",
  0x0d: "BR_IF",

  0x10: "CALL",
  0x11: "CALL_INDIRECT",

  0xfc: "PREFIX_FC"
};

function readMemArg() {
  const [align, q1] = uleb(p);
  p = q1;
  const [offset, q2] = uleb(p);
  p = q2;
  return { align, offset };
}

while (p < f.stop) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s = `${hex(at)}  ${opNames[op] ?? "opcode=0x" + op.toString(16)}`;

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {

    const [idx, q1] = uleb(p);
    p = q1;
    s += ` L${idx}`;

  } else if (op === 0x41) {

    const [v, q1] = uleb(p);
    p = q1;
    s += ` ${v} (${hex(v)})`;

  } else if (op === 0x10) {

    const [idx, q1] = uleb(p);
    p = q1;
    s += ` FUNC ${idx}`;

  } else if (op === 0x11) {

    const [type, q1] = uleb(p);
    p = q1;
    const table = b[p++];
    s += ` TYPE ${type} TABLE ${table}`;

  } else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x2d || op === 0x36 || op === 0x3a) {

    const m = readMemArg();
    s += ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

  } else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const [depth, q1] = uleb(p);
    p = q1;
    s += ` ${depth}`;

  } else if (op === 0xfc) {

    const sub = b[p++];
    s += ` SUBOP=0x${sub.toString(16)}`;

    // Preserve raw bytes for bulk-memory/table instructions
    while (p < f.stop && (b[p] & 0x80)) p++;
    if (p < f.stop) p++;
  }

  console.log(s);
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFunc = 1416;

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);
let p = 8;

function u8() {
  return b[p++];
}

function readULEB() {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = u8();
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return v;
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB() {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x;

  while (true) {
    x = u8();
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
    v -= 2 ** s;
  }

  return v;
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

// ============================================================
// PARSE WASM SECTIONS
// ============================================================

let types = [];
let importedFunctions = 0;
let funcTypeIndices = [];
let codeBodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const sectionId = u8();
  const sectionSize = readULEB();
  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // TYPE SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  if (sectionId === 1) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const form = u8();

      if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)}`
        );
      }

      const paramCount = readULEB();
      const params = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
        params.push(u8());
      }

      const resultCount = readULEB();
      const results = [];

      for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
        results.push(u8());
      }

      types.push({
        params,
        results
      });
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // IMPORT SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 2) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      readULEB(); // module string length + bytes
      readULEB(); // field string length + bytes

      const kind = u8();

      if (kind === 0) {
        readULEB(); // type index
        importedFunctions++;
      }

      else if (kind === 1) {
        u8(); // element type

        const flags = readULEB();
        readULEB(); // min

        if (flags & 1) {
          readULEB(); // max
        }
      }

      else if (kind === 2) {
        const flags = readULEB();
        readULEB();

        if (flags & 1) {
          readULEB();
        }
      }

      else if (kind === 3) {
        u8();
        u8();
      }
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // FUNCTION SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 3) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      funcTypeIndices.push(readULEB());
    }
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  // CODE SECTION
  // ----------------------------------------------------------
  else if (sectionId === 10) {
    const count = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const bodySize = readULEB();

      const bodyStart = p;
      const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

      codeBodies.push({
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        bodySize
      });

      p = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

// ============================================================
// RESOLVE TARGET FUNCTION
// ============================================================

const definedIndex = targetFunc - importedFunctions;

if (
  definedIndex < 0 ||
  definedIndex >= codeBodies.length
) {
  throw new Error(
    `FUNC ${targetFunc} not found`
  );
}

const body = codeBodies[definedIndex];

);

);

  `SIZE       : ${body.bodySize}`
);

const localGroups = readULEB();

console.log(
  `LOCAL GROUPS: ${localGroups}`
);

let totalLocals = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {

const names = {
  0x00: "UNREACHABLE",
  0x01: "NOP",

  0x02: "BLOCK",
  0x03: "LOOP",
  0x04: "IF",
  0x05: "ELSE",

  0x0b: "END",
  0x0c: "BR",

  0x11: "CALL_INDIRECT",

  0x1b: "SELECT",

  0x21: "LOCAL.SET",

  0x6a: "I32.ADD",

  0x6c: "I32.MUL",
  0x6d: "I32.DIV_S",
  0x6e: "I32.DIV_U",

  0x71: "I32.AND",
  0x72: "I32.OR",
  0x73: "I32.XOR",
  0x74: "I32.SHL",
  0x75: "I32.SHR_S",
  0x76: "I32.SHR_U",

  0x79: "I32.ROTL",
  0x7a: "I32.ROTR",

  0xa7: "I32.WRAP_I64",
  0xad: "I64.EXTEND_I32_S"
};

function blockType() {
  const x = u8();

  if (x === 0x40) return "0x40";
  if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
  if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
  if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
  if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";

  return hex(x, 2);
}

function memArg() {
  const align = readULEB();
  const offset = readULEB();

  return {
    align,
    offset
  };
}

// EXACT INSTRUCTION DECODE
// ============================================================

while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
  const at = p;
  const op = u8();

  let line =
    names[op] ||
    `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BLOCK / LOOP / IF
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      line += ` blocktype=${blockType()}`;
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BR / BR_IF
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const depth = readULEB();
      line += ` ${depth}`;
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BR_TABLE
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x0e: {
      const count = readULEB();
      const labels = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        labels.push(readULEB());
      }

      const def = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` labels=[${labels.join(",")}]` +
        ` default=${def}`;

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x10: {
      const func = readULEB();
      line += ` FUNC ${func}`;
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL INDIRECT
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x11: {
      const typeIndex = readULEB();
      const tableIndex = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` TYPE ${typeIndex}` +
        ` TABLE ${tableIndex}`;

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCAL
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const local = readULEB();
      line += ` L${local}`;
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // GLOBAL
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const global = readULEB();
      line += ` G${global}`;
      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // MEMORY
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x2f:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b: {
      const m = memArg();

      line +=
        ` align=${m.align}` +
        ` offset=${m.offset}`;

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I32 CONST
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x41: {
      const v = readSLEB();

      line +=
        ` ${v}` +
        ` (${hex(v)})`;

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I64 CONST
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0x42: {
      const v = readSLEB();

      line += ` ${v}`;

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // FC PREFIX
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` subopcode=0x${sub.toString(16)}`;

      break;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // FE PREFIX
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    case 0xfe: {
      const sub = readULEB();

      line +=
        ` subopcode=0x${sub.toString(16)}`;

      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)}  ${line}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const FUNC = 2057;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let bodies = null;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const r = uleb(p);
    if (!r) break;

    p = r.end;
    const end = p + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      const c = uleb(p);
      let q = c.end;

      bodies = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const z = uleb(q);
        q = z.end;

        bodies.push({
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = findBodies();
const body = bodies[FUNC - IMPORTS];

if (!body)
  throw new Error("FUNC2057 body not found");

console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("BODY END   :", hex(body.end));
console.log("SIZE       :", body.end - body.start);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// locals
const groups = uleb(p);
p = groups.end;

console.log("LOCAL GROUPS:", groups.value);

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.end;

  const type = b[p++];

  console.log(
    `locals[${i}] count=${n.value} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
  );
}

function memarg() {
  const a = uleb(p);
  p = a.end;

  const o = uleb(p);
  p = o.end;

  return {
    align:a.value,
    offset:o.value
  };

while (p < body.end) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s =
    `${hex(at)}  ${names[op] ?? "opcode=0x" + op.toString(16)}`;

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` L${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x41) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;

  } else if (op === 0x10) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` FUNC ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x11) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    const table = b[p++];

    s += ` TYPE ${r.value} TABLE ${table}`;

  } else if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {

    const m = memarg();

    s +=
      ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}` +
      ` [abs=0x${m.offset.toString(16)}]`;

  } else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    s += ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  console.log(s);
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 0x093e16;
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function s32(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** (s + 7);
      return { value: v, next: p };
    }
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const id = buf[p++];

  const si = u32(p);
  p = si.next;

  const end = p + si.value;

  if (id !== 10) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  const ci = u32(p);
  const count = ci.value;
  p = ci.next;

  for (let body = 0; body < count; body++) {

    const bi = u32(p);
    const size = bi.value;
    p = bi.next;

    const start = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size;

    const li = u32(p);
    const locals = li.value;
    p = li.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals; i++) {
      p = u32(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    if (start === TARGET) {

      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("FUNC 2057 EXACT DECODE");
      console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log(`REAL FUNC  : ${body + 83}`);
      console.log(`BODY INDEX : ${body}`);
      console.log(`BODY START : ${hx(start)}`);
      console.log(`CODE START : ${hx(codeStart)}`);
      console.log(`BODY END   : ${hx(bodyEnd)}`);
      console.log(`BODY SIZE  : ${size}`);
      console.log("============================================================");

      let q = codeStart;

      while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const at = q;
        const op = buf[q++];

        let text = `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        switch (op) {

          case 0x00:
            text = "unreachable";
            break;

          case 0x02:
            text = "block";
            q++;
            break;

          case 0x03:
            text = "loop";
            q++;
            break;

          case 0x04:
            text = "if";
            q++;
            break;

          case 0x05:
            text = "else";
            break;

          case 0x0b:
            text = "end";
            break;

          case 0x0c: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `br ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x0d: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x0f:
            text = "return";
            break;

          case 0x1a:
            text = "drop";
            break;

          case 0x20: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x21: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x22: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x23: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x24: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x28: {
            const a = u32(q);
            q = a.next;
            const o = u32(q);
            q = o.next;
            text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x36: {
            const a = u32(q);
            q = a.next;
            const o = u32(q);
            q = o.next;
            text = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x41: {
            const x = s32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `i32.const ${x.value} (${hx(x.value >>> 0)})`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x45:
            text = "i32.eqz";
            break;

          case 0x47:
            text = "i32.ne";
            break;

          case 0x4b:
            text = "i32.gt_u";
            break;

          case 0x6a:
            text = "i32.add";
            break;

          case 0x6b:
            text = "i32.sub";
            break;

          case 0x71:
            text = "i32.and";
            break;

          case 0x74:
            text = "i32.shl";
            break;

          case 0x7a:
            text = "i32.shr_u";
            break;

          case 0x10: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `call FUNC ${x.value}`;
            break;
          }

          case 0x0e: {
            const x = u32(q);
            q = x.next;
            const labels = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < x.value; i++) {
              const y = u32(q);
              q = y.next;
              labels.push(y.value);
            }
            const d = u32(q);
            q = d.next;
            text = `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`;
            break;
          }

          default:
            /*
             * Stop decoding unknown extended instruction,
             * but print its location.
             */
            break;
        }

        console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${text}`);
      }

      console.log("============================================================");
      console.log("DONE");
      console.log("============================================================");

      process.exit(0);
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  p = end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunc2088CallsExact.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x00095afd;
const END   = 0x00095b83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function ins(p) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let text = `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  `;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x01:
      text += "NOP";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text += "END";
      break;

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `BR_IF ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p); p = t.next;
      const tab = uleb(p); p = tab.next;
      text += `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      text += "DROP";
      break;

    case 0x1b:
      text += "SELECT";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text += `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p); p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p); p = o.next;
      text += `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p); p = x.next;
      text += `I32.CONST ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      text += "I32.EQZ";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text += "I32.EQ";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text += "I32.NE";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text += "I32.ADD";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text += "I32.SUB";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      text += "I32.AND";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      text += "I32.OR";
      break;

    case 0x04:
      text += `IF type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x05:
      text += "ELSE";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      text += `BLOCK type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    case 0x03:
      text += `LOOP type=0x${b[p++].toString(16)}`;
      break;

    default:
      text += `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
      break;
  }

  return { next:p, text };
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunc3315.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const targetFunc = 3315;

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);
let p = 8;

// ---------- helpers ----------
function u32() {
  const v = b.readUInt32LE(p);
  p += 4;
  return v;
}

function u8() {
  return b[p++];
}

function readULEB() {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = u8();
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return v >>> 0;
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB() {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = u8();
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return v | 0;
}

function skipLimits() {
  const flags = readULEB();
  readULEB();
  if (flags & 1) readULEB();
}

function skipType() {
  const form = u8();
  if (form === 0x60) {
    const n = readULEB();
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) u8();
    const r = readULEB();
    for (let i = 0; i < r; i++) u8();
  }
}

function skipSectionPayload(size) {
  p += size;
}

// ---------- parse sections ----------
let types = [];
let imports = [];
let funcTypeIndices = [];
let codeBodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = u8();
  const size = readULEB();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 1) {
    const n = readULEB();
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const form = u8();
      if (form !== 0x60) {
        throw new Error("Unexpected type form");
      }

      const np = readULEB();
      const params = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < np; j++) params.push(u8());

      const nr = readULEB();
      const results = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < nr; j++) results.push(u8());

      types.push({ params, results });
    }
  } else if (id === 2) {
    const n = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      readULEB(); // module name
      readULEB(); // field name

      const kind = u8();

      if (kind === 0) {
        const typeIndex = readULEB();
        imports.push(typeIndex);
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        u8(); // table type
        skipLimits();
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        skipLimits();
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        u8();
        u8();
      }
    }
  } else if (id === 3) {
    const n = readULEB();
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      funcTypeIndices.push(readULEB());
    }
  } else if (id === 10) {
    const n = readULEB();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const bodySize = readULEB();
      const bodyStart = p;
      const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

      codeBodies.push({
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        bodySize
      });

      p = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

// ---------- function index ----------
const importedFunctions = imports.length;
const definedIndex = targetFunc - importedFunctions;

if (definedIndex < 0 || definedIndex >= codeBodies.length) {
  throw new Error(
    `FUNC ${targetFunc} not found. imported=${importedFunctions}, defined=${codeBodies.length}`
  );
}

const body = codeBodies[definedIndex];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 3315 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
  `BODY START : 0x${body.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log(
  `BODY END   : 0x${body.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log(`SIZE       : ${body.bodySize}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ---------- decode body ----------
p = body.bodyStart;

const localGroupCount = readULEB();

let localCount = 0;

console.log(`LOCAL GROUPS: ${localGroupCount}`);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroupCount; i++) {
  const count = readULEB();
  const type = u8();

  localCount += count;

  console.log(
    `locals[${i}] count=${count} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const instructionsStart = p;

const names = {
  0x00: "UNREACHABLE",
  0x01: "NOP",
  0x02: "BLOCK",
  0x03: "LOOP",
  0x04: "IF",
  0x05: "ELSE",
  0x0b: "END",
  0x0c: "BR",
  0x0d: "BR_IF",
  0x0e: "BR_TABLE",
  0x0f: "RETURN",

  0x10: "CALL",
  0x11: "CALL_INDIRECT",

  0x1a: "DROP",
  0x1b: "SELECT",

  0x20: "LOCAL.GET",
  0x21: "LOCAL.SET",
  0x22: "LOCAL.TEE",

  0x23: "GLOBAL.GET",
  0x24: "GLOBAL.SET",

  0x28: "I32.LOAD",
  0x2d: "I32.LOAD8_U",
  0x36: "I32.STORE",
  0x3a: "I32.STORE8",

  0x41: "I32.CONST",
  0x45: "I32.EQZ",
  0x46: "I32.EQ",
  0x47: "I32.NE",

  0x4a: "I32.GT_U",
  0x4b: "I32.GE_U",
  0x4c: "I32.LT_S",

  0x6a: "I32.ADD",
  0x6b: "I32.SUB",
  0x6c: "I32.MUL",
  0x6d: "I32.DIV_S",

  0x71: "I32.AND",
  0x72: "I32.OR",
  0x73: "I32.XOR",
  0x74: "I32.SHL",
  0x75: "I32.SHR_S",
  0x76: "I32.SHR_U",

  0xa7: "I32.WRAP_I64",
  0xad: "I64.EXTEND_I32_S"
};

function hex(x, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function readMemArg() {
  const align = readULEB();
  const offset = readULEB();
  return { align, offset };
}

function decodeBlockType() {
  const x = u8();

  if (x === 0x40) return "0x40";

  if (x === 0x7f) return "i32";
  if (x === 0x7e) return "i64";
  if (x === 0x7d) return "f32";
  if (x === 0x7c) return "f64";

  return hex(x, 2);
}

while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
  const at = p;
  const op = u8();

  let text = names[op] || `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const bt = decodeBlockType();
      text += ` blocktype=${bt}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const depth = readULEB();
      text += ` ${depth}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = readULEB();
      const labels = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) labels.push(readULEB());
      const def = readULEB();

      text += ` labels=[${labels.join(",")}] default=${def}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const f = readULEB();
      text += ` FUNC ${f}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const typeIndex = readULEB();
      const tableIndex = readULEB();

      text += ` TYPE ${typeIndex} TABLE ${tableIndex}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const l = readULEB();
      text += ` L${l}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const g = readULEB();
      text += ` G${g}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x3a: {
      const m = readMemArg();

      text +=
        ` align=${m.align}` +
        ` offset=${m.offset}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const v = readSLEB();
      text += ` ${v} (${hex(v)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readULEB();
      text += ` subopcode=0x${sub.toString(16)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0xfe: {
      const sub = readULEB();
      text += ` subopcode=0x${sub.toString(16)}`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)}  ${text}`
  );
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x2cc1f;
const END   = 0x2ccc3;

function hex(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function decodeSLEB32(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let bytes = [];

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    bytes.push(x);

    result |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      shift += 7;

      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1 << shift);
      }

      return {
        signed: result | 0,
        unsigned: result >>> 0,
        bytes
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 656 DIRECT RAW I32.CONST DECODE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x41) {
    continue;
  }

  const r = decodeSLEB32(p + 1);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`I32.CONST @ ${hex(p)}`);

  console.log(
    "RAW : " +
    r.bytes
      .map(x =>
        x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
      )
      .join(" ")
  );

  console.log(
    `SIGNED   : ${r.signed}`
  );

  console.log(
    `UNSIGNED : ${r.unsigned}`
  );

  console.log(
    `HEX      : ${hex(r.unsigned)}`
  );
}
