import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const FUNC = 6826;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** s;
      return { value: v | 0, end: pos };
    }
  }
  throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  const bodies = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    const end = p + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      const c = uleb(p);
      let q = c.end;

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const z = uleb(q);
        q = z.end;

        bodies.push({
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

if (!body)

function memarg() {
  const a = uleb(p);
  p = a.end;

  const o = uleb(p);
  p = o.end;

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

while (p < body.end) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s =
    `${hex(at)}  ${names[op] ?? "opcode=0x" + op.toString(16)}`;

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` L${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` FUNC ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x11) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    const table = b[p++];
    s += ` TYPE ${r.value} TABLE ${table}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const m = memarg();

    s +=
      ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}` +
      ` [abs=0x${m.offset.toString(16)}]`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  console.log(s);
}

  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function memarg() {
  const a = uleb(p);
  p = a.end;

  const o = uleb(p);
  p = o.end;

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

while (p < body.end) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let s =
    `${hex(at)}  ${names[op] ?? "opcode=0x" + op.toString(16)}`;

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` L${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` ${r.value} (${hex(r.value)})`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;
    s += ` FUNC ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x11) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    const table = b[p++];

    s += ` TYPE ${r.value} TABLE ${table}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const m = memarg();

    s +=
      ` align=${m.align}` +
      ` offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    s += ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  console.log(s);
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = pos;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payload;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

p = countInfo.next;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < codeCount; ordinal++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + bodySize;

    if (ordinal === targetOrdinal) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Stack representation
// ------------------------------------------------------------

type Value = {
    name: string;
    origin: string;
};

const stack: Value[] = [];

function push(name: string, origin: string) {
    stack.push({ name, origin });
}

function pop(): Value {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return {
            name: "<UNKNOWN>",
            origin: "stack underflow"
        };
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function showStack() {

    if (stack.length === 0) {
        console.log("    STACK = [ empty ]");
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        "    STACK = [ " +
        stack.map(v => `${v.name}{${v.origin}}`).join(" , ") +
        " ]"
    );
}

x = localInfo.next;

let localCount = 3; // parameters 0,1,2

console.log(`PARAMETERS = local0, local1, local2`);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {

    const c = readULEB(x);
    const count = c.value;
    x = c.next;

    const type = wasm[x++];

    console.log(
        `LOCAL GROUP ${i}: count=${count} type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );

    localCount += count;
}

while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const offset = x;
    const op = wasm[x++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // block
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x02) {

        const blockType = wasm[x++];

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // loop
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x03) {

        const blockType = wasm[x++];

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // if
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x04) {

        const blockType = wasm[x++];

        const cond = pop();

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  if type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}` +
            ` cond=${cond.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // end
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x0b) {

        console.log(`${hex(offset)}  end`);

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // br
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x0c) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  br depth=${r.value}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // br_if
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x0d) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const cond = pop();

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  br_if depth=${r.value}` +
            ` cond=${cond.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // return
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x0f) {

        console.log(`${hex(offset)}  return`);
        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // local.get
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        push(`local${r.value}`, `local.get ${r.value}`);

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  local.get ${r.value}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // local.set
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x21) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const value = pop();

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  local.set ${r.value} <- ${value.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // local.tee
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x22) {

        const r = readULEB(x);
        x = r.next;

        const value = pop();

        push(
            value.name,
            `local.tee ${r.value}`
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  local.tee ${r.value} <- ${value.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.const
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const r = readSLEB32(x);
        x = r.next;

        push(
            `i32.const(${r.value})`,
            `constant ${r.value}`
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  i32.const ${r.value}` +
            ` (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.load
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x28) {

        const align = readULEB(x);
        x = align.next;

        const memOffset = readULEB(x);
        x = memOffset.next;

        const address = pop();

        push(
            `load32(${address.name}+${hex(memOffset.value)})`,
            `i32.load`
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  i32.load` +
            ` align=${align.value}` +
            ` offset=${memOffset.value}` +
            ` addr=${address.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.add
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x6a) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(
            `(${a.name}+${b.name})`,
            "i32.add"
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  i32.add` +
            ` ${a.name} + ${b.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.eqz
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x45) {

        const a = pop();

        push(
            `eqz(${a.name})`,
            "i32.eqz"
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  i32.eqz ${a.name}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.eq
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x46) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(
            `(${a.name}==${b.name})`,
            "i32.eq"
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  i32.eq`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // i32.ne
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x47) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(
            `(${a.name}!=${b.name})`,
            "i32.ne"
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  i32.ne`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // call_indirect
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x11) {

        const typeInfo = readULEB(x);
        x = typeInfo.next;

        const reserved = wasm[x++];

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  CALL_INDIRECT` +
            ` type=${typeInfo.value}` +
            ` reserved=${reserved}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    BEFORE CALL_INDIRECT:`
        );

        showStack();

        // type 6 = 3 i32 params.
        // WebAssembly call_indirect consumes:
        //   arguments + table index
        //
        // Stack top is table index.

        const tableIndex = pop();

        const arg2 = pop();
        const arg1 = pop();
        const arg0 = pop();

        console.log(
            `    TABLE INDEX = ${tableIndex.name}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    ARG0 = ${arg0.name}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    ARG1 = ${arg1.name}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    ARG2 = ${arg2.name}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    TARGET TYPE = ${typeInfo.value}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    EXPECTED TYPE = (i32,i32,i32)->void`
        );

        showStack();

        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // direct call
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const target = readULEB(x);
        x = target.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  CALL FUNC ${target.value}`
        );

        if (target.value === 83) {

            const arg = pop();

            console.log(
                `    FUNC 83 ARG0 = ${arg.name}`
            );
        }

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // misc integer operations
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
        op === 0x47 ||
        op === 0x48 ||
        op === 0x49 ||
        op === 0x4a ||
        op === 0x4b ||
        op === 0x4c ||
        op === 0x4d ||
        op === 0x4e ||
        op === 0x4f
    ) {

        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();

        push(
            `cmp(${a.name},${b.name})`,
            `compare opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `${hex(offset)}  compare opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`
        );

        showStack();
        continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Unknown opcode
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        `${hex(offset)}  UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "    Decoder stopped to avoid desynchronization."
    );

    break;
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function sleb64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (-1n) << shift;
  }

  return { value: BigInt.asIntN(64, result), next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readName(pos: number): { name: string; next: number } {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  pos = n.next;

  const end = pos + n.value;
  return {
    name: Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(pos, end)).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function readBlockType(pos: number): {
  text: string;
  next: number;
} {
  const b = wasm[pos++];

  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x40: "empty",
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
  };

  if (names[b] !== undefined) {
    return { text: names[b], next: pos };
  }

  return {
    text: `typeidx/raw ${hex(b)}`,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",

    0x3f: "memory.size",
    0x40: "memory.grow",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x50: "i64.eqz",
    0x51: "i64.eq",
    0x52: "i64.ne",
    0x53: "i64.lt_s",
    0x54: "i64.lt_u",
    0x55: "i64.gt_s",
    0x56: "i64.gt_u",
    0x57: "i64.le_s",
    0x58: "i64.le_u",
    0x59: "i64.ge_s",
    0x5a: "i64.ge_u",

    0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",

    0xad: "i64.extend_i32_s",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    0x77: "i32.rotl",
    0x78: "i32.rotr",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",
  };

  return m[op] ?? `UNKNOWN_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

const FUNCTION_INDEX = 1020;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionStart = pos;
  const id = wasm[pos++];

  const sz = uleb(pos);
  pos = sz.next;

  const payloadStart = pos;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payloadStart;
    codeEnd = payloadEnd;
    console.log(
      `CODE section: 0x${codeStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${codeEnd.toString(16)}`
    );
    break;
  }

  pos = payloadEnd;

  if (sectionStart === pos) {
    throw new Error("parser stalled");
  }
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

let p = codeStart;

p = bodyCount.next;

let targetStart = -1;
let targetEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount.value; i++) {
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (i === bodyIndex) {
    targetStart = start;
    targetEnd = end;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (targetStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Target body not found");

console.log(`body end       : ${hex(targetEnd)}`);
console.log(`body size      : ${targetEnd - targetStart}`);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Skip locals
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = targetStart;

const localGroups = uleb(q);
q = localGroups.next;

console.log("LOCALS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
  const count = uleb(q);
  q = count.next;

  const type = wasm[q++];

  let typeName = `0x${type.toString(16)}`;

  if (type === 0x7f) typeName = "i32";
  if (type === 0x7e) typeName = "i64";
  if (type === 0x7d) typeName = "f32";
  if (type === 0x7c) typeName = "f64";

  console.log(`group ${i}: count=${count.value} type=${typeName}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let depth = 0;
let instructionCount = 0;

while (q < targetEnd) {
  const addr = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];

  const prefix = `${hex(addr)}  depth=${depth.toString().padStart(2, " ")}  `;

  let text = opcodeName(op);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const bt = readBlockType(q);
      q = bt.next;
      text += ` type=${bt.text}`;
      console.log(prefix + text);
      depth++;
      continue;
    }

    case 0x05:
      depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
      console.log(prefix + "else");
      depth++;
      continue;

    case 0x0b:
      depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
      console.log(prefix + "end");
      instructionCount++;
      continue;

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const d = uleb(q);
      q = d.next;
      text += ` depth=${d.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = uleb(q);
        q = x.next;
        labels.push(x.value);
      }

      const def = uleb(q);
      q = def.next;

      text += ` labels=[${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const f = uleb(q);
      q = f.next;
      text += ` func=${f.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(q);
      q = t.next;
      const table = uleb(q);
      q = table.next;
      text += ` type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      text += ` local=${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      text += ` global=${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2a:
    case 0x2b:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x2e:
    case 0x2f:
    case 0x30:
    case 0x31:
    case 0x32:
    case 0x33:
    case 0x34:
    case 0x35: {
      const align = uleb(q);
      q = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(q);
      q = offset.next;

      text += ` align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x38:
    case 0x39:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b:
    case 0x3c:
    case 0x3d:
    case 0x3e: {
      const align = uleb(q);
      q = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(q);
      q = offset.next;

      text += ` align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3f:
    case 0x40: {
      const mem = uleb(q);
      q = mem.next;
      text += ` memory=${mem.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb32(q);
      q = x.next;
      text += ` ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb64(q);
      q = x.next;
      text += ` ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1c: {
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      const types: number[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        types.push(wasm[q++]);
      }

      text += ` types=${types
        .map((x) => "0x" + x.toString(16))
        .join(",")}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = uleb(q);
      q = sub.next;
      text += ` subopcode=${sub.value}`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  console.log(prefix + text);

  instructionCount++;

  if (instructionCount > 1000) {
    console.log("STOP: instruction limit reached");
    break;
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Instructions decoded : ${instructionCount}`);
console.log(`Final parser offset  : ${hex(q)}`);
console.log(`Expected body end    : ${hex(targetEnd)}`);
console.log();

console.log("SPECIAL REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = targetStart; i < targetEnd; i++) {
  if (wasm[i] === 0x10) {
    const f = uleb(i + 1);
    console.log(
      `${hex(i)}  call func=${f.value}`
    );
  }

  if (wasm[i] === 0x0f) {
    console.log(`${hex(i)}  RETURN`);
  }

  if (wasm[i] === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb32(i + 1);
    if (
      x.value === -1 ||
      x.value === 0 ||
      x.value === 7 ||
      x.value === 16 ||
      x.value === 48 ||
      x.value === 127
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(i)}  i32.const ${x.value}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

// code section
let p = 0x6e7d;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.p;

const bodies: any[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.v; i++) {
  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.v;
  p = s.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + size;

  const lc = uleb(p);
  p = lc.p;

  for (let j = 0; j < lc.v; j++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.p;
    p++;
  }

  bodies.push({
    start,
    end,
    code: p
  });

  p = end;
}

const f = bodies[116];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 116");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Body start : ${hex(f.start)}`);
console.log(`Code start : ${hex(f.code)}`);
console.log(`Body end   : ${hex(f.end)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

p = f.code;

while (p < f.end) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    const names: Record<number,string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set"
    };

    console.log(`${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD"} ` +
      `op=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`);

    if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.p;
      const c = uleb(p);
      p = c.p;
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x0b || op === 0x05 || op === 0x1a ||
    op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b ||
    op === 0x71 || op === 0x72 ||
    op === 0x73 || op === 0x74 ||
    op === 0x75 || op === 0x76 ||
    op === 0x7c || op === 0x7d ||
    op === 0x7e || op === 0x7f ||
    op === 0x80 || op === 0x81 ||
    op === 0x82 || op === 0x83 ||
    op === 0x84 || op === 0x85 ||
    op === 0x86 || op === 0x87 ||
    op === 0x88
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${r.v}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (b[p] === 0x40) {
      p++;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} block/loop/if 0x40`);
    } else {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} blocktype ${r.v}`);
    }
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END FUNCTION 116");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x126fa;
const END   = 0x1295b;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 ARITHMETIC DATAFLOW DECODER v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range    : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (pos < buf.length) {
        byte = buf[pos++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((byte & 0x40) !== 0 && shift < 63n) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("SLEB64 overflow");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB64 overflow");
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function localName(i: number): string {
    return `L${i}`;
}

function fmtI64(v: bigint): string {
    return v.toString();
}

type Value = string;

const stack: Value[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

let teeCounter = 0;

function pop(): Value {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return "STACK_UNDERFLOW";
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(v: Value) {
    stack.push(v);
}

function setLocal(i: number, v: Value) {
    locals.set(i, v);
}

function getLocal(i: number): Value {
    return locals.get(i) ?? localName(i);
}

function binary(op: string) {
    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a} ${op} ${b})`);
}

function printStack() {
    console.log(
        "    STACK = [" +
        stack.map((x, i) => `${i}:${x}`).join(", ") +
        "]"
    );
}

let p = START;

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(" DECODING");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

while (p < END) {
    const at = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    try {
        switch (op) {

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // Control
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x05:
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  else`);
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  end`);
                break;

            case 0x0f:
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  return`);
                break;

            case 0x1a: {
                const v = pop();
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  drop   ${v}`);
                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // locals
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x20: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = getLocal(r.value);

                push(v);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  local.get ${r.value}   => ${v}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = pop();
                setLocal(r.value, v);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  local.set ${r.value} = ${v}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = pop();

                setLocal(r.value, v);
                push(v);

                teeCounter++;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  local.tee ${r.value} = ${v}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i32 constants
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x41: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                push(`i32.const(${r.value})`);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i32.const ${r.value}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i64 constants
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x42: {
                const r = readSLEB64(p);
                p = r.next;

                push(`i64.const(${fmtI64(r.value)})`);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.const ${fmtI64(r.value)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i32 arithmetic
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x6a:
                binary("i32.add");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.add`);
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                binary("i32.sub");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.sub`);
                break;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i64 arithmetic
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x7c:
                binary("+");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.add`);
                break;

            case 0x7d:
                binary("-");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.sub`);
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                binary("*");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.mul`);
                break;

            case 0x7f:
                binary("div_s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.div_s`);
                break;

            case 0x80:
                binary("div_u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.div_u`);
                break;

            case 0x81:
                binary("rem_s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.rem_s`);
                break;

            case 0x82:
                binary("rem_u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.rem_u`);
                break;

            case 0x83:
                binary("&");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.and`);
                break;

            case 0x84:
                binary("|");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.or`);
                break;

            case 0x85:
                binary("^");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.xor`);
                break;

            case 0x86:
                binary("<<");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.shl`);
                break;

            case 0x87:
                binary(">>s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.shr_s`);
                break;

            case 0x88:
                binary(">>u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.shr_u`);
                break;

            case 0x89:
                binary("rotl");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.rotl`);
                break;

            case 0x8a:
                binary("rotr");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.rotr`);
                break;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // comparisons
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x54:
                binary("<u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.lt_u`);
                break;

            case 0x55:
                binary("<s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.lt_s`);
                break;

            case 0x56:
                binary(">u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.gt_u`);
                break;

            case 0x57:
                binary(">s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.gt_s`);
                break;

            case 0x58:
                binary("<=u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.le_u`);
                break;

            case 0x59:
                binary("<=s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.le_s`);
                break;

            case 0x5a:
                binary(">=u");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.ge_u`);
                break;

            case 0x5b:
                binary(">=s");
                console.log(`${hex(at)}  i64.ge_s`);
                break;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // conversions
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0xa7: {
                const v = pop();
                push(`i32.wrap_i64(${v})`);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i32.wrap_i64(${v})`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0xac: {
                const v = pop();
                push(`i64.extend8_s(${v})`);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.extend8_s(${v})`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0xad: {
                const v = pop();
                push(`i64.extend32_s(${v})`);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.extend32_s(${v})`
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // memory loads
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x29: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                const addr = pop();

                const expr =
                    `MEM64[${addr} + ${r.value}]`;

                push(expr);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}   => ${expr}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // memory stores
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x37: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                const value = pop();
                const addr = pop();

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  i64.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}` +
                    `   MEM64[${addr} + ${r.value}] = ${value}`
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // calls
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                /*
                 * Function 87 has already been proven to consume:
                 *
                 *   destination
                 *   A
                 *   zero
                 *   B
                 *   zero
                 *
                 * and write LOW/HIGH to destination.
                 *
                 * For this arithmetic pass we do not emulate
                 * CALL 87 on the stack. Instead annotate it.
                 */

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  call ${r.value}`
                );

                if (r.value === 87) {
                    console.log(
                        `          [CALL87: writes 128-bit result to destination]`
                    );
                }

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // select
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x1b: {
                const falseValue = pop();
                const trueValue = pop();
                const cond = pop();

                const expr =
                    `(if ${cond} then ${trueValue} else ${falseValue})`;

                push(expr);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  select => ${expr}`
                );

                break;
            }

            default:
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
                );
                break;
        }

        /*
         * Do not print the entire stack after every instruction.
         * Only show it around important dataflow operations.
         */
        if (
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x7c ||
            op === 0x7d ||
            op === 0x83 ||
            op === 0x84 ||
            op === 0x54 ||
            op === 0x56 ||
            op === 0xad ||
            op === 0x37
        ) {
            printStack();
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log();
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(" DECODE ERROR");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`Offset : ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`Opcode : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
        console.log(`Error  : ${String(e)}`);
        console.log();

        break;
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`teeCounter : ${teeCounter}`);
console.log(`Final PC    : ${hex(p)}`);
console.log(`End target  : ${hex(END)}`);
console.log();

console.log("FINAL LOCAL VALUES:");
for (const [k, v] of [...locals.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {
    console.log(`  L${k} = ${v}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("FINAL STACK:");
printStack();

console.log();
console.log("NO TRANSACTION / NO NETWORK ACCESS");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunction122ArithmeticV2.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const OUT =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\function122_arithmetic_v2_full.txt";

const START = 0x126fa;
const END   = 0x1295b;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

type Val = string;

const stack: Val[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Val>();

let output: string[] = [];
let important: string[] = [];

function hx(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb64(pos: number) {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((b & 0x40) !== 0 && shift < 63n) {
                result |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value: result, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function pop(): Val {
    return stack.length
        ? stack.pop()!
        : "STACK_UNDERFLOW";
}

function push(v: Val) {
    stack.push(v);
}

function getLocal(n: number): Val {
    return locals.get(n) ?? `L${n}`;
}

function setLocal(n: number, v: Val) {
    locals.set(n, v);
}

function binary(symbol: string) {
    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(`(${a} ${symbol} ${b})`);
}

function emit(line: string, isImportant = false) {
    output.push(line);

    if (isImportant) {
        important.push(line);
    }
}

function stackShort() {
    if (stack.length === 0) return "[]";

    const start = Math.max(0, stack.length - 6);

    return "[" +
        stack
            .slice(start)
            .map((x, i) => `${start + i}:${x}`)
            .join(", ") +
        "]";
}

emit("============================================================");
emit(" FUNCTION 122 ARITHMETIC DATAFLOW DECODER v2");
emit(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
emit("============================================================");
emit("");
emit(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
emit(`Range     : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
emit("");

let p = START;
let instructionCount = 0;
let unknownCount = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    instructionCount++;

    try {

        switch (op) {

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // control
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x02: {
                const blockType = buf[p++];

                emit(`${hx(at)}  block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x03: {
                const blockType = buf[p++];

                emit(`${hx(at)}  loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const blockType = buf[p++];

                emit(`${hx(at)}  if type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x05:
                emit(`${hx(at)}  else`);
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                emit(`${hx(at)}  end`);
                break;

            case 0x0f:
                emit(`${hx(at)}  return`);
                break;

            case 0x1a: {
                const v = pop();

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  drop ${v}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // locals
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x20: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = getLocal(r.value);

                push(v);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  local.get ${r.value} => ${v}`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = pop();

                setLocal(r.value, v);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  local.set ${r.value} = ${v}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = pop();

                setLocal(r.value, v);
                push(v);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  local.tee ${r.value} = ${v}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // constants
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x41: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                push(`i32.const(${r.value})`);

                emit(`${hx(at)}  i32.const ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const r = sleb64(p);
                p = r.next;

                push(`i64.const(${r.value})`);

                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.const ${r.value}`);
                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i32
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x6a:
                binary("i32.add");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i32.add`);
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                binary("i32.sub");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i32.sub`);
                break;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i64 arithmetic
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x7c:
                binary("+");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.add`);
                break;

            case 0x7d:
                binary("-");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.sub`);
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                binary("*");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.mul`);
                break;

            case 0x7f:
                binary("div_s");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.div_s`);
                break;

            case 0x80:
                binary("div_u");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.div_u`);
                break;

            case 0x81:
                binary("rem_s");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.rem_s`);
                break;

            case 0x82:
                binary("rem_u");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.rem_u`);
                break;

            case 0x83:
                binary("&");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.and`);
                break;

            case 0x84:
                binary("|");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.or`);
                break;

            case 0x85:
                binary("^");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.xor`);
                break;

            case 0x86:
                binary("<<");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.shl`);
                break;

            case 0x87:
                binary(">>s");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.shr_s`);
                break;

            case 0x88:
                binary(">>u");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.shr_u`);
                break;

            case 0x89:
                binary("rotl");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.rotl`);
                break;

            case 0x8a:
                binary("rotr");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.rotr`);
                break;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // comparisons
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x54:
                binary("<u");

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.lt_u => ${stack[stack.length - 1]}`,
                    true
                );

                break;

            case 0x55:
                binary("<s");

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.lt_s => ${stack[stack.length - 1]}`,
                    true
                );

                break;

            case 0x56:
                binary(">u");

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.gt_u => ${stack[stack.length - 1]}`,
                    true
                );

                break;

            case 0x57:
                binary(">s");

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.gt_s => ${stack[stack.length - 1]}`,
                    true
                );

                break;

            case 0x58:
                binary("<=u");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.le_u`);
                break;

            case 0x59:
                binary("<=s");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.le_s`);
                break;

            case 0x5a:
                binary(">=u");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.ge_u`);
                break;

            case 0x5b:
                binary(">=s");
                emit(`${hx(at)}  i64.ge_s`);
                break;

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // conversions
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0xa7: {
                const v = pop();

                push(`i32.wrap_i64(${v})`);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i32.wrap_i64(${v})`
                );

                break;
            }

            case 0xad: {
                const v = pop();

                push(`i64.extend32_s(${v})`);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.extend32_s(${v}) => ${stack[stack.length - 1]}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i64.load
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x29: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const addr = pop();

                const expr =
                    `MEM64[${addr} + ${r.value}]`;

                push(expr);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.load align=${align} offset=${r.value} => ${expr}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // i64.store
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x37: {
                const align = buf[p++];

                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const value = pop();
                const addr = pop();

                const line =
                    `${hx(at)}  i64.store align=${align} offset=${r.value}` +
                    ` => MEM64[${addr} + ${r.value}] = ${value}`;

                emit(line, true);

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // call
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x10: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                if (r.value === 87) {

                    /*
                     * Function 87:
                     *
                     * destination, A, zero, B, zero
                     *
                     * writes:
                     *
                     * destination+0
                     * destination+8
                     *
                     * No stack return values.
                     */

                    emit(
                        `${hx(at)}  CALL87`,
                        true
                    );

                    emit(
                        `          [CALL87 writes LOW/HIGH to memory]`,
                        true
                    );

                } else {

                    emit(
                        `${hx(at)}  call ${r.value}`
                    );
                }

                break;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // select
            // ------------------------------------------------

            case 0x1b: {
                const f = pop();
                const t = pop();
                const c = pop();

                const expr =
                    `(if ${c} then ${t} else ${f})`;

                push(expr);

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  select => ${expr}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
            }

            default:

                unknownCount++;

                emit(
                    `${hx(at)}  UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                    true
                );

                break;
        }

    } catch (e) {

        emit("");
        emit("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
        emit("DECODE ERROR");
        emit(`Offset : ${hx(at)}`);
        emit(`Opcode : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
        emit(`Error  : ${String(e)}`);
        emit("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");

        break;
    }
}

// full output
// ------------------------------------------------------------

emit("");
emit("============================================================");
emit(" SUMMARY");
emit("============================================================");
emit(`Instruction count : ${instructionCount}`);
emit(`Unknown opcodes   : ${unknownCount}`);
emit(`Final PC          : ${hx(p)}`);
emit(`Target END        : ${hx(END)}`);
emit("");

emit("FINAL LOCALS:");

for (const [n, v] of [...locals.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0])) {
    emit(`  L${n} = ${v}`);
}

emit("");
emit("FINAL STACK:");
emit(stackShort());

emit("");

emit("DONE");

// Save complete decode

    OUT,
    output.join("\n"),
    "utf8"
);

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END   = 0x0001295c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number; bytes: string } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    const raw: number[] = [];

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        raw.push(b);

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: p,
                bytes: raw.map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too long at 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF in ULEB at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = pos;
    let byte = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((byte & 0x40) !== 0 && shift < 64n) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return { value: result, next: p };
        }

        if (shift >= 70n) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB too long at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF in SLEB at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readU32(pos: number) {
    return uleb(pos);
}

function memarg(pos: number): { align: number; offset: number; next: number } {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next
    };
}

function decodeAt(pos: number): { next: number; text: string } {
    const op = wasm[pos];

    if (op === undefined) {
        throw new Error(`EOF at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
    }

    const name = names[op];

    if (!name) {
        if (op === 0xfc) {
            const sub = uleb(pos + 1);
            return {
                next: sub.next,
                text: `0xfc <bulk-memory subopcode ${sub.value}>`
            };
        }

        if (op === 0xfd) {
            const sub = uleb(pos + 1);
            return {
                next: sub.next,
                text: `0xfd <SIMD subopcode ${sub.value}>`
            };
        }

        if (op === 0xfe) {
            const sub = uleb(pos + 1);
            return {
                next: sub.next,
                text: `0xfe <atomic subopcode ${sub.value}>`
            };
        }

        throw new Error(
            `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} at 0x${hex(pos)}`
        );
    }

    let p = pos + 1;
    let text = name;

    switch (op) {
        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const bt = wasm[p++];
            text += ` blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text += ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = uleb(p);
            p = n.next;

            const labels: number[] = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                labels.push(x.value);
            }

            const def = uleb(p);
            p = def.next;

            text += ` [${labels.join(", ")}] default=${def.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text += ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;
            text += ` type=${x.value} table=${table.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24:
        case 0x25:
        case 0x26: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text += ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {
            const m = memarg(p);
            p = m.next;
            text += ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x3f:
        case 0x40: {
            const mem = uleb(p);
            p = mem.next;
            text += ` memory=${mem.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text += ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text += ` ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x43:
            p += 4;
            text += ` f32-bytes`;
            break;

        case 0x44:
            p += 8;
            text += ` f64-bytes`;
            break;
    }

    return { next: p, text };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 EXACT DECODER v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : 122`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${hex(START)} .. 0x${hex(END - 1)}`);
console.log("");

let pos = START;
let count = 0;

while (pos < END) {
    const old = pos;

    try {
        const d = decodeAt(pos);

        let raw = "";
        for (let i = old; i < d.next; i++) {
            raw += wasm[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(
            `${count.toString().padStart(4)}  0x${hex(old)}  ` +
            `${raw.padEnd(30)} ${d.text}`
        );

        pos = d.next;
        count++;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
        console.log(`STOP at 0x${hex(pos)}`);
        console.log(String(e));
        console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
        console.log("");

        for (let p = Math.max(START, pos - 16); p < Math.min(END, pos + 32); p += 16) {
            let raw = "";

            for (let q = p; q < Math.min(END, p + 16); q++) {
                raw += wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
            }

            console.log(`0x${hex(p)}  ${raw}`);
        }

        process.exit(1);
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Decoded instructions : ${count}`);
console.log(`Final position       : 0x${hex(pos)}`);
console.log(`Expected end         : 0x${hex(END)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END = 0x1295b;

function hx(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        if (p >= wasm.length) {
            throw new Error(`EOF ULEB @ ${hx(pos)}`);
        }

        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error(`ULEB overflow @ ${hx(pos)}`);
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let byte = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        if (p >= wasm.length) {
            throw new Error(`EOF SLEB @ ${hx(pos)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[p++];

        value += (byte & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < bits && (byte & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`SLEB overflow @ ${hx(pos)}`);
}

function readF32(pos: number): number {
    return pos + 4;
}

function readF64(pos: number): number {
    return pos + 8;
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): {
    align: number;
    offset: number;
    next: number;
} {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: o.value,
        next: o.next,
    };
}
