interface Insn {
    pos: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    loadOffset?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Insn {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    // Control instructions
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos, 33);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const count = readULEB(pos);
        let p = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        const def = readULEB(p);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: def.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} count=${count.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `call ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = readULEB(pos);
        const b = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: b.next,
            op,
            text: `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x1c) {
        const n = readULEB(pos);
        let p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            p++;
        }

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: p,
            op,
            text: `select_t ${n.value}`,
        };
    }

    // locals
    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    // globals
    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    // tables
    if (op === 0x25 || op === 0x26) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    // memory loads/stores
    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const m = readMemArg(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
            loadOffset:
                op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35
                    ? m.offset
                    : undefined,
        };
    }

    // memory size/grow
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        const r = readULEB(pos);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `${name(op)} memory=${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    // constants
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos, 32);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const r = readSLEB(pos, 64);

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: r.next,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${r.value}`,
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x43) {
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: readF32(pos),
            op,
            text: "f32.const",
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x44) {
        return {
            pos: start,
            end: readF64(pos),
            op,
            text: "f64.const",
        };
    }

    // FC prefix - consume subopcode and its known immediate shapes
    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const sub = readULEB(pos);
        let p = sub.next;

        // memory/table bulk operations with immediates
        if (sub.value === 8) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 9) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 10) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 11) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 12) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 13) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 14) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        } else if (sub.value === 15 || sub.value === 16 ||
                   sub.value === 17) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        return {
            pos: start,
            end: p,
            op,
            text: `0xfc.${sub.value}`,
        };
    }

    // Default: no immediate.
    return {
        pos: start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text: name(op),
    };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 EXACT DECODER v2");
console.log(" SIGNED/UNSIGNED LEB128 CORRECTED");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("");

const insns: Insn[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    try {
        const x = decode(p);

        if (x.end <= p) {
            throw new Error(`decoder stall`);
        }

        if (x.end > END) {
            throw new Error(
                `instruction crosses function end: ${hx(x.end)}`
            );
        }

        insns.push(x);
        p = x.end;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            `DECODE ERROR @ ${hx(p)} : ${String(e)}`
        );
        break;
    }
}

console.log(`Decoded instructions : ${insns.length}`);
console.log(`Final decoder offset  : ${hx(p)}`);
console.log(`Expected function end : ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("");

const byPos = new Map<number, Insn>();

for (const x of insns) {
    byPos.set(x.pos, x);
}

const TARGETS = [
    0x124c6,
    0x124f9,
    0x12675,
    0x126fa,
    0x12701,
    0x1283f,
    0x12846,
];

function showAround(target: number, before = 8, after = 20) {
    const idx = insns.findIndex(x => x.pos === target);

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`TARGET ${hx(target)}`);

    if (idx < 0) {
        console.log("NOT FOUND");
        return;
    }

    const lo = Math.max(0, idx - before);
    const hi = Math.min(insns.length, idx + after + 1);

    for (let i = lo; i < hi; i++) {
        const x = insns[i];
        const mark = x.pos === target ? " <== TARGET" : "";

        console.log(
            `${hx(x.pos)}  ${x.text}${mark}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET WINDOWS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    showAround(t);
    console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" EXACT TARGET BOUNDARY CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    const x = byPos.get(t);

    if (!x) {
        console.log(`${hx(t)} : NOT-INSTRUCTION-BOUNDARY`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `${hx(t)} : VALID  ${x.text}  end=${hx(x.end)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DONE - NO TRANSACTION / NO NETWORK ACCESS");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunction122V1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END   = 0x0001295c;

function readULEB(pos: { value: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos.value++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function memArg(pos: { value: number }) {
    const align = readULEB(pos);
    const offset = readULEB(pos);

    return { align, offset };
}

function blockType(pos: { value: number }): string {
    const b = wasm[pos.value++];

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x40: "void",
        0x7f: "i32",
        0x7e: "i64",
        0x7d: "f32",
        0x7c: "f64",
    };

    return names[b] ?? `0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

                    );

                );

        }

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x0;
const TARGET = 136;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
    v |= (-1 << s);
  }

  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  let codeStart = 0;
  let codeEnd = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    const size = s.v;
    p = s.p;

    const payload = p;

    if (id === 10) {
      codeStart = payload;
      codeEnd = payload + size;
      break;
    }

    p += size;
  }

  return { codeStart, codeEnd };
}

const sec = readSections();

let p = sec.codeStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.p;

type Body = {
  ordinal: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  codeStart: number;
};

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.v; i++) {
  const s = uleb(p);
  const bodySize = s.v;
  p = s.p;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  const localCount = uleb(p);
  p = localCount.p;

  for (let j = 0; j < localCount.v; j++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.p;
    p++;
  }

  bodies.push({
    ordinal: i,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
    codeStart: p
  });

  p = bodyEnd;
}

const body = bodies[TARGET];

if (!body) {
  throw new Error("Function body not found");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION ${TARGET}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Body start : ${hex(body.start)}`);
console.log(`Code start : ${hex(body.codeStart)}`);
console.log(`Body end   : ${hex(body.end)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let q = body.codeStart;

while (q < body.end) {
  const at = q;
  const op = b[q++];

  if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
    const r = uleb(q);
    q = r.p;

    const names: Record<number,string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set"
    };

    console.log(`${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(q);
    q = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb(q);
    q = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(q);
    q = r.p;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(q);
    q = a.p;

    const o = uleb(q);
    q = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD"} ` +
      `op=0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = uleb(q);
    q = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`);

    if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.p;

      const c = uleb(q);
      q = c.p;
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x0b || op === 0x05 || op === 0x1a ||
    op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b ||
    op === 0x71 || op === 0x72 || op === 0x73 ||
    op === 0x74 || op === 0x75 || op === 0x76 ||
    op === 0x7c || op === 0x7d ||
    op === 0x7e || op === 0x7f ||
    op === 0x80 || op === 0x81 ||
    op === 0x82 || op === 0x83 ||
    op === 0x84 || op === 0x85 ||
    op === 0x86 || op === 0x87 ||
    op === 0x88
  ) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(q);
    q = r.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${r.v}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (b[q] === 0x40) {
      q++;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} block/loop/if blocktype=0x40`);
    } else {
      const r = sleb(q);
      q = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} blocktype=${r.v}`);
    }

    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 185;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 185 COMPLETE DECODER v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log();

function u8(p: number): number {
  if (p >= wasm.length) throw new Error(`EOF @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  return wasm[p];
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`ULEB too large @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits = 32): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(p++);
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > bits + 7) {
      throw new Error(`SLEB too large @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  if (shift < bits && (b & 0x40)) {
    value -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 0): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function hexBytes(start: number, end: number): string {
  return Array.from(wasm.slice(start, end))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

function readName(pos: number): { name: string; next: number } {
  const n = readULEB(pos);
  const start = n.next;
  const end = start + n.value;

  return {
    name: Buffer.from(wasm.slice(start, end)).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * WASM section discovery
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface Section {
  id: number;
  start: number;
  payloadStart: number;
  payloadEnd: number;
  size: number;
}

function parseSections(): Section[] {
  const sections: Section[] = [];

  if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
  ) {
    throw new Error("Not a WASM binary");
  }

  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const start = p;
    const id = u8(p++);

    const sz = readULEB(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + sz.value;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start,
      payloadStart,
      payloadEnd,
      size: sz.value,
    });

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = parseSections();

console.log("SECTIONS:");
for (const s of sections) {
  console.log(
    ` id=${s.id.toString().padStart(2)} ` +
    `start=${hex(s.start, 8)} ` +
    `payload=${hex(s.payloadStart, 8)}..${hex(s.payloadEnd, 8)} ` +
    `size=${s.size}`
  );
}
console.log();

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Import section
 * Count imported functions.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

let importedFunctions = 0;

const importSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

if (importSection) {
  let p = importSection.payloadStart;
  const end = importSection.payloadEnd;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = readName(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const field = readName(p);
    p = field.next;

    const kind = u8(p++);

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      const typeIdx = readULEB(p);
      p = typeIdx.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      // table
      const elemType = u8(p++);
      void elemType;

      const flags = readULEB(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readULEB(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      // memory
      const flags = readULEB(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readULEB(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readULEB(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      // global
      p += 2;
    } else if (kind === 0x04) {
      // tag
      const attr = readULEB(p);
      p = attr.next;

      const typeIdx = readULEB(p);
      p = typeIdx.next;
    } else {
      throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }

  if (p !== end) {
    console.log(
      `Import parser ended at ${hex(p)}, section end ${hex(end)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log(`Imported function count : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log();

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Type section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface FuncType {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}

function valTypeName(t: number): string {
  switch (t) {
    case 0x7f: return "i32";
    case 0x7e: return "i64";
    case 0x7d: return "f32";
    case 0x7c: return "f64";
    case 0x7b: return "v128";
    case 0x70: return "funcref";
    case 0x6f: return "externref";
    default: return `type(0x${t.toString(16)})`;
  }
}

const types: FuncType[] = [];

const typeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 1);

if (typeSection) {
  let p = typeSection.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const form = u8(p++);

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unsupported type form 0x${form.toString(16)} @ ${hex(p - 1)}`
      );
    }

    const pc = readULEB(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
      params.push(u8(p++));
    }

    const rc = readULEB(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
      results.push(u8(p++));
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Function section
 * Maps defined function index -> type index.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

const functionSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

if (!functionSection) {
  throw new Error("Function section missing");
}

{
  let p = functionSection.payloadStart;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const typeIdx = readULEB(p);
    p = typeIdx.next;

    functionTypes.push(typeIdx.value);
  }
}

const definedOrdinal = TARGET_FUNC - importedFunctions;

console.log(`Target function index   : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`Defined-function ordinal : ${definedOrdinal}`);
console.log();

if (definedOrdinal < 0 || definedOrdinal >= functionTypes.length) {
  throw new Error(
    `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} is not a defined function. ` +
    `defined ordinal=${definedOrdinal}, defined count=${functionTypes.length}`
  );
}

const targetTypeIndex = functionTypes[definedOrdinal];
const targetType = types[targetTypeIndex];

console.log("SIGNATURE:");
console.log(` type index : ${targetTypeIndex}`);

if (targetType) {
  console.log(
    ` params     : ${targetType.params.length} ` +
    `(${targetType.params.map(valTypeName).join(", ")})`
  );

  console.log(
    ` results    : ${targetType.results.length} ` +
    `(${targetType.results.map(valTypeName).join(", ")})`
  );
}

console.log();

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Code section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

const codeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Code section missing");
}

let codeP = codeSection.payloadStart;

const bodyCount = readULEB(codeP);
codeP = bodyCount.next;

console.log(`Code bodies : ${bodyCount.value}`);
console.log();

/* Find target body */
let targetBody:
  | {
      ordinal: number;
      sizeFieldStart: number;
      bodyStart: number;
      bodyEnd: number;
      size: number;
    }
  | undefined;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount.value; ordinal++) {
  const sizeFieldStart = codeP;

  const sz = readULEB(codeP);
  codeP = sz.next;

  const bodyStart = codeP;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sz.value;

  const funcIndex = importedFunctions + ordinal;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {
    targetBody = {
      ordinal,
      sizeFieldStart,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      size: sz.value,
    };
    break;
  }

  codeP = bodyEnd;
}

if (!targetBody) {
  throw new Error(`Body for Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

console.log("TARGET BODY:");
console.log(` body ordinal : ${targetBody.ordinal}`);
console.log(` size field   : ${hex(targetBody.sizeFieldStart, 8)}`);
console.log(` body start   : ${hex(targetBody.bodyStart, 8)}`);
console.log(` body end     : ${hex(targetBody.bodyEnd, 8)}`);
console.log(` body size    : ${targetBody.size}`);
console.log();

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Decode instruction immediates
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

function decodeMemArg(p: number): {
  text: string;
  next: number;
} {
  const align = readULEB(p);
  p = align.next;

  const offset = readULEB(p);
  p = offset.next;

  return {
    text:
      `align=${align.value}` +
      ` offset=${offset.value}` +
      ` (${hex(offset.value)})`,
    next: p,
  };
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): {
  next: number;
  text: string;
} {
  const opPos = pos;
  const op = u8(pos++);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return { next: pos, text: "unreachable" };
    case 0x01: return { next: pos, text: "nop" };

    case 0x02: {
      const b = readSLEB(pos, 33);
      return { next: b.next, text: `block ${hex(b.value)}` };
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const b = readSLEB(pos, 33);
      return { next: b.next, text: `loop ${hex(b.value)}` };
    }

    case 0x04: {
      const b = readSLEB(pos, 33);
      return { next: b.next, text: `if ${hex(b.value)}` };
    }

    case 0x05:
      return { next: pos, text: "else" };

    case 0x0b:
      return { next: pos, text: "end" };

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `br ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `br_if ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = readULEB(pos);
      pos = n.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        labels.push(x.value);
      }

      const def = readULEB(pos);
      pos = def.next;

      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `br_table [${labels.join(", ")}] default=${def.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x0f:
      return { next: pos, text: "return" };

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `call ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      const table = readULEB(pos);
      pos = table.next;

      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${table.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x1a:
      return { next: pos, text: "drop" };

    case 0x1b:
      return { next: pos, text: "select" };

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `local.get ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `local.set ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `local.tee ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `global.get ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `global.set ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.load ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x2a: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `f32.load ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x2b: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `f64.load ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x2c: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.load8_s ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.load8_u ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x2e: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.load16_s ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x2f: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.load16_u ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x30: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load8_s ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x31: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load8_u ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x32: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load16_s ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x33: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load16_u ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x34: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load32_s ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x35: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.load32_u ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.store ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.store ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x38: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `f32.store ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x39: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `f64.store ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.store8 ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x3b: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i32.store16 ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x3c: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.store8 ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x3d: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.store16 ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x3e: {
      const m = decodeMemArg(pos);
      return { next: m.next, text: `i64.store32 ${m.text}` };
    }

    case 0x3f: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `memory.size ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x40: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { next: x.next, text: `memory.grow ${x.value}` };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 32);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex((x.value >>> 0))})`,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 64);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x43: {
      const bytes = hexBytes(pos, pos + 4);
      return { next: pos + 4, text: `f32.const ${bytes}` };
    }

    case 0x44: {
      const bytes = hexBytes(pos, pos + 8);
      return { next: pos + 8, text: `f64.const ${bytes}` };
    }

    /* i32 */
    case 0x45: return { next: pos, text: "i32.eqz" };
    case 0x46: return { next: pos, text: "i32.eq" };
    case 0x47: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ne" };
    case 0x48: return { next: pos, text: "i32.lt_s" };
    case 0x49: return { next: pos, text: "i32.lt_u" };
    case 0x4a: return { next: pos, text: "i32.gt_s" };
    case 0x4b: return { next: pos, text: "i32.gt_u" };
    case 0x4c: return { next: pos, text: "i32.le_s" };
    case 0x4d: return { next: pos, text: "i32.le_u" };
    case 0x4e: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ge_s" };
    case 0x4f: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ge_u" };

    case 0x50: return { next: pos, text: "i64.eqz" };
    case 0x51: return { next: pos, text: "i64.eq" };
    case 0x52: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ne" };
    case 0x53: return { next: pos, text: "i64.lt_s" };
    case 0x54: return { next: pos, text: "i64.lt_u" };
    case 0x55: return { next: pos, text: "i64.gt_s" };
    case 0x56: return { next: pos, text: "i64.gt_u" };
    case 0x57: return { next: pos, text: "i64.le_s" };
    case 0x58: return { next: pos, text: "i64.le_u" };
    case 0x59: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ge_s" };
    case 0x5a: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ge_u" };

    case 0x5b: return { next: pos, text: "f32.eq" };
    case 0x5c: return { next: pos, text: "f32.ne" };
    case 0x5d: return { next: pos, text: "f32.lt" };
    case 0x5e: return { next: pos, text: "f32.gt" };
    case 0x5f: return { next: pos, text: "f32.le" };
    case 0x60: return { next: pos, text: "f32.ge" };
    case 0x61: return { next: pos, text: "f64.eq" };
    case 0x62: return { next: pos, text: "f64.ne" };
    case 0x63: return { next: pos, text: "f64.lt" };
    case 0x64: return { next: pos, text: "f64.gt" };
    case 0x65: return { next: pos, text: "f64.le" };
    case 0x66: return { next: pos, text: "f64.ge" };

    case 0x67: return { next: pos, text: "i32.clz" };
    case 0x68: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ctz" };
    case 0x69: return { next: pos, text: "i32.popcnt" };
    case 0x6a: return { next: pos, text: "i32.add" };
    case 0x6b: return { next: pos, text: "i32.sub" };
    case 0x6c: return { next: pos, text: "i32.mul" };
    case 0x6d: return { next: pos, text: "i32.div_s" };
    case 0x6e: return { next: pos, text: "i32.div_u" };
    case 0x6f: return { next: pos, text: "i32.rem_s" };
    case 0x70: return { next: pos, text: "i32.rem_u" };
    case 0x71: return { next: pos, text: "i32.and" };
    case 0x72: return { next: pos, text: "i32.or" };
    case 0x73: return { next: pos, text: "i32.xor" };
    case 0x74: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shl" };
    case 0x75: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shr_s" };
    case 0x76: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shr_u" };
    case 0x77: return { next: pos, text: "i32.rotl" };
    case 0x78: return { next: pos, text: "i32.rotr" };

    /* i64 */
    case 0x79: return { next: pos, text: "i64.clz" };
    case 0x7a: return { next: pos, text: "i64.ctz" };
    case 0x7b: return { next: pos, text: "i64.popcnt" };
    case 0x7c: return { next: pos, text: "i64.add" };
    case 0x7d: return { next: pos, text: "i64.sub" };
    case 0x7e: return { next: pos, text: "i64.mul" };
    case 0x7f: return { next: pos, text: "i64.div_s" };
    case 0x80: return { next: pos, text: "i64.div_u" };
    case 0x81: return { next: pos, text: "i64.rem_s" };
    case 0x82: return { next: pos, text: "i64.rem_u" };
    case 0x83: return { next: pos, text: "i64.and" };
    case 0x84: return { next: pos, text: "i64.or" };
    case 0x85: return { next: pos, text: "i64.xor" };
    case 0x86: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shl" };
    case 0x87: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shr_s" };
    case 0x88: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shr_u" };
    case 0x89: return { next: pos, text: "i64.rotl" };
    case 0x8a: return { next: pos, text: "i64.rotr" };

    /* conversions */
    case 0xa7: return { next: pos, text: "i32.wrap_i64" };
    case 0xac: return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend_i32_s" };
    case 0xad: return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend_i32_u" };

    /* common float/conversion */
    case 0xaa: return { next: pos, text: "i64.trunc_f32_s" };
    case 0xab: return { next: pos, text: "i64.trunc_f32_u" };
    case 0xae: return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend32_s" };

    /* sign extension */
    case 0xc0: return { next: pos, text: "i32.extend8_s" };
    case 0xc1: return { next: pos, text: "i32.extend16_s" };
    case 0xc2: return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend8_s" };
    case 0xc3: return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend16_s" };
    case 0xc4: return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend32_s" };

    /* prefixes */
    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        next: sub.next,
        text: `0xfc subopcode=${sub.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0xfd: {
      const sub = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        next: sub.next,
        text: `0xfd subopcode=${sub.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0xfe: {
      const sub = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        next: sub.next,
        text: `0xfe subopcode=${sub.value}`,
      };
    }

    default:
      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `UNKNOWN op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      };
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Locals
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

let p = targetBody.bodyStart;

const localDeclCount = readULEB(p);
p = localDeclCount.next;

interface LocalDecl {
  count: number;
  type: number;
}

const localDecls: LocalDecl[] = [];
let totalLocals = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount.value; i++) {
  const n = readULEB(p);
  p = n.next;

  const type = u8(p++);

  localDecls.push({
    count: n.value,
    type,
  });

  totalLocals += n.value;
}

const paramCount = targetType?.params.length ?? 0;
const totalLocalSlots = paramCount + totalLocals;

console.log("LOCALS:");
console.log(` param count       : ${paramCount}`);
console.log(` local declarations: ${localDeclCount.value}`);
console.log(` declared locals   : ${totalLocals}`);
console.log(` total local slots : ${totalLocalSlots}`);

let localIndex = paramCount;

for (const d of localDecls) {
  console.log(
    ` local ${localIndex}..${localIndex + d.count - 1}` +
    ` : ${d.count} x ${valTypeName(d.type)}`
  );

  localIndex += d.count;
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" COMPLETE INSTRUCTION DECODE");
console.log("============================================================");

let instructionCount = 0;
let unknownCount = 0;

while (p < targetBody.bodyEnd) {
  const start = p;

  let decoded;

  try {
    decoded = decodeInstruction(p);
  } catch (e) {
    console.log();
    console.log(
      `DECODE ERROR @ ${hex(start, 8)}: ${
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
      }`
    );
    console.log(
      `remaining bytes: ${hexBytes(start, targetBody.bodyEnd)}`
    );
    break;
  }

  const bytes = hexBytes(start, decoded.next);

  console.log(
    `${hex(start, 8)}  ` +
    `${bytes.padEnd(38)}  ` +
    `${decoded.text}`
  );

  if (decoded.text.startsWith("UNKNOWN")) {
    unknownCount++;
  }

  instructionCount++;
  p = decoded.next;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" VERIFICATION");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`instruction count : ${instructionCount}`);
console.log(`unknown opcodes   : ${unknownCount}`);
console.log(`final decoder pos : ${hex(p, 8)}`);
console.log(`expected body end : ${hex(targetBody.bodyEnd, 8)}`);
console.log(
  `STATUS            : ${
    p === targetBody.bodyEnd && unknownCount === 0
      ? "FULL DECODE OK"
      : p === targetBody.bodyEnd
        ? "REACHED END WITH UNKNOWN OPCODES"
        : "INCOMPLETE DECODE"
  }`
);

console.log();
console.log("READ ONLY - NO NETWORK - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\decodeFunction205Local11ProducerV2.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("WASM path is required.");
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x122dc;
const END = 0x12303;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (b & 0x40) {
                value -= 1n << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function opname(op: number): string {
    const n: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x10: "call",
        0x1a: "drop",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
    };

    return n[op] ?? `OP_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

const stack: string[] = [];

function pop(): string {
    return stack.length
        ? stack.pop()!
        : "<UNDERFLOW>";
}

function push(x: string) {
    stack.push(x);
}

function showStack(): string {
    return "[" + stack.join(", ") + "]";
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 LOCAL11 PRODUCER v2");
console.log(" EXACT STACK EXPRESSION");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    let desc = opname(op);

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            desc += ` ${x.value}`;
            push(`L${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = pop();

            desc += ` ${x.value} <= ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : "<UNDERFLOW>";

            desc += ` ${x.value} <= ${v}`;

            if (x.value === 11) {
                console.log();
                console.log("******** LOCAL11 PRODUCER ********");
                console.log(`address : 0x${addr.toString(16)}`);
                console.log(`value   : ${v}`);
                console.log(`stack   : ${showStack()}`);
                console.log("**********************************");
                console.log();
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            desc += ` ${x.value}`;
            push(`G${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = pop();

            desc += ` ${x.value} <= ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            /*
             * i32.const is normally not needed for the i64 expression,
             * but parse its signed LEB correctly.
             */
            let value = 0;
            let shift = 0;
            let b = 0;

            while (true) {
                b = buf[p++];
                value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
                shift += 7;

                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= 2 ** shift;
            }

            desc += ` ${value}`;
            push(`${value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB64(p);
            p = x.next;

            desc += ` ${x.value}`;
            push(`${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const a = readULEB(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(p);
            p = o.next;

            const base = pop();

            const expr = `MEM64[${base}+0x${o.value.toString(16)}]`;

            desc +=
                ` align=${a.value}` +
                ` offset=${o.value} => ${expr}`;

            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} + ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x6b: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} - ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x7c: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} + ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x7d: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} - ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x7e: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} * ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x83: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} & ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x84: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} | ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x85: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} ^ ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x86: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} << ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x87: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} >>s ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x88: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            const expr = `(${a} >>u ${b})`;

            desc += ` => ${expr}`;
            push(expr);
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            /*
             * Function 87 has:
             *   (i32, i64, i64, i64, i64) -> ()
             *
             * We do not know stack boundaries automatically here,
             * so preserve the call marker instead of corrupting
             * the expression.
             */
            desc += ` ${x.value}`;

            if (x.value === 87) {
                desc += " [FUNCTION 87]";
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x1a: {
            const v = pop();

            desc += ` drop ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        default:
            desc += " [UNHANDLED]";
            break;
    }

    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        `${desc.padEnd(65)} STACK=${showStack()}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\decodeFunction205PreludeV1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("WASM path is required.");
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END = 0x12290;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((b & 0x40) !== 0) {
                value -= 1n << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function hx(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opname(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x0b: "end",
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",
        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",
        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x10: "call",
        0x1a: "drop",
        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
        0x50: "i64.eqz"
    };

    return names[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 PRELUDE v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : 205`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

/*
 * Function 205:
 *
 * body start = 0x1226b
 * code start = 0x1226d
 *
 * The code-start bytes contain:
 *
 *   local declaration vector
 *
 * We parse it first.
 */

const groupInfo = readULEB(p);
const groupCount = groupInfo.value;
p = groupInfo.next;

console.log(`local declaration groups : ${groupCount}`);

let totalLocals = 0;

for (let g = 0; g < groupCount; g++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const type = buf[p++];

    console.log(
        `  group ${g}: count=${countInfo.value} ` +
        `type=0x${type.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
        `locals=${totalLocals}..${totalLocals + countInfo.value - 1}`
    );

    totalLocals += countInfo.value;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INSTRUCTION TRACE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const stack: string[] = [];

function push(x: string) {
    stack.push(x);
}

function pop(): string {
    return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "<STACK_UNDERFLOW>";
}

function showStack() {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return "[]";
    }

    return "[" + stack.join(", ") + "]";
}

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    let text = opname(op);

    switch (op) {
        case 0x20: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            text += ` ${x.value}`;

            push(`L${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = pop();

            text += ` ${x.value}    <= ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : "<STACK_UNDERFLOW>";

            text += ` ${x.value}    <= ${v}`;

            /*
             * local.tee keeps the value on stack.
             */
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            text += ` ${x.value}`;

            push(`G${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = pop();

            text += ` ${x.value}    <= ${v}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readSLEB32(p);
            p = x.next;

            text += ` ${x.value} (${hx(x.value >>> 0)})`;

            push(`${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readSLEB64(p);
            p = x.next;

            text += ` ${x.value}`;

            push(`${x.value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            text += `    ${a} + ${b}`;

            push(`(${a}+${b})`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x6b: {
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            text += `    ${a} - ${b}`;

            push(`(${a}-${b})`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29: {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(p);
            p = offset.next;

            const a = pop();

            text +=
                ` align=${align.value}` +
                ` offset=${offset.value}` +
                `    mem[${a}+${offset.value}]`;

            push(`MEM[${a}+${offset.value}]`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x36:
        case 0x37: {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(p);
            p = offset.next;

            const value = pop();
            const addrExpr = pop();

            text +=
                ` align=${align.value}` +
                ` offset=${offset.value}` +
                `    MEM[${addrExpr}+${offset.value}] = ${value}`;

            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            text += ` ${x.value}`;

            /*
             * We don't attempt signature-based argument popping here.
             * The goal of this first pass is the local2 producer.
             */
            break;
        }

        case 0x1a: {
            const v = pop();
            text += `    drop ${v}`;
            break;
        }

        default:
            /*
             * Stop rather than silently mis-decoding.
             */
            console.log(
                `${hx(addr)}  ${text}  [decoder stop]`
            );

            console.log();
            console.log("DECODER STOP");
            console.log(`offset : ${hx(addr)}`);
            console.log(`opcode : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
            console.log(`stack  : ${showStack()}`);
            process.exit(0);
    }

    console.log(
        `${hx(addr)}  ${text.padEnd(55)} STACK=${showStack()}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`final offset : ${hx(p)}`);
console.log(`stack        : ${showStack()}`);

FILE: scripts\decodeFunction205ProducersV1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("WASM path is required.");
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const WINDOWS = [
    { name: "LOCAL17", start: 0x12320, end: 0x12350 },
    { name: "LOCAL34", start: 0x12435, end: 0x12460 },
];

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (b & 0x40) {
                value -= 1n << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function name(op: number): string {
    const n: Record<number, string> = {
        0x03: "block",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",

        0x1a: "drop",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",

        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",
        0x7f: "i64.div_s",
        0x80: "i64.div_u",
        0x81: "i64.rem_s",
        0x82: "i64.rem_u",
        0x83: "i64.and",
        0x84: "i64.or",
        0x85: "i64.xor",
        0x86: "i64.shl",
        0x87: "i64.shr_s",
        0x88: "i64.shr_u",
    };

    return n[op] ?? `OP_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeWindow(
    label: string,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(` ${label}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {
        const addr = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        let extra = "";

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20:
            case 0x21:
            case 0x22:
            case 0x23:
            case 0x24: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                extra = ` ${x.value}`;

                if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
                    extra += `   [local${x.value}]`;
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = readSLEB32(p);
                p = x.next;

                extra =
                    ` ${x.value}` +
                    ` (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const x = readSLEB64(p);
                p = x.next;

                extra = ` ${x.value}`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x28:
            case 0x29:
            case 0x36:
            case 0x37: {
                const a = readULEB(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = readULEB(p);
                p = o.next;

                extra =
                    ` align=${a.value}` +
                    ` offset=${o.value}` +
                    ` (0x${o.value.toString(16)})`;

                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                extra = ` ${x.value}`;

                if (x.value === 87) {
                    extra += "   [FUNCTION 87]";
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x03:
            case 0x04: {
                const type = buf[p++];
                extra =
                    ` type=0x${type.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0c:
            case 0x0d: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                extra = ` ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            default:
                break;
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${name(op).padEnd(16)}${extra}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 PRODUCER WINDOWS v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);

for (const w of WINDOWS) {
    decodeWindow(w.name, w.start, w.end);
}

console.log();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunction2094V1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const CODE_START = 0x6e7d;
const CODE_END = 0x324ef5;
const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

function readULEB(offset: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        if (x === undefined) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB EOF at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too long at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(offset: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
} {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = offset;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        if (byte === undefined) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB EOF at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((shift < 64n) && (byte & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return {
                value: result,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB too long at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
}

function i64hex(v: bigint): string {
    let x = v;

    if (x < 0n) {
        x = (1n << 64n) + x;
    }

    return `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(16, "0")}`;
}

function u32(offset: number): number {
    return (
        b[offset] |
        (b[offset + 1] << 8) |
        (b[offset + 2] << 16) |
        (b[offset + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

type FuncRange = {
    bodyIndex: number;
    realIndex: number;
    sizeField: number;
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 LIMITED DECODER V1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Build the verified Function Map
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = CODE_START;

const count = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = count.value;
p = count.next;

const funcs: FuncRange[] = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const sizeField = p;

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);

    const codeStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = codeStart + sizeInfo.value;

    funcs.push({
        bodyIndex,
        realIndex: IMPORT_COUNT + bodyIndex,
        sizeField,
        codeStart,
        bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

if (p !== CODE_END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Function map mismatch: ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
    );
}

const target = funcs.find(
    f => f.realIndex === TARGET_FUNC
);

if (!target) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

console.log(`Function bodies : ${functionCount}`);
console.log(`Target Function : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`Body #          : ${target.bodyIndex}`);
console.log(`Size field      : ${hex(target.sizeField)}`);
console.log(`Code start      : ${hex(target.codeStart)}`);
console.log(`Body end        : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`Code bytes      : ${target.bodyEnd - target.codeStart}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Function-local declaration decoder
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let q = target.codeStart;

const localDecl = readULEB(q);
q = localDecl.next;

const localGroups = localDecl.value;

console.log("LOCAL DECLARATIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`local groups : ${localGroups}`);

type LocalInfo = {
    index: number;
    type: number;
};

const locals: LocalInfo[] = [];

for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
    const n = readULEB(q);
    q = n.next;

    const type = b[q++];

    if (type === undefined) {
        throw new Error(`Missing local type at ${hex(q - 1)}`);
    }

    console.log(
        `group #${g.toString().padStart(2, "0")} ` +
        `count=${n.value} type=0x${type.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        locals.push({
            index: locals.length,
            type,
        });
    }
}

console.log(`total declared locals : ${locals.length}`);
console.log("");

console.log("CODE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Limited opcode decoder
 *
 * Important:
 * This is deliberately conservative.
 * Unknown opcodes are reported rather than guessed.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const OPCODES: Record<number, string> = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x50: "i64.eqz",
    0x51: "i64.eq",
    0x52: "i64.ne",
    0x53: "i64.lt_s",
    0x54: "i64.lt_u",
    0x55: "i64.gt_s",
    0x56: "i64.gt_u",
    0x57: "i64.le_s",
    0x58: "i64.le_u",
    0x59: "i64.ge_s",
    0x5a: "i64.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",

    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",

    0x83: "i64.and",
    0x84: "i64.or",
    0x85: "i64.xor",

    0x86: "i64.shl",
    0x87: "i64.shr_s",
    0x88: "i64.shr_u",

    0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
    0xac: "i64.extend_i32_s",
    0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u",
};

let pc = q;
let instructionCount = 0;

const calls: {
    offset: number;
    target: number;
}[] = [];

const stores: {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
}[] = [];

const loads: {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
}[] = [];

while (pc < target.bodyEnd) {
    const start = pc;
    const op = b[pc++];

    if (op === undefined) {
        break;
    }

    instructionCount++;

    const name = OPCODES[op];

    if (!name) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}  ` +
            `UNKNOWN`
        );

        /*
         * Stop here rather than guessing operand width.
         */
        console.log("");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("TRACE STOP");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`Offset : ${hex(start)}`);
        console.log(`Opcode : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
        console.log(`Decoded instructions : ${instructionCount}`);
        console.log(`Expected body end     : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log("");
        console.log("Unknown opcode encountered.");
        console.log("No bytes after this opcode were guessed.");

        process.exit(0);
    }

    /*
     * Control flow
     */
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const blockType = b[pc++];

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(16)} ` +
            `blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b) {
        console.log(`${hex(start)}  ${name}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const depth = readULEB(pc);
        pc = depth.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(16)} depth=${depth.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readULEB(pc);
        pc = n.next;

        const labels: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(pc);
            pc = x.next;
            labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const def = readULEB(pc);
        pc = def.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  br_table ` +
            `count=${n.value} default=${def.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * Calls
     */
    if (op === 0x10) {
        const callee = readULEB(pc);
        pc = callee.next;

        calls.push({
            offset: start,
            target: callee.value,
        });

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  call             ${callee.value}` +
            (callee.value === 205 ? "  <== CALL 205" : "")
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const typeIndex = readULEB(pc);
        pc = typeIndex.next;

        const tableIndex = readULEB(pc);
        pc = tableIndex.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  call_indirect    ` +
            `type=${typeIndex.value} table=${tableIndex.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * Locals / globals
     */
    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
        const idx = readULEB(pc);
        pc = idx.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(16)} ${idx.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * Memory loads/stores
     */
    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39
    ) {
        const align = readULEB(pc);
        pc = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(pc);
        pc = offset.next;

        const textLine =
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(16)} ` +
            `align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

        console.log(textLine);

        if (op === 0x29) {
            loads.push({
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: textLine,
            });
        }

        if (op === 0x37) {
            stores.push({
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                text: textLine,
            });
        }

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * Constants
     */
    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  i32.const        ${x.value.toString()}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  i64.const        ${x.value.toString()} ` +
            `[${i64hex(x.value)}]`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * Everything else in OPCODES is one byte.
     */
    console.log(
        `${hex(start)}  ${name}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DECODE COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Instructions : ${instructionCount}`);
console.log(`Final PC     : ${hex(pc)}`);
console.log(`Body end     : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("CALL SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of calls) {
    console.log(
        `call @ ${hex(c.offset)} -> Function ${c.target}` +
        (c.target === 205 ? "  <== TARGET" : "")
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("LOAD SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const x of loads) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.text}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("i64 STORE SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const x of stores) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.text}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `CALL 205 count : ` +
    `${calls.filter(x => x.target === 205).length}`
);

console.log(`i64.load count : ${loads.length}`);
console.log(`i64.store count: ${stores.length}`);

    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const CODE_START = 0x6e7d;
const CODE_END = 0x324ef5;
const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

function readULEB(offset: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        if (x === undefined) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB EOF @ 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too long @ 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB(offset: number) {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        if (x === undefined) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB EOF @ 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }

        result |= BigInt(x & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (x & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return {
                value: result,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB too long @ 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }
}

    return `0x${v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
}
