}[] = [];

const loads: {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    name: string;
    align: number;
    memOffset: number;
}[] = [];

while (pc < target.bodyEnd) {
    const start = pc;
    const op = b[pc++];

    if (op === undefined) {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF @ ${hex(start)}`);
    }

    instructionCount++;

    const name = NAME[op];

    if (!name && op !== 0xfc) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}  UNKNOWN`
        );

        console.log("");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("TRACE STOP");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`Offset               : ${hex(start)}`);
        console.log(
            `Opcode               : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(`Decoded instructions : ${instructionCount}`);
        console.log(`Expected body end    : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log("");
        console.log("Unknown opcode encountered.");
        console.log("No bytes after this opcode were guessed.");

        process.exit(0);
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * block / loop / if
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
        const bt = b[pc++];

        if (bt === undefined) {
            throw new Error(
                `Missing blocktype @ ${hex(start)}`
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(17)} ` +
            `blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * else / end
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b) {
        console.log(`${hex(start)}  ${name}`);
        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * br / br_if
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const x = readULEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(17)} depth=${x.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * br_table
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = readULEB(pc);
        pc = n.next;

        const labels: number[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(pc);
            pc = x.next;
            labels.push(x.value);
        }

        const def = readULEB(pc);
        pc = def.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  br_table          ` +
            `count=${n.value} default=${def.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * call
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        calls.push({
            offset: start,
            target: x.value,
        });

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  call              ${x.value}` +
            (x.value === 205 ? "  <== CALL 205" : "")
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * call_indirect
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const type = readULEB(pc);
        pc = type.next;

        const table = readULEB(pc);
        pc = table.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  call_indirect     ` +
            `type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * locals / globals
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
        const x = readULEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(17)} ${x.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * Memory instructions
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Every memory opcode has:
     *
     *   align  : ULEB128
     *   offset : ULEB128
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const align = readULEB(pc);
        pc = align.next;

        const memOffset = readULEB(pc);
        pc = memOffset.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  ${name.padEnd(17)} ` +
            `align=${align.value} offset=${memOffset.value}`
        );

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
            loads.push({
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                name,
                align: align.value,
                memOffset: memOffset.value,
            });
        }

        if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
            stores.push({
                offset: start,
                opcode: op,
                name,
                align: align.value,
                memOffset: memOffset.value,
            });
        }

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * i32.const
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  i32.const         ${x.value.toString()}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * i64.const
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(pc);
        pc = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(start)}  i64.const         ${x.value.toString()}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * 0xfc EXTENDED OPCODES
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * 0xfc is an extended-opcode prefix.
     *
     * The subopcode immediately following 0xfc is ULEB128.
     *
     * Known bulk-memory / table instructions:
     *
     *   0x08 memory.init
     *   0x09 data.drop
     *   0x0a memory.copy
     *   0x0b memory.fill
     *   0x0c table.init
     *   0x0d elem.drop
     *   0x0e table.copy
     *   0x0f table.grow
     *   0x10 table.size
     *   0x11 table.fill
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const sub = readULEB(pc);
        pc = sub.next;

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * memory.init
         *
         * immediates:
         *   data index : ULEB128
         *   memory     : ULEB128
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x08) {
            const dataIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = dataIndex.next;

            const memoryIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = memoryIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  memory.init       ` +
                `data=${dataIndex.value} memory=${memoryIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * data.drop
         *
         * immediate:
         *   data index : ULEB128
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x09) {
            const dataIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = dataIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  data.drop         ` +
                `data=${dataIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * memory.copy
         *
         * immediates:
         *   destination memory index : ULEB128
         *   source memory index      : ULEB128
         *
         * We deliberately decode BOTH values.
         * We do not assume they are zero.
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x0a) {
            const dstMemory = readULEB(pc);
            pc = dstMemory.next;

            const srcMemory = readULEB(pc);
            pc = srcMemory.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  memory.copy       ` +
                `dst=${dstMemory.value} src=${srcMemory.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * memory.fill
         *
         * immediate:
         *   memory index : ULEB128
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x0b) {
            const memoryIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = memoryIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  memory.fill       ` +
                `memory=${memoryIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * table.init
         *
         * immediates:
         *   element index : ULEB128
         *   table index    : ULEB128
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x0c) {
            const elemIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = elemIndex.next;

            const tableIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = tableIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  table.init        ` +
                `elem=${elemIndex.value} table=${tableIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * elem.drop
         *
         * immediate:
         *   element index : ULEB128
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x0d) {
            const elemIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = elemIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  elem.drop         ` +
                `elem=${elemIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * table.copy
         *
         * immediates:
         *   destination table : ULEB128
         *   source table      : ULEB128
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x0e) {
            const dstTable = readULEB(pc);
            pc = dstTable.next;

            const srcTable = readULEB(pc);
            pc = srcTable.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  table.copy        ` +
                `dst=${dstTable.value} src=${srcTable.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * table.grow
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x0f) {
            const tableIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = tableIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  table.grow        ` +
                `table=${tableIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * table.size
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x10) {
            const tableIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = tableIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  table.size        ` +
                `table=${tableIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * table.fill
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        if (sub.value === 0x11) {
            const tableIndex = readULEB(pc);
            pc = tableIndex.next;

            console.log(
                `${hex(start)}  table.fill        ` +
                `table=${tableIndex.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         * Unknown 0xfc subopcode
         * ----------------------------------------------------
         */

        console.log("");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("TRACE STOP");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`Offset               : ${hex(start)}`);
        console.log(`Opcode               : 0xfc`);
        console.log(`Subopcode            : ${hex(sub.value)}`);
        console.log(`Decoded instructions : ${instructionCount}`);
        console.log(`Expected body end    : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log("");
        console.log("Unknown 0xfc subopcode.");
        console.log("No bytes after this opcode were guessed.");

        process.exit(0);
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * One-byte numeric opcode
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        `${hex(start)}  ${name}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Final result
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DECODE COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Instructions : ${instructionCount}`);
console.log(`Final PC     : ${hex(pc)}`);
console.log(`Body end     : ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("CALL 205 SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of calls.filter(x => x.target === 205)) {
    console.log(
        `CALL 205 @ ${hex(c.offset)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("ALL CALLS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of calls) {
    console.log(
        `call @ ${hex(c.offset)} -> Function ${c.target}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("LOAD SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const x of loads) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.name.padEnd(17)} ` +
        `align=${x.align} offset=${x.memOffset}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("STORE SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const x of stores) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.name.padEnd(17)} ` +
        `align=${x.align} offset=${x.memOffset}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `CALL 205 count : ${calls.filter(x => x.target === 205).length}`
);
console.log(`LOAD count     : ${loads.length}`);
console.log(`STORE count    : ${stores.length}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunction2330.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_GLOBAL = 2330;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= wasm.length)
            throw new Error("ULEB past EOF");

        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow at 0x" + pos.toString(16));
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            break;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function count
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const fc = uleb(codeStart);
const functionCount = fc.value;

let bodyPos = fc.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 2330 DECODER");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", functionCount);
console.log("TARGET GLOBAL      =", TARGET_GLOBAL);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find body
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const bodyOrdinal = TARGET_GLOBAL - 83;

if (bodyOrdinal < 0 || bodyOrdinal >= functionCount)
    throw new Error("Invalid global function index");

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i <= bodyOrdinal; i++) {

    const bs = uleb(bodyPos);

    bodyStart = bs.next;
    bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

    bodyPos = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("BODY ORDINAL =", bodyOrdinal);
console.log("BODY START   =", hex(bodyStart));
console.log("BODY END     =", hex(bodyEnd));
console.log("BODY SIZE    =", bodyEnd - bodyStart);
console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Raw bytes
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW BODY BYTES");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(bodyStart, bodyEnd))
        .toString("hex")
        .match(/.{1,2}/g)
        ?.join(" ")
);

console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find exact interesting LOAD @ 0xB3CCD
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const INTEREST = 0x000b3ccd;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INTERESTING LOCATION");
console.log("============================================================");

const from = Math.max(bodyStart, INTEREST - 64);
const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, INTEREST + 96);

for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

    const e = Math.min(q + 16, to);

    console.log(
        hex(q),
        Buffer.from(wasm.subarray(q, e))
            .toString("hex")
            .match(/.{1,2}/g)
            ?.join(" ")
    );
}

console.log();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Lightweight instruction decoder
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSTRUCTION STREAM");
console.log("============================================================");

let q = bodyStart;

// locals
const localDecl = uleb(q);
q = localDecl.next;

let localCount = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {

    const count = uleb(q);
    q = count.next;

    q++;
    localCount += count.value;
}

console.log("LOCAL DECL GROUPS =", localDecl.value);
console.log("EXTRA LOCALS      =", localCount);
console.log();

let depth = 0;

while (q < bodyEnd) {

    const pos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let text = "";

    switch (op) {

        case 0x0b:
            text = "end";
            depth--;
            break;

        case 0x05:
            text = "else";
            break;

        case 0x02: {
            const x = sleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `block ${x.value}`;
            depth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const x = sleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `loop ${x.value}`;
            depth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const x = sleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `if ${x.value}`;
            depth++;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `br ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `call ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;

            const y = uleb(q);
            q = y.next;

            text = `call_indirect type=${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            text = `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x2c: {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            text = `i32.load8_s align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            text = `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = uleb(q);
            q = a.next;

            const b = uleb(q);
            q = b.next;

            text = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb(q);
            q = x.next;
            text = `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value >>> 0)})`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a:
            text = "i32.add";
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            text = "i32.sub";
            break;

        case 0x6c:
            text = "i32.mul";
            break;

        case 0x71:
            text = "i32.and";
            break;

        case 0x72:
            text = "i32.or";
            break;

        case 0x73:
            text = "i32.xor";
            break;

        case 0x74:
            text = "i32.shl";
            break;

        default:
            text = `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            break;
    }

    console.log(
        `${hex(pos)}  ${text}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunction249V1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x00018c42;
const END   = 0x00018c62;

function readULEB(pos: { value: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos.value++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 249 DECODER v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let p = START;

/*
 * Function body begins with local declarations.
 *
 * First byte:
 *   01 = one local declaration group
 *
 * Then:
 *   02 7f
 *
 * means:
 *   two i32 locals
 */

const localGroups = wasm[p++];

console.log(
    `${hex(START)} local.decl.groups = ${localGroups}`
);

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
    const countPos = { value: p };
    const count = readULEB(countPos);
    p = countPos.value;

    const type = wasm[p++];

    const typeName =
        type === 0x7f ? "i32" :
        type === 0x7e ? "i64" :
        type === 0x7d ? "f32" :
        type === 0x7c ? "f64" :
        `0x${type.toString(16)}`;

    console.log(
        `  group ${i}: ${count} × ${typeName}`
    );
}

while (p < END) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get ${local}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.set ${local}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.tee ${local}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const value = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${value}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = wasm[p++];

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} if blockType=0x${blockType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} else`);
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} end`);
            break;

        case 0x10: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const funcIndex = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;

            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} call ${funcIndex}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0xfc: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const subOpcode = readULEB(tmp);
            p = tmp.value;

            if (subOpcode === 0x0a) {
                const tmp1 = { value: p };
                const dstMem = readULEB(tmp1);
                p = tmp1.value;

                const tmp2 = { value: p };
                const srcMem = readULEB(tmp2);
                p = tmp2.value;

                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)} memory.copy dstMem=${dstMem} srcMem=${srcMem}`
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    `${hex(at)} 0xfc subopcode=${subOpcode}`
                );
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x45:
            console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.eqz`);
            break;

        default:
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );

            console.log();
            console.log(`Stopped at ${hex(at)}`);

            process.exit(0);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x14380;
const END   = 0x14570;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function i32(n: number) {
    return n | 0;
}

function decode(pos: number) {
    const op = wasm[pos];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x20: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`local.get ${r.value}`, r.next];
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`local.set ${r.value}`, r.next];
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`local.tee ${r.value}`, r.next];
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`, r.next];
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`i64.const ${r.value}`, r.next];
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
            const b = readULEB(a.next);
            return [`i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${hex(b.value)}`, b.next];
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
            const b = readULEB(a.next);
            return [`i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${hex(b.value)}`, b.next];
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
            const b = readULEB(a.next);
            return [`i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${hex(b.value)}`, b.next];
        }

        case 0x6a: return ["i32.add", pos + 1];
        case 0x6b: return ["i32.sub", pos + 1];
        case 0x68: return ["i32.mul", pos + 1];
        case 0x71: return ["i32.and", pos + 1];
        case 0x72: return ["i32.or", pos + 1];
        case 0x73: return ["i32.xor", pos + 1];
        case 0x74: return ["i32.shr_u", pos + 1];
        case 0x75: return ["i32.shr_s", pos + 1];
        case 0x76: return ["i32.rotl", pos + 1];
        case 0x77: return ["i32.rotr", pos + 1];

        case 0x45: return ["i32.eqz", pos + 1];
        case 0x46: return ["i32.eq", pos + 1];
        case 0x47: return ["i32.ne", pos + 1];

        case 0x0b: return ["end", pos + 1];
        case 0x0c: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`br ${r.value}`, r.next];
        }
        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
            return [`br_if ${r.value}`, r.next];
        }

        case 0x04: return ["if", pos + 1];
        case 0x03: return ["loop", pos + 1];
        case 0x02: return ["block", pos + 1];

        case 0x1b: return ["select", pos + 1];

        default:
            return [`op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`, pos + 1];
    }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x15200;
const END   = 0x15317;
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 LOCAL WINDOW v22");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`START     : 0x${START.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`END       : 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function uleb(offset: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) break;
    }

    return null;
}

function hex(bytes: number[]) {
    return bytes
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function decode(offset: number) {
    const op = wasm[offset];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x41: {
            const x = uleb(offset + 1);
            if (!x) return null;
            return {
                text: `i32.const ${x.value} (0x${x.value.toString(16)})`,
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = uleb(offset + 1);
            if (!x) return null;
            return {
                text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x20:
            return { text: `local.get ${wasm[offset + 1]}`, next: offset + 2 };

        case 0x21:
            return { text: `local.set ${wasm[offset + 1]}`, next: offset + 2 };

        case 0x22:
            return { text: `local.tee ${wasm[offset + 1]}`, next: offset + 2 };

        case 0x23:
            return { text: `global.get ${wasm[offset + 1]}`, next: offset + 2 };

        case 0x24:
            return { text: `global.set ${wasm[offset + 1]}`, next: offset + 2 };

        case 0x28: {
            const align = wasm[offset + 1];
            const x = uleb(offset + 2);
            if (!x) return null;
            return {
                text: `i32.load align=${align} offset=0x${x.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const align = wasm[offset + 1];
            const x = uleb(offset + 2);
            if (!x) return null;
            return {
                text: `i64.load align=${align} offset=0x${x.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const align = wasm[offset + 1];
            const x = uleb(offset + 2);
            if (!x) return null;
            return {
                text: `i32.store align=${align} offset=0x${x.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = wasm[offset + 1];
            const x = uleb(offset + 2);
            if (!x) return null;
            return {
                text: `i64.store align=${align} offset=0x${x.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: x.next
            };
        }

        case 0x04:
            return { text: "if", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x05:
            return { text: "else", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x0b:
            return { text: "end", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(offset + 1);
            if (!x) return null;
            return { text: `br ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(offset + 1);
            if (!x) return null;
            return { text: `br_if ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
        }

        case 0x1b:
            return { text: "select", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x45:
            return { text: "i32.eqz", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x46:
            return { text: "i32.eq", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x47:
            return { text: "i32.ne", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x48:
            return { text: "i32.lt_s", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x49:
            return { text: "i32.lt_u", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x4a:
            return { text: "i32.gt_s", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x4b:
            return { text: "i32.gt_u", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x6a:
            return { text: "i32.add", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x6b:
            return { text: "i32.sub", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x68:
            return { text: "i32.mul", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x71:
            return { text: "i32.and", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x72:
            return { text: "i32.or", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x73:
            return { text: "i32.xor", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x74:
            return { text: "i32.shr_u", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x75:
            return { text: "i32.shr_s", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x76:
            return { text: "i32.rotl", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x77:
            return { text: "i32.rotr", next: offset + 1 };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(offset + 1);
            if (!x) return null;
            return { text: `call ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
        }

        default:
            return {
                text: `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                next: offset + 1
            };
    }
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const result = decode(p);

    if (!result) {
        console.log(`0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  [decode error]`);
        p++;
        continue;
    }

    const raw = hex(Array.from(wasm.slice(p, result.next)));

    console.log(
        `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        `${raw.padEnd(32)}  ${result.text}`
    );

    p = result.next;
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x148af;
const END   = 0x1493c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 - BLOCK 148AF..1493C v25");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`RANGE     : 0x${START.toString(16)} - 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: p
            };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid SLEB128");
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= 1n << shift;
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: p
            };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Invalid SLEB128 i64");
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function raw(pos: number, n = 32) {
    return Array.from(
        wasm.subarray(pos, Math.min(pos + n, END))
    )
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function memoryOp(
    addr: number,
    name: string,
    p: number
) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    console.log(
        `${name.padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
        `align=${align.value} offset=${hex8(offset.value)}`
    );

    console.log(`             RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

    return p;
}

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Control flow
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x02: {
            const r = readSLEB32(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `block        @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
                `type=${r.value === 64 ? "empty" : r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const r = readSLEB32(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `loop         @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
                `type=${r.value === 64 ? "empty" : r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const r = readSLEB32(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `if           @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
                `type=${r.value === 64 ? "empty" : r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            console.log(`else         @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            console.log(`end          @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `br           @ ${hex8(addr)} depth=${r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `br_if        @ ${hex8(addr)} depth=${r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            console.log(`return       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Locals
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22: {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const name =
                op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
                op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
                "local.tee";

            console.log(
                `${name.padEnd(12)} @ ${hex8(addr)} local=${r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32.const
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x41: {
            const r = readSLEB32(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `i32.const    @ ${hex8(addr)} value=${r.value} ` +
                `(${hex8(r.value >>> 0)})`
            );
            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.const
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x42: {
            const r = readSLEB64(p);
            p = r.next;

            console.log(
                `i64.const    @ ${hex8(addr)} value=${r.value}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Loads
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x28:
            p = memoryOp(addr, "i32.load", p);
            break;

        case 0x29:
            p = memoryOp(addr, "i64.load", p);
            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Stores
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x36:
            p = memoryOp(addr, "i32.store", p);
            break;

        case 0x37:
            p = memoryOp(addr, "i64.store", p);
            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Comparisons / arithmetic / bitwise
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x45:
            console.log(`i32.eqz      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x46:
            console.log(`i32.eq       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x47:
            console.log(`i32.ne       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x48:
            console.log(`i32.lt_s     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x49:
            console.log(`i32.lt_u     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x4a:
            console.log(`i32.gt_s     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x4b:
            console.log(`i32.gt_u     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x4c:
            console.log(`i32.le_s     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x4d:
            console.log(`i32.le_u     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x4e:
            console.log(`i32.ge_s     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x4f:
            console.log(`i32.ge_u     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x6a:
            console.log(`i32.add      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            console.log(`i32.sub      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x6c:
            console.log(`i32.mul      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x71:
            console.log(`i32.and      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x72:
            console.log(`i32.or       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x73:
            console.log(`i32.xor      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x74:
            console.log(`i32.shl      @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x75:
            console.log(`i32.shr_s     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x76:
            console.log(`i32.shr_u     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x77:
            console.log(`i32.rotl     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        case 0x78:
            console.log(`i32.rotr     @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // select
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x1b:
            console.log(`select       @ ${hex8(addr)}`);
            break;

        default:
            console.log(
                `UNKNOWN      @ ${hex8(addr)} ` +
                `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );

            console.log(`             RAW: ${raw(addr)}`);

            console.log("");
            console.log(
                "STOP: unknown instruction; decoder intentionally stopped."
            );

            process.exit(0);
    }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x14e80;
const END   = 0x14fe0;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 - LOCAL 7 CONTEXT DECODE v22");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    `RANGE     : 0x${START.toString(16)} - 0x${END.toString(16)}`

function u32leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: result >>> 0,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        value: 0,
        next: pos + 1,
    };
}

function i32leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return {
                value: result | 0,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        value: 0,
        next: pos + 1,
    };
}

function hexByte(b: number): string {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

function hexBytes(a: number, b: number): string {
    return Array.from(wasm.slice(a, b))
        .map(hexByte)
        .join(" ");
}

function decodeAt(pos: number): {
    text: string;
    next: number;
} {
    const op = wasm[pos];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
            return { text: "unreachable", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x01:
            return { text: "nop", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x02: {
            const bt = wasm[pos + 1];
            return {
                text: `blocktype 0x${hexByte(bt)}`,
                next: pos + 2,
            };
        }

        case 0x03: {
            const bt = wasm[pos + 1];
            return {
                text: `looptype 0x${hexByte(bt)}`,
                next: pos + 2,
            };
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const bt = wasm[pos + 1];
            return {
                text: `if type 0x${hexByte(bt)}`,
                next: pos + 2,
            };
        }

        case 0x05:
            return { text: "else", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x0b:
            return { text: "end", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x0c: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `br ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `br_if ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `call ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x18: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `delegate/op_0x18 ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const r = u32leb(pos + 1);
            return {
                text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const align = wasm[pos + 1];
            const off = u32leb(pos + 2);

            return {
                text: `i32.load align=${align} offset=0x${off.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: off.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const align = wasm[pos + 1];
            const off = u32leb(pos + 2);

            return {
                text: `i32.store align=${align} offset=0x${off.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: off.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = wasm[pos + 1];
            const off = u32leb(pos + 2);

            return {
                text: `i64.store align=${align} offset=0x${off.value.toString(16)}`,
                next: off.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = i32leb(pos + 1);

            const unsigned = r.value >>> 0;

            return {
                text:
                    `i32.const ${r.value}` +
                    ` (0x${unsigned.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")})`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const r = i32leb(pos + 1);

            return {
                text: `i64.const ${r.value}`,
                next: r.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x45:
            return { text: "i32.eqz", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x46:
            return { text: "i32.eq", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x47:
            return { text: "i32.ne", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x4d:
            return { text: "i32.lt_u / op_0x4d", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x67:
            return { text: "i32.clz / op_0x67", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x6a:
            return { text: "i32.add", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x6b:
            return { text: "i32.sub", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x68:
            return { text: "i32.mul", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x71:
            return { text: "i32.and", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x72:
            return { text: "i32.or", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x73:
            return { text: "i32.xor", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x74:
            return { text: "i32.shr_u", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x76:
            return { text: "i32.rotl", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x77:
            return { text: "i32.rotr", next: pos + 1 };

        case 0x1b:
            return { text: "select", next: pos + 1 };

        default:
            return {
                text: `op_0x${hexByte(op)}`,
                next: pos + 1,
            };
    }
}

console.log(" DECODED INSTRUCTIONS");

let p = START;
let count = 0;

while (p < END) {
    const before = p;

    try {
        const d = decodeAt(p);

        console.log(
            `0x${before.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${hexBytes(before, d.next).padEnd(28)}  ` +
            `${d.text}`
        );

        p = d.next;
        count++;

        if (count > 1000) {
            console.log("STOP: decoder instruction limit reached.");
            break;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            `0x${before.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${hexByte(wasm[before])}  DECODE ERROR`
        );

        p++;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");

for (let q = START; q < END - 1; q++) {
    if (wasm[q] === 0x21 && wasm[q + 1] === 0x07) {
        const a = Math.max(START, q - 20);
        const b = Math.min(END, q + 25);

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `HIT local.set 7 @ 0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(`RAW: ${hexBytes(a, b)}`);
    }
}

while (p < END) {
    const before = p;

    try {
        const d = decodeAt(p);

        console.log(
            `0x${before.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${hexBytes(before, d.next).padEnd(28)}  ` +
            `${d.text}`
        );

        p = d.next;
        count++;

        if (count > 1000) {
            console.log("STOP: decoder instruction limit reached.");
            break;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            `0x${before.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
            `${hexByte(wasm[before])}  DECODE ERROR`
        );

        p++;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RAW CONTEXT AROUND local.set 7");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let q = START; q < END - 1; q++) {
    if (wasm[q] === 0x21 && wasm[q + 1] === 0x07) {
        const a = Math.max(START, q - 20);
        const b = Math.min(END, q + 25);

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `HIT local.set 7 @ 0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log(`RAW: ${hexBytes(a, b)}`);
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\decodeFunction3550IfV58.ts

import fs from "fs";

const path =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x001245a1;
const END   = 0x0012460b;

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3550 IF-BODY DECODER v58");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

type T = "i32" | "i64";

interface V {
    type: T;
    expr: string;
}

const stack: V[] = [];

// The value surviving before IF in the real stack.
stack.push({
    type: "i32",
    expr: "local4"
});

function push(type: T, expr: string) {
    stack.push({ type, expr });
}

function pop(type?: T): V {
    const v = stack.pop();

    if (!v) {
        throw new Error("STACK UNDERFLOW");
    }

    if (type && v.type !== type) {
        throw new Error(
            `TYPE MISMATCH: expected ${type}, got ${v.type}: ${v.expr}`
        );
    }

    return v;
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[pos++];

        result |=
            BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40))
        result |= (-1n) << shift;

    return { value: result, next: pos };
}

function binary(
    op: string,
    type: T
) {
    const rhs = pop(type);
    const lhs = pop(type);

    push(
        type,
        `(${lhs.expr} ${op} ${rhs.expr})`
    );
}

function showStack() {
    return stack.length
        ? stack.map(
            (v, i) =>
                `${i}:${v.type}:${v.expr}`
          ).join(" | ")
        : "[]";
}

let p = START;

let depth = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const addr = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    try {

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const type: T =
                    r.value === 4
                        ? "i32"
                        : "i64";

                push(type, `local${r.value}`);

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `local.get ${r.value} [${type}]`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = pop();

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `local.set ${r.value} = ` +
                    `${v.type} ${v.expr}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                const v = pop();

                push(v.type, v.expr);

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `local.tee ${r.value} = ` +
                    `${v.type} ${v.expr}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const r = sleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                push("i32", r.value.toString());

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `i32.const ${r.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x42: {
                const r = sleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                push(
                    "i64",
                    r.value.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `i64.const ${r.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x52: {
                const rhs = pop("i64");
                const lhs = pop("i64");

                push(
                    "i32",
                    `(${lhs.expr} != ${rhs.expr})`
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.ne`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x54: {
                const rhs = pop("i64");
                const lhs = pop("i64");

                push(
                    "i32",
                    `(${lhs.expr} <u ${rhs.expr})`
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.lt_u`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x72:
                binary("|", "i32");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i32.or`
                );
                break;

            case 0x7c:
                binary("+", "i64");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.add`
                );
                break;

            case 0x7d:
                binary("-", "i64");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.sub`
                );
                break;

            case 0x7e:
                binary("*", "i64");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.mul`
                );
                break;

            case 0x83:
                binary("&", "i64");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.and`
                );
                break;

            case 0x84:
                binary("|", "i64");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.or`
                );
                break;

            case 0x86: {
                const rhs = pop("i64");
                const lhs = pop("i64");

                push(
                    "i64",
                    `(${lhs.expr} << ${rhs.expr})`
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.shl`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x88: {
                const rhs = pop("i64");
                const lhs = pop("i64");

                push(
                    "i64",
                    `(${lhs.expr} >>u ${rhs.expr})`
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.shr_u`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x87: {
                const rhs = pop("i64");
                const lhs = pop("i64");

                push(
                    "i64",
                    `(${lhs.expr} >>s ${rhs.expr})`
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  i64.shr_s`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const cond = pop("i32");

                depth++;

                console.log("");
                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  IF depth=${depth}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  condition = ${cond.expr}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  outer stack = ${showStack()}`
                );
                console.log("");

                /*
                 * Important:
                 * IF consumes only its condition.
                 * The surviving local4 remains below it.
                 */
                break;
            }

            case 0x05:
                console.log("");
                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ELSE depth=${depth}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `  stack = ${showStack()}`
                );
                console.log("");
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                if (depth > 0) {
                    console.log("");
                    console.log(
                        `0x${addr.toString(16)}  END IF depth=${depth}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `  stack = ${showStack()}`
                    );
                    console.log("");

                    depth--;
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        `0x${addr.toString(16)}  END`
                    );
                }
                break;

            case 0x3f: {
                // memory.size memidx=0
                const r = uleb(p);
                p = r.next;

                push(
                    "i32",
                    "memory.size"
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  memory.size`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0xfc: {
                const sub = uleb(p);
                p = sub.next;

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `0xfc subopcode=${sub.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                const base = pop("i32");

                push(
                    "i32",
                    `MEM32[${base.expr}+${o.value}]`
                );

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `i32.load offset=${o.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                const value = pop("i64");
                const addrExpr = pop("i32");

                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `i64.store offset=${o.value} ` +
                    `value=${value.expr} ` +
                    `addr=${addrExpr.expr}`
                );
                break;
            }

            default:
                console.log(
                    `0x${addr.toString(16)}  ` +
                    `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
                );
                break;
        }

    } catch (e) {

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `*** TRACE ERROR at ` +
            `0x${addr.toString(16)} ***`
        );

        console.log(
            e instanceof Error
                ? e.message
                : String(e)
        );

        console.log(
            `STACK = ${showStack()}`
        );

        console.log("");

        break;
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FINAL STACK");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(showStack());
console.log("");
console.log("No bytes modified.");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x1243d3;
const END = 0x124729;

function readULEB(pos: { value: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos.value++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.lt_s`);
    break;

            console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.eqz`);
            break;

case 0x6a:

        case 0x88:

        }

        case 0x22: {
            const tmp = { value: p };
            const local = readULEB(tmp);

            p = tmp.value;

            console.log(`${hex(at)} local.tee ${local}`);
            break;
        }

case 0x11: {
    const tmp = { value: p };

    const typeIndex = readULEB(tmp);
    const tableIndex = readULEB(tmp);

    p = tmp.value;

    console.log(
        `${hex(at)} call_indirect type=${typeIndex} table=${tableIndex}`
    );

    break;
}
case 0x0c: {
    const tmp = { value: p };

    const depth = readULEB(tmp);

    p = tmp.value;

    console.log(
        `${hex(at)} br ${depth}`
    );

    break;
}

case 0x0b: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} end`);
    break;
}

case 0x52: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.ne`);
    break;
}
case 0x54: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.lt_u`);
    break;
}

case 0x72: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.or`);
    break;
}
case 0x05: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} else`);
    break;
}

case 0x23: {
    const globalIndex = wasm[p++];
    console.log(`${hex(at)} global.get ${globalIndex}`);
    break;
}

case 0x6b: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.sub`);
    break;
}

case 0x24: {
    const globalIndex = wasm[p++];
    console.log(`${hex(at)} global.set ${globalIndex}`);
    break;
}

case 0x02: {
    const blockType = wasm[p++];
    console.log(`${hex(at)} block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
    break;
}

case 0x45: {
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.eqz`);
    break;
}

case 0xfc: {
    const tmp = { value: p };

    const subOpcode = readULEB(tmp);

    p = tmp.value;

    console.log(
        `${hex(at)} 0xfc subopcode=${subOpcode}`
    );

    if (subOpcode === 0x0b) {
        const tmp2 = { value: p };

        const memidx = readULEB(tmp2);

        p = tmp2.value;

        console.log(
            `  memory.fill memidx=${memidx}`
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            `  extended opcode not yet decoded`
        );
    }

    break;
}
case 0x10: {
    const tmp = { value: p };
    const funcIndex = readULEB(tmp);
    p = tmp.value;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} call ${funcIndex}`);
    break;
}

case 0x3a: {
    const tmp = { value: p };
    const align = readULEB(tmp);
    const offset = readULEB(tmp);
    p = tmp.value;
    console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.store8 align=${align} offset=${offset}`);
    break;
}
        default:
            console.log(
                `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
            );

            console.log();
            console.log(`Stopped at ${hex(at)}`);

            process.exit(0);
    }
}
