            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i32.add`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.add
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x7c: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i64",
                `(${a.expr} + ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.add`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.sub
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x7d: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i64",
                `(${a.expr} - ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.sub`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i32.or
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x72: {
            const b = pop("i32");
            const a = pop("i32");

            push(
                "i32",
                `(${a.expr} | ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i32.or`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.and
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x83: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i64",
                `(${a.expr} & ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.and`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.or
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x84: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i64",
                `(${a.expr} | ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.or`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.shr_u
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x88: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i64",
                `(${a.expr} >>u ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.shr_u`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.ne
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x52: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i32",
                `(${a.expr} != ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.ne`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.lt_u
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x54: {
            const b = pop("i64");
            const a = pop("i64");

            push(
                "i32",
                `(${a.expr} <u ${b.expr})`
            );

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} i64.lt_u`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // IF
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x04: {

            const resultType =
                readBlockType();

            const cond =
                pop("i32");

            const ctrl: Ctrl = {
                kind: "if",
                resultType,
                entryHeight: stack.length,
                condition: cond.expr,
            };

            controls.push(ctrl);

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} IF -> ${resultType}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  condition = ${cond.expr}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  entry stack = [` +
                stack.map(
                    (v, i) =>
                        `${i}:${v.type}:${v.expr}`
                ).join(" | ") +
                `]`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // ELSE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x05: {

            const ctrl =
                controls[controls.length - 1];

            if (!ctrl || ctrl.kind !== "if") {
                throw new Error(
                    `ELSE without IF @ 0x${at.toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            if (ctrl.resultType !== "void") {

                const thenResult =
                    pop(ctrl.resultType);

                ctrl.thenResult =
                    thenResult;
            }

            stack.length =
                ctrl.entryHeight;

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} ELSE`
            );

            console.log(
                `  restore height = ` +
                `${ctrl.entryHeight}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  stack = [` +
                stack.map(
                    (v, i) =>
                        `${i}:${v.type}:${v.expr}`
                ).join(" | ") +
                `]`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // END
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x0b: {

            if (controls.length === 0) {

                console.log(
                    `0x${at.toString(16)} END`
                );

                break;
            }

            const ctrl =
                controls.pop()!;

            if (ctrl.resultType !== "void") {

                const elseResult =
                    pop(ctrl.resultType);

                if (!ctrl.thenResult) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `IF THEN result missing ` +
                        `@ 0x${at.toString(16)}`
                    );
                }

                const merged =
                    `IF(${ctrl.condition}) ? ` +
                    `${ctrl.thenResult.expr} : ` +
                    `${elseResult.expr}`;

                push(
                    ctrl.resultType,
                    merged
                );

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    `0x${at.toString(16)} END`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  IF result = ${ctrl.resultType}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  result = ${merged}`
                );

            } else {

                console.log(
                    `0x${at.toString(16)} END`
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  stack = [` +
                stack.map(
                    (v, i) =>
                        `${i}:${v.type}:${v.expr}`
                ).join(" | ") +
                `]`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // i64.store
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        case 0x37: {

            const align =
                buf[p++];

            const offset =
                u32();

            const value =
                pop("i64");

            const address =
                pop("i32");

            console.log(
                `0x${at.toString(16)} ` +
                `i64.store offset=${offset}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  address = ${address.expr}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  value   = ${value.expr}`
            );

            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // UNKNOWN
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        default:

            throw new Error(
                `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x` +
                op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0") +
                ` @ 0x${at.toString(16)}`
            );
    }
}

type Val = {
    type: "i32" | "i64";
    expr: string;
};

type Ctrl = {
    kind: "if";
    resultType: "i32" | "i64" | "void";
    entryHeight: number;
    condition: string;
    thenResult?: Val;
};

let p = START;

const stack: Val[] = [
    { type: "i32", expr: "local4" },
    {
        type: "i64",
        expr:
            "IF(C2) ? local7 : (((local10 >>u 52) - 1) & 281474976710655)"
    }
];

const controls: Ctrl[] = [];

function u32(): number {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(b & 0x80))
            return v >>> 0;

        s += 7;
    }
}

function i64leb(): bigint {
    let v = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        v |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) {
            if (shift < 63n && (b & 0x40))
                v |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);

            return BigInt.asIntN(64, v);
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function hex64(v: bigint): string {
    return (
        "0x" +
        BigInt.asUintN(64, v)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(16, "0")
    );
}

function localType(n: number): "i32" | "i64" {
    return n <= 5 ? "i32" : "i64";
}

function pop(type?: "i32" | "i64"): Val {
    const v = stack.pop();

    if (!v)
        throw new Error(
            `STACK UNDERFLOW @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
        );

    if (type && v.type !== type) {
        throw new Error(
            `TYPE ERROR @ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}: ` +
            `expected ${type}, got ${v.type}: ${v.expr}`
        );
    }

    return v;
}

function push(
    type: "i32" | "i64",
    expr: string
) {
    stack.push({ type, expr });
}

function readBlockType():
    "i32" | "i64" | "void"
{
    const b = buf[p++];

    if (b === 0x7f) return "i32";
    if (b === 0x7e) return "i64";
    if (b === 0x40) return "void";

    throw new Error(
        `UNKNOWN BLOCK TYPE 0x${b.toString(16)} ` +
        `@ 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
    );
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x001243ce;
const END   = 0x00124729;

const FUNCTION_INDEX = 3550;
const TARGET_CALLEE = 3632;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3550 FULL DECODER v50");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : ${FUNCTION_INDEX}`);
console.log(`Start     : 0x${START.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`End       : 0x${END.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`Length    : ${END - START} bytes`);
console.log("");

if (wasm.length !== 5093309) {
    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected WASM size: ${wasm.length}`
    );
}

let p = START;

function hx(n: number, width = 8): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) {
            throw new Error(
                `ULEB past END @ ${hx(p)}`
            );
        }

        const b = wasm[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `ULEB too large @ ${hx(p)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) {
            throw new Error(
                `SLEB32 past END @ ${hx(p)}`
            );
        }

        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `SLEB32 too large @ ${hx(p)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return result;
}

function readSLEB64(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) {
            throw new Error(
                `SLEB64 past END @ ${hx(p)}`
            );
        }

        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |=
            BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(
                `SLEB64 too large @ ${hx(p)}`
            );
        }
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return BigInt.asIntN(64, result);
}

function readMemarg() {
    const align = readULEB();
    const offset = readULEB();

    return {
        align,
        offset
    };
}

function print(at: number, text: string) {
    console.log(
        `${hx(at)}  ${text}`
    );
}

while (p < END) {

    const at = p;

            print(at, `block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
            break;

            print(at, `loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = wasm[p++];
            print(at, `if type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x05:
            print(at, "else");
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            print(at, "end");
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const depth = readULEB();
            print(at, `br ${depth}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const depth = readULEB();
            print(at, `br_if ${depth}`);
            break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const count = readULEB();
            const labels: number[] = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                labels.push(readULEB());
            }

            const def = readULEB();

            print(
                at,
                `br_table [${labels.join(", ")}] default=${def}`
            );

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            print(at, "return");
            break;

        // calls

                );

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x001243ce;
const END   = 0x00124729;

const TARGET_CALLEE = 3632;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3550 DECODER v51");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Start     : 0x${START.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`End       : 0x${END.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`Length    : ${END - START}`);

if (wasm.length !== 5093309) {
    throw new Error(`Unexpected WASM size: ${wasm.length}`);
}

let p = START;

function hx(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB past END @ ${hx(p)}`);
        }

        const b = wasm[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too large @ ${hx(p)}`);
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB32 past END @ ${hx(p)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB32 too large @ ${hx(p)}`);
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return result;
}

function readSLEB64(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB64 past END @ ${hx(p)}`);
        }

        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB64 too large @ ${hx(p)}`);
        }
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (-1n) << shift;
    }

    return BigInt.asIntN(64, result);
}

function readMemarg() {
    return {
        align: readULEB(),
        offset: readULEB()
    };
}

function readBlockType(): string {
    const b = wasm[p];

    if (b === 0x40) {
        p++;
        return "void";
    }

    if (
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c
    ) {
        p++;
        return `valtype=0x${b.toString(16)}`;
    }

    const v = readSLEB32();
    return `typeidx=${v}`;
}

function print(at: number, s: string) {
    console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${s}`);
}

let instructions = 0;
let calls3632 = 0;
let depth = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    instructions++;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
            print(at, "unreachable");
            break;

        case 0x01:

            break;

            const bt = readBlockType();
            print(at, `block ${bt}`);
            depth++;
            break;

            const bt = readBlockType();
            print(at, `loop ${bt}`);
            depth++;
            break;

            print(at, `if ${bt}`);
            depth++;
            break;

            break;

            break;

            break;

        case 0x6e:
            print(at, "i32.div_u");
            break;

        case 0x71:
            print(at, "i32.and");
            break;

            break;

        case 0x76:
            print(at, "i32.shr_u");
            break;

        case 0x7c:
            print(at, "i64.add");
            break;

        case 0x7d:
            print(at, "i64.sub");
            break;

        case 0x7e:
            print(at, "i64.mul");
            break;

            print(at, "i64.and");
            break;

        case 0x84:
            print(at, "i64.or");
            break;

        case 0x85:
            print(at, "i64.xor");
            break;

        case 0x86:
            print(at, "i64.shl");
            break;

        case 0x87:
            print(at, "i64.shr_s");
            break;

        case 0x88:
            print(at, "i64.shr_u");
            break;

        /*
         * 0xfc = WebAssembly extended opcode prefix.
         *
         * IMPORTANT:
         * Do not guess unknown subopcodes.
         *
         * For this function the first occurrence is:
         *
         *   fc 0b 00
         *
         * We report the exact subopcode and immediate byte,
         * while consuming the known byte sequence.
         */
        case 0xfc: {
            const sub = readULEB();

            if (sub === 0x0b) {
                const immediate = readULEB();

                print(
                    at,
                    `0xfc subopcode=0x0b immediate=${immediate}`
                );
            } else {
                throw new Error(
                    `UNKNOWN 0xfc SUBOPCODE=0x${sub.toString(16)} ` +
                    `at ${hx(at)}`
                );
            }

            break;
        }

        default:
            throw new Error(
                `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
                `at ${hx(at)}`
            );
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const path =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x001243d3;
const END   = 0x00124729;

let p = START;

function hx(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(): number {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0)
            return v;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function sleb32(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];
        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40))
        result |= (-1 << shift);

    return result;
}

function sleb64(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40))
        result |= (-1n) << shift;

    return BigInt.asIntN(64, result);
}

function memarg() {
    const align = uleb();
    const offset = uleb();
    return `align=${align} offset=${offset}`;
}

function blocktype(): string {
    const x = b[p];

    if (x === 0x40) {
        p++;
        return "void";
    }

    if (
        x === 0x7f ||
        x === 0x7e ||
        x === 0x7d ||
        x === 0x7c ||
        x === 0x7b
    ) {
        p++;
        return `valtype=0x${x.toString(16)}`;
    }

    return `typeidx=${sleb32()}`;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x12422b;
const END   = 0x1243cc;

function readULEB(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
    bytes: number[];
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    const bytes: number[] = [];

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        bytes.push(b);

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: p,
                bytes
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`ULEB too long at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading ULEB at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function readSLEB32(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
    bytes: number[];
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    const bytes: number[] = [];
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        bytes.push(b);

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: p,
                bytes
            };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB32 too long at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading SLEB32 at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function readSLEB64(pos: number): {
    value: bigint;
    next: number;
    bytes: number[];
} {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let p = pos;
    const bytes: number[] = [];
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        bytes.push(b);

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7n;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 64n && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return {
                value,
                next: p,
                bytes
            };
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error(`SLEB64 too long at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF reading SLEB64 at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function printInstruction(
    addr: number,
    end: number,
    text: string
) {
    console.log(
        `${hex(addr)} .. ${hex(end - 1)}  ${text}`
    );
}

let p = START;
let instructionCount = 0;

while (p < END) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    try {

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // CONTROL
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        switch (op) {

            case 0x00:
                printInstruction(addr, p, "unreachable");
                break;

            case 0x01:
                printInstruction(addr, p, "nop");
                break;

            case 0x02: {
                const blockType = wasm[p++];
                let typeText = "";

                if (blockType === 0x40) {
                    typeText = "empty";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7f) {
                    typeText = "i32";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7e) {
                    typeText = "i64";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7d) {
                    typeText = "f32";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7c) {
                    typeText = "f64";
                } else {
                    typeText = `blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                }

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `block type=${typeText}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x03: {
                const blockType = wasm[p++];
                let typeText = "";

                if (blockType === 0x40) {
                    typeText = "empty";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7f) {
                    typeText = "i32";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7e) {
                    typeText = "i64";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7d) {
                    typeText = "f32";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7c) {
                    typeText = "f64";
                } else {
                    typeText = `blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                }

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `loop type=${typeText}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x04: {
                const blockType = wasm[p++];
                let typeText = "";

                if (blockType === 0x40) {
                    typeText = "empty";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7f) {
                    typeText = "i32";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7e) {
                    typeText = "i64";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7d) {
                    typeText = "f32";
                } else if (blockType === 0x7c) {
                    typeText = "f64";
                } else {
                    typeText = `blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
                }

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `if type=${typeText}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x05:
                printInstruction(addr, p, "else");
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                printInstruction(addr, p, "end");
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `br depth=${r.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `br_if depth=${r.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const count = readULEB(p);
                p = count.next;

                const labels: number[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
                    const r = readULEB(p);
                    p = r.next;
                    labels.push(r.value);
                }

                const def = readULEB(p);
                p = def.next;

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `br_table labels=[${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x0f:
                printInstruction(addr, p, "return");
                break;

            // --------------------------------------------------------
            // CALLS
            // --------------------------------------------------------

            case 0x10: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                let suffix = "";

                if (r.value === 60) {
                    suffix = "  <<< IMPORT #60 = a.ga >>>";
                }

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `call function=${r.value}${suffix}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const type = readULEB(p);
                p = type.next;

                const table = readULEB(p);
                p = table.next;

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x12: {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `return_call function=${r.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x13: {
                const type = readULEB(p);
                p = type.next;

                const table = readULEB(p);
                p = table.next;

                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    `return_call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
                );
                break;
            }

            case 0x14: {
                printInstruction(
                    addr,
                    p,
                    "call_ref"
                );
                break;
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------------

        );
        console.log(err);
        break;

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x0012422b;
const END   = 0x001243cc;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3632 FULL DECODER v47");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : 3632`);
console.log(`Start     : 0x${START.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`End       : 0x${END.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`Length    : ${END - START} bytes`);
console.log("");

if (wasm.length !== 5093309) {
    throw new Error(`Unexpected WASM size: ${wasm.length}`);
}

function hex(n: number, width = 2): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) throw new Error("ULEB past function end");

        const b = wasm[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function readSLEB64(): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (p >= END) throw new Error("SLEB past function end");

        byte = wasm[p++];

        result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7n;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((shift < 64n) && (byte & 0x40)) {
                result |= (-1n) << shift;
            }

            return result;
        }

        if (shift > 70n) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too large");
        }
    }
}

function readMemarg() {
    const align = readULEB();
    const offset = readULEB();

    return { align, offset };
}

function line(at: number, text: string) {
    console.log(
        `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${text}`
    );
}

let p = START;

let instructionCount = 0;
let storeCount = 0;
let unknownCount = 0;

const stores: {
    index: number;
    at: number;
}[] = [];

while (p < END) {
    const at = p;
    const opcode = wasm[p++];

    instructionCount++;

    switch (opcode) {

        // local.get
        case 0x20: {
            const index = readULEB();
            line(at, `local.get ${index}`);
            break;
        }

        // i64.load
        case 0x29: {
            const { align, offset } = readMemarg();

            line(
                at,
                `i64.load align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // i64.load16_u
        case 0x33: {
            const { align, offset } = readMemarg();

            line(
                at,
                `i64.load16_u align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // i64.load32_u
        case 0x35: {
            const { align, offset } = readMemarg();

            line(
                at,
                `i64.load32_u align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // i64.store8
        case 0x3c: {
            const { align, offset } = readMemarg();

            storeCount++;

            stores.push({
                index: offset,
                at
            });

            line(
                at,
                `i64.store8 align=${align} offset=${offset}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // i64.const
        case 0x42: {
            const value = readSLEB64();

            line(
                at,
                `i64.const ${value.toString()}`
            );
            break;
        }

        // i64.and
        case 0x83:
            line(at, "i64.and");
            break;

        // i64.or
        case 0x84:
            line(at, "i64.or");
            break;

        // i64.shl
        case 0x86:
            line(at, "i64.shl");
            break;

        // i64.shr_u
        case 0x88:
            line(at, "i64.shr_u");
            break;

        // end
        case 0x0b:
            line(at, "end");

            if (p !== END) {
                console.log(
                    `WARNING: end encountered at 0x${at.toString(16)}, ` +
                    `but expected final end at 0x${(END - 1).toString(16)}`
                );
            }

            break;

        default:
            unknownCount++;

            line(
                at,
                `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${hex(opcode)}`
            );

            throw new Error(
                `Parser stopped at 0x${at.toString(16)} ` +
                `opcode=0x${hex(opcode)}`
            );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" STORE8 DESTINATION MAP");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const s of stores) {
    console.log(
        `dest[${s.index.toString().padStart(2, "0")}] ` +
        `@ 0x${s.at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `Instruction count : ${instructionCount}`
);

console.log(
    `i64.store8 count  : ${storeCount}`
);

console.log(
    `Unknown opcodes   : ${unknownCount}`
);

const expectedOffsets = Array.from(
    { length: 32 },
    (_, i) => i
);

const actualOffsets = stores.map(s => s.index);

const exact32 =
    actualOffsets.length === 32 &&
    actualOffsets.every((v, i) => v === expectedOffsets[i]);

console.log("");

if (exact32) {
    console.log(
        "STATUS: EXACT 32-BYTE DESTINATION SEQUENCE 0x00..0x1f"
    );
} else {
    console.log(
        "STATUS: DESTINATION SEQUENCE DOES NOT MATCH 0x00..0x1f"
    );
}

if (p === END) {
    console.log("STATUS: EXACT END MATCH");
} else {
    console.log(
        `STATUS: END MISMATCH parser=0x${p.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("No bytes modified.");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 3632;
const TARGET_IMPORT = 60;
const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function hex(pos: number, count: number): string {
    return Array.from(bytes.subarray(pos, pos + count))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const sectionId = bytes[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
        const payloadSize = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const payloadStart = p;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return {
                start: payloadStart,
                size: payloadSize,
            };
        }

        p += payloadSize;
    }

    throw new Error(`Section ${id} not found`);
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",
        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x1b: "select",

        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",

        0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
        0xad: "i64.extend_i32_s",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3632 -> IMPORT #60 ANALYSIS v41");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size: ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);

const code = findSection(10);

console.log(
    `Code section: offset=0x${code.start.toString(16)} size=${code.size}`
);
console.log();

let p = code.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readU32Leb(p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

console.log(`Defined function bodies: ${bodyCount}`);
console.log(`Target function: ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`Import count: ${IMPORT_COUNT}`);
console.log();

const targetBodyIndex = TARGET_FUNC - IMPORT_COUNT;

if (targetBodyIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Target function is imported, not defined.");
}

let targetBodyStart = -1;
let targetBodyEnd = -1;
let targetInstructionStart = -1;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyIndex === targetBodyIndex) {
        targetBodyStart = bodyStart;
        targetBodyEnd = bodyEnd;

        let q = bodyStart;

        const localDecls = readU32Leb(q);
        q = localDecls.next;

        console.log(`Body index: ${bodyIndex}`);
        console.log(`Body start: 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`Body end:   0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`Body size:  ${bodySize}`);
        console.log(`Local groups: ${localDecls.value}`);
        console.log();

        for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
            const countInfo = readU32Leb(q);
            q = countInfo.next;

            const typeByte = bytes[q++];

            console.log(
                `  local group ${i}: count=${countInfo.value}, type=0x${typeByte
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(2, "0")}`
            );
        }

        targetInstructionStart = q;

        break;
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

if (
    targetBodyStart < 0 ||
    targetBodyEnd < 0 ||
    targetInstructionStart < 0
) {
    throw new Error("Function 3632 body not found.");
}

console.log();
console.log(
    `Instruction start: 0x${targetInstructionStart.toString(16)}`
);
console.log();

const targetCalls: number[] = [];

for (
    let q = targetInstructionStart;
    q < targetBodyEnd;
) {
    const opOffset = q;
    const op = bytes[q++];

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const info = readU32Leb(q);
        q = info.next;

        if (info.value === TARGET_IMPORT) {
            targetCalls.push(opOffset);
        }

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const info = readU32Leb(q);
        q = info.next;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const info = readS32Leb(q);
        q = info.next;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const info = readU32Leb(q);
        q = info.next;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 || op === 0x2d) {
        const align = readU32Leb(q);
        q = align.next;

        const offset = readU32Leb(q);
        q = offset.next;

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36 || op === 0x37) {
        const align = readU32Leb(q);
        q = align.next;

        const offset = readU32Leb(q);
        q = offset.next;

        continue;
    }

    if (
        op === 0x02 ||
        op === 0x03 ||
        op === 0x04
    ) {
        q++;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
        const info = readU32Leb(q);
        q = info.next;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const countInfo = readU32Leb(q);
        q = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value + 1; i++) {
            const info = readU32Leb(q);
            q = info.next;
        }

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const typeInfo = readU32Leb(q);
        q = typeInfo.next;

        const tableInfo = readU32Leb(q);
        q = tableInfo.next;

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x1c) {
        const countInfo = readU32Leb(q);
        q = countInfo.next + countInfo.value;

        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
        q++;
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
        continue;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORT #60 CALLS");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Total calls to import #60: ${targetCalls.length}`);
console.log();

for (let i = 0; i < targetCalls.length; i++) {
    const callOffset = targetCalls[i];

    console.log(`CALL #${i + 1}`);
    console.log(`offset: 0x${callOffset.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`target: function #${TARGET_IMPORT} = a.ga`);

    const end = Math.min(targetBodyEnd, callOffset + 32);

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `bytes 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${(end - 1).toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log(hex(start, end - start));
    console.log();
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0014b240;
const END   = 0x0014b350;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if ((b & 0x40) !== 0)
                value -= Math.pow(2, shift);

            return { value, next: pos };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readString(v: number) {
    return hex(v);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 3790 DISPATCH WINDOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Window:", hex(START), "..", hex(END));
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    let text = "";

    switch (op) {

        case 0x0b:
            text = "end";
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `br ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            text = "return";
            break;

        case 0x10: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `call ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = uleb(p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;

            text = `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
            text = "drop";
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x2c: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            text = `i32.load8_s align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            text = `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            text = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const a = uleb(p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = uleb(p);
            p = o.next;

            text = `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            text = `i32.const ${x.value} (${readString(x.value >>> 0)})`;

            if ((x.value >>> 0) >= 0x00187000 &&
                (x.value >>> 0) <= 0x0018f600) {
                text += "  <<< SEGMENT DATA";
            }

            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            text = `i64.const ${x.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x45:
            text = "i32.eqz";
            break;

        case 0x46:
            text = "i32.eq";
            break;

        case 0x47:
            text = "i32.ne";
            break;

        case 0x48:
            text = "i32.lt_s";
            break;

        case 0x4e:
            text = "i32.ge_u";
            break;

        case 0x6a:
            text = "i32.add";
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            text = "i32.sub";
            break;

        case 0x71:
            text = "i32.and";
            break;

        case 0x7c:
            text = "i64.add";
            break;

        case 0x7e:
            text = "i64.and";
            break;

        default:
            text = `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
            break;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(opPos)}  ${text}`);
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: result >>> 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];

    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= ~0 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function sleb64(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];

    result |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (-1n) << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: BigInt.asIntN(64, result),
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x10: "call",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",

    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",

    0x3f: "memory.size",
    0x40: "memory.grow",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x50: "i64.eqz",
    0x51: "i64.eq",
    0x52: "i64.ne",
    0x53: "i64.lt_s",
    0x54: "i64.lt_u",
    0x55: "i64.gt_s",
    0x56: "i64.gt_u",
    0x57: "i64.le_s",
    0x58: "i64.le_u",
    0x59: "i64.ge_s",
    0x5a: "i64.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",
    0x7f: "i64.div_s",
    0x80: "i64.div_u",
    0x81: "i64.rem_s",
    0x82: "i64.rem_u",
    0x83: "i64.and",
    0x84: "i64.or",
    0x85: "i64.xor",
    0x86: "i64.shl",
    0x87: "i64.shr_s",
    0x88: "i64.shr_u",

    0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
    0xad: "i64.extend_i32_s",
    0xae: "i64.extend_i32_u",
  };

  return m[op] ?? `UNKNOWN_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

  const addr = q;

  let line =
    `${hex(addr)}  depth=${depth.toString().padStart(2, " ")}  ` +
    opcodeName(op);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {

      const btName =
        bt === 0x40 ? "empty" :
        bt === 0x7f ? "i32" :
        bt === 0x7e ? "i64" :
        bt === 0x7d ? "f32" :
        bt === 0x7c ? "f64" :
        hex(bt);

      line += ` type=${btName}`;
      console.log(line);

      depth++;
      continue;
    }

    case 0x05:
      depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
      console.log(line);
      depth++;
      continue;

    case 0x0b:
      depth = Math.max(0, depth - 1);
      console.log(line);
      count++;
      continue;

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const d = uleb(q);
      q = d.next;
      line += ` depth=${d.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const f = uleb(q);
      q = f.next;
      line += ` func=${f.value}`;
      break;

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      line += ` local=${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      line += ` global=${x.value}`;
      break;

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x2e:
    case 0x2f:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37: {
      const align = uleb(q);
      q = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(q);
      q = offset.next;

      line +=
        ` align=${align.value}` +
        ` offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x3f:
    case 0x40: {
      const mem = uleb(q);
      q = mem.next;
      line += ` memory=${mem.value}`;
      break;

    case 0x41: {

    default:
      break;

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunction8620.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 8620;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

type Instr = {
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    text: string;
    target?: number;
};

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= ~0 << shift;
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }
    }
}

function readU64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function skipMemArg(pos: number): number {
    const a = readU32Leb(pos);
    return readU32Leb(a.next).next;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): {
    instr: Instr;
    next: number;
} {
    const start = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "unreachable",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x01:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "nop",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const blockType = readS32Leb(pos);
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text:
                        op === 0x02
                            ? `block ${blockType.value}`
                            : op === 0x03
                              ? `loop ${blockType.value}`
                              : `if ${blockType.value}`,
                },
                next: blockType.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x05:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "else",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x0b:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "end",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `br ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readU32Leb(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                pos = readU32Leb(pos).next;
            }

            pos = readU32Leb(pos).next;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "br_table",
                },
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        case 0x0f:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "return",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `call ${x.value}`,
                    target: x.value,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const type = readU32Leb(pos);
            pos = type.next;

            const table = readU32Leb(pos);
            pos = table.next;

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        case 0x1a:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "drop",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x1b:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "select",
                },
                next: pos,
            };

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x23: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x24: {
            const x = readU32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x28:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "i32.load",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x29:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "i64.load",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x2a:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f32.load",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x2b:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f64.load",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x36:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "i32.store",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x37:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "i64.store",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x38:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f32.store",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x39:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f64.store",
                },
                next: skipMemArg(pos),
            };

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readS32Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const x = readU64Leb(pos);

            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
                },
                next: x.next,
            };
        }

        case 0x43:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f32.const",
                },
                next: pos + 4,
            };

        case 0x44:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: "f64.const",
                },
                next: pos + 8,
            };

        default:
            return {
                instr: {
                    offset: start,
                    opcode: op,
                    text: `op=0x${op
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, "0")}`,
                },
                next: pos,
            };
    }
}

function findCodeSection(): {
    start: number;
    size: number;
} {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = readU32Leb(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start: p,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function findTargetBody(): {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    bodyIndex: number;
} {
    const sec = findCodeSection();

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const targetBodyIndex =
        TARGET_FUNC - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

    if (
        targetBodyIndex < 0 ||
        targetBodyIndex >= count.value
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} is outside code section`,
        );
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const size = readU32Leb(p);
        p = size.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size.value;

        if (i === targetBodyIndex) {
            return {
                start,
                end,
                bodyIndex: i,
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error(
        `Body for Function ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`,
    );
}

function decodeBody(
    start: number,
    end: number,
): Instr[] {
    let p = start;

    const localDecls = readU32Leb(p);
    p = localDecls.next;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < localDecls.value;
        i++
    ) {
        const n = readU32Leb(p);
        p = n.next;

        p++; // valtype
    }

    const result: Instr[] = [];

    while (p < end) {
        const d = decodeInstruction(p);

        if (d.next <= p) {
            throw new Error(
                `Decoder stalled at 0x${p.toString(16)}`,
            );
        }

        result.push(d.instr);
        p = d.next;
    }

    return result;
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return (
        "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
}

function isInteresting(x: Instr): boolean {
    return (
        x.text.includes("1837608") ||
        x.text.includes("8620") ||
        x.text.includes("call_indirect") ||
        x.text.includes("i32.load") ||
        x.text.includes("i32.store") ||
        x.text.includes("local.")
    );
}

async function main() {
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " DECODE FUNCTION 8620",
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target    : Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log(
        `Body idx  : ${
            TARGET_FUNC - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS
        }`,
    );
    console.log();

    const body = findTargetBody();

    console.log(
        `Body start : ${hex(body.start)}`,
    );
    console.log(
        `Body end   : ${hex(body.end)}`,
    );
    console.log(
        `Body size  : ${body.end - body.start}`,
    );
    console.log();

    const instructions = decodeBody(
        body.start,
        body.end,
    );

    console.log(
        `Instructions decoded : ${instructions.length}`,
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " FULL FUNCTION 8620",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    for (const x of instructions) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.text}`,
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " INTERESTING INSTRUCTIONS",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const x = instructions[i];

        if (!isInteresting(x)) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(x.offset)}  ${x.text}`,
        );

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 3);
        const to = Math.min(
            instructions.length,
            i + 4,
        );

        for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
            if (j === i) continue;

            console.log(
                `    ${hex(
                    instructions[j].offset,
                )}  ${instructions[j].text}`,
            );
        }

        console.log();
    }

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(" END");
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
}

main().catch((err) => {
    console.error();
    console.error("ERROR");
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
});

console.log(

function u8(p: number): number {
  if (p >= wasm.length) {
    throw new Error(`OOB byte read @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  }
  return wasm[p];
}

function readSLEB128(start: number): {
  value: bigint;
  next: number;
  bytes: number[];
} {
  let p = start;
  let result = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  const bytes: number[] = [];

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    bytes.push(b);

    result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if ((b & 0x40) !== 0) {
        result -= 1n << (shift + 7n);
      }
      return { value: result, next: p, bytes };
    }

    shift += 7n;

    if (shift > 70n) {
      throw new Error(
        `SLEB128 too long @ 0x${start.toString(16)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

function hex8(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function fmtI64(v: bigint): string {
  return v.toString();
}

function opName(op: number): string {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",
    0x7f: "i64.div_s",
    0x80: "i64.div_u",
    0x81: "i64.rem_s",
    0x82: "i64.rem_u",
    0x83: "i64.and",
    0x84: "i64.or",
    0x85: "i64.xor",
    0x86: "i64.shl",
    0x87: "i64.shr_s",
    0x88: "i64.shr_u",
    0x89: "i64.rotl",
    0x8a: "i64.rotr",

    0x37: "i64.store",

    0x0b: "end",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`;
}

type Expr = string;

interface LocalState {
  version: number;
  expr: Expr;
}

let locals: LocalState[] = Array.from(
  { length: 6 },
  (_, i) => ({
    version: 0,
    expr: `L${i}_0`,
  })
);

const stack: Expr[] = [];

const stores: {
  offset: number;
  align: number;
  value: Expr;
  address: Expr;
}[] = [];

let p = FUNC87_CODE_START;
let instructionCount = 0;

function pop(): Expr {
  if (stack.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(
      `STACK UNDERFLOW @ ${hex8(p)}`
    );
  }

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function push(x: Expr) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function bin(
  name: string
) {
  const rhs = pop();
  const lhs = pop();

  push(`(${lhs} ${name} ${rhs})`);
}

function newLocalVersion(
  localIndex: number,
  expr: Expr
): string {
  locals[localIndex].version++;

  const v =
    `L${localIndex}_${locals[localIndex].version}`;

  locals[localIndex].expr = expr;

  return v;
}

function parseLocalIndex(start: number): {
  value: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const r = readSLEB128(start);
  const n = Number(r.value);

  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 0 || n > 100000) {
    throw new Error(
      `Invalid local index ${r.value} @ ${hex8(start)}`
    );
  }

  return {
    value: n,
    next: r.next,
  };
}

function parseMemArg(start: number): {
  align: number;
  offset: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const alignR = readSLEB128(start);
  const offsetR = readSLEB128(alignR.next);

  return {
    align: Number(alignR.value),
    offset: Number(offsetR.value),
    next: offsetR.next,
  };
}

  switch (op) {

      console.log(`${hex8(at)}  i64.rotr`);

    case 0x37: {

    }

    case 0x0b: {
      console.log(`${hex8(at)}  end`);
      break;

      throw new Error(
        `UNKNOWN OPCODE ${hex8(op)} @ ${hex8(at)}`
      );

  }
