import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const FUNCTION_INDEX = 87;
const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const BODY_ORDINAL = FUNCTION_INDEX - IMPORT_COUNT;

// Function 87 body from previous verified mapping.
const start = 0x00007672;
const end = 0x000076e7;

const b = wasm.subarray(start, end);

function uleb(offset: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = offset;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb64(offset: number) {
  let value = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let p = offset;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = b[p++];
    value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7n;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function i64hex(v: bigint) {
  return "0x" + BigInt.asUintN(64, v).toString(16).padStart(16, "0");
}

    case 0x0b:

      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
      break;

      const x = uleb(p);

      text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;

      const align = uleb(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const align = uleb(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x37: {
      const align = uleb(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb64(p);
      p = x.next;
      text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb64(p);
      p = x.next;
      text = `i64.const ${x.value} (${i64hex(x.value)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x7c:
      text = "i64.add";
      break;

    case 0x7e:
      text = "i64.shr_s";
      break;

    case 0x83:
      text = "i64.and";
      break;

    case 0x84:
      text = "i64.or";
      break;

    case 0x88:
      text = "i64.shr_u";
      break;

    default:
      text = `UNKNOWN opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  if (text) {
    console.log(`${hex(addr)}  ${text}`);
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) break;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNCTION_INDEX = 87;
const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const BODY_ORDINAL = FUNCTION_INDEX - IMPORT_COUNT;

const start = 0x00007672;
const end = 0x000076e7;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const b = wasm.subarray(start, end);

function uleb(offset: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = offset;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(offset: number) {
  let value = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let p = offset;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = b[p++];

    value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    shift += 7n;
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
  }

  return {
    value,
    next: p,
  };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function hex64(v: bigint) {
  return (
    "0x" +
    BigInt.asUintN(64, v).toString(16).padStart(16, "0")
  );
}

let p = 0;

console.log("============================================================");

      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0")}`
  );

      break;

    // constants
    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);

      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      text =
        `i64.const ${x.value} ` +
        `(${hex64(x.value)})`;

      break;
    }

    // i64 arithmetic
    case 0x7c:
      text = "i64.add";
      break;

    case 0x7d:
      text = "i64.sub";
      break;

    case 0x7e:
      text = "i64.mul";
      break;

    case 0x7f:
      text = "i64.div_s";
      break;

    case 0x80:
      text = "i64.div_u";
      break;

    case 0x81:
      text = "i64.rem_s";
      break;

    case 0x82:
      text = "i64.rem_u";

    case 0x83:

      break;

    case 0x85:

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 8952;
const TARGET_TYPE = 4;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 8952 - FULL DISPATCH ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("FUNCTION  :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("TARGET TYPE:", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");


/*
 * ============================================================
 * BASIC READERS
 * ============================================================
 */

function readULEB(pos) {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {

        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                "ULEB overflow at 0x" +
                pos.toString(16)
            );
        }
    }

    return {
        value,
        next: p
    };
}


function readSLEB(pos) {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b;

    while (true) {

        b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
    };
}


function hex(v) {

    return "0x" +
        v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}


/*
 * ============================================================
 * SECTION MAP
 * ============================================================
 */

function readSectionHeader(pos) {

    const sectionStart = pos;

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);

    const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size;

    return {
        id,
        sectionStart,
        size,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd
    };
}


const sections = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const section = readSectionHeader(p);

    sections.push(section);

    p = section.payloadEnd;
}


const typeSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 1);

const importSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 2);

const functionSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 3);

const codeSection =
    sections.find(s => s.id === 10);


if (!typeSection) {
    throw new Error("TYPE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

if (!functionSection) {
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

if (!codeSection) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}


console.log("");
console.log("SECTION MAP");

console.log(
    "TYPE     :",
    hex(typeSection.payloadStart),
    "..",

    hex(functionSection.payloadEnd)
);

console.log(
    "CODE     :",
    hex(codeSection.payloadStart),
    "..",
    hex(codeSection.payloadEnd)
);

 * ============================================================
 * FUNCTION SECTION
 * ============================================================
 */

let fp = functionSection.payloadStart;

const functionCountInfo = readULEB(fp);

    functionCountInfo.value;

fp = functionCountInfo.next;

    "DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT :",
    definedFunctionCount
);


/*
 * ============================================================
 * IMPORT SECTION
 * ============================================================
 */

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

if (importSection) {

    let ip = importSection.payloadStart;

    const importCountInfo = readULEB(ip);

    const importCount =
        importCountInfo.value;

    ip = importCountInfo.next;

    console.log(
        "IMPORT COUNT :",
        importCount
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

        const moduleInfo = readULEB(ip);

        ip = moduleInfo.next + moduleInfo.value;


        const nameInfo = readULEB(ip);

        ip = nameInfo.next + nameInfo.value;


        const kind = buf[ip++];


        /*
         * Function import
         */

        if (kind === 0) {

            importedFunctionCount++;

            const typeInfo = readULEB(ip);

            ip = typeInfo.next;
        }


        /*
         * Table import
         */

        else if (kind === 1) {

            /*
             * elemtype + limits
             */

            ip++;

            const flags = buf[ip++];

            ip = readULEB(ip).next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                ip = readULEB(ip).next;
            }
        }


        /*
         * Memory import
         */

        else if (kind === 2) {

            const flags = buf[ip++];

            ip = readULEB(ip).next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                ip = readULEB(ip).next;
            }
        }


        /*
         * Global import
         */

        else if (kind === 3) {

            /*
             * valtype + mutability
             */

            ip += 2;
        }


        else {

            throw new Error(
                "Unknown import kind " +
                kind
            );
        }
    }
}


console.log(
    "IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :",
    importedFunctionCount
);

    TARGET_FUNC -
    importedFunctionCount;

    definedIndex
);


if (
    definedIndex < 0 ||
    definedIndex >= definedFunctionCount
) {

    throw new Error(
        "Target function outside defined range"
    );
}

            bodySize

        break;

/*

const names = {

    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",

    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",

    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",

    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",

    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

    0x30: "i64.load8_s",
    0x31: "i64.load8_u",

    0x32: "i64.load16_s",
    0x33: "i64.load16_u",

    0x34: "i64.load32_s",
    0x35: "i64.load32_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",

    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",

    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",

    0x3c: "i64.store8",
    0x3d: "i64.store16",
    0x3e: "i64.store32",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",

    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",

    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",

    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",

    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",

    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",

    0xd0: "ref.null",
    0xd2: "ref.func"
};


/*
 * ============================================================
 * INSTRUCTION DECODER
 * ============================================================
 */

function decodeInstruction(pos) {

    const start = pos;

    const op =
        buf[pos++];

    const name =
        names[op] ||
        ("opcode_0x" +
         op.toString(16));


    const operands = [];


    /*
     * local / global
     */

    if (
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24
    ) {

        const r =
            readULEB(pos);

        operands.push(
            r.value
        );

        pos = r.next;
    }


    /*
     * direct call
     */

    else if (op === 0x10) {

        const r =
            readULEB(pos);

        operands.push(
            r.value
        );

        pos = r.next;
    }


    /*
     * call_indirect
     *
     * typeidx
     * tableidx
     */

    else if (op === 0x11) {

        const type =
            readULEB(pos);

        pos = type.next;


        const table =
            readULEB(pos);

        pos = table.next;


        operands.push(
            "type=" +
            type.value,

            "table=" +
            table.value
        );
    }


    /*
     * Memory loads/stores
     *
     * memarg:
     *   align
     *   offset
     */

    else if (
        op >= 0x28 &&
        op <= 0x3e
    ) {

        const align =
            readULEB(pos);

        pos = align.next;


        const offset =
            readULEB(pos);

        pos = offset.next;


        operands.push(
            "align=" +
            align.value,

            "offset=" +
            offset.value
        );
    }


    /*
     * i32.const
     */

    else if (op === 0x41) {

        const r =
            readSLEB(pos);

        operands.push(
            r.value
        );

        pos = r.next;
    }


    /*
     * i64.const
     */

    else if (op === 0x42) {

        const r =
            readULEB(pos);

        operands.push(
            r.value
        );

        pos = r.next;
    }


    /*
     * block / loop / if
     */

    else if (
        op === 0x02 ||
        op === 0x03 ||
        op === 0x04
    ) {

        const blockType =
            buf[pos++];

        operands.push(
            "blocktype=0x" +
            blockType.toString(16)
        );
    }


    /*
     * branch
     */

    else if (
        op === 0x0c ||
        op === 0x0d
    ) {

        const r =
            readULEB(pos);

        operands.push(
            r.value
        );

        pos = r.next;
    }


    return {

        start,

        end: pos,

        op,

        name,

        operands
    };
}


/*
 * ============================================================
 * INSTRUCTION DECODE
 * ============================================================
 */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("============================================================");


let ip = q;

let instructionCount = 0;

const instructions = [];

const indirectCalls = [];

const directCalls = [];

const memoryAccesses = [];


while (
    ip < targetBody.bodyEnd
) {

    const ins =
        decodeInstruction(ip);


    instructions.push(ins);


    console.log(
        hex(ins.start),
        "  ",
        ins.name.padEnd(20),
        ins.operands.length
            ? ins.operands.join(" ")
            : ""
    );


    /*
     * call_indirect
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x11) {

        indirectCalls.push(ins);


        console.log(
            "        >>> CALL_INDIRECT FOUND"
        );


        console.log(
            "        OPERANDS:",
            ins.operands.join(" ")
        );
    }


    /*
     * direct call
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x10) {

        directCalls.push(ins);
    }


    /*
     * memory access
     */

    if (
        ins.op >= 0x28 &&
        ins.op <= 0x3e
    ) {

        memoryAccesses.push(ins);
    }


    ip = ins.end;

    instructionCount++;


    if (instructionCount > 10000) {

        throw new Error(
            "Instruction decoder runaway"
        );
    }
}

    instructionCount

        stack.push(

    }


    else if (
        ins.op >= 0x28 &&
        ins.op <= 0x35
    ) {

        const source =
            stack.pop() || "?";


        const offset =
            ins.operands.find(
                x => x.startsWith("offset=")
            );


        stack.push(
            source +
            "[" +
            offset +
            "]"
        );

    else if (ins.op === 0x11) {

        console.log("");

        console.log(
            "AT",
            hex(ins.start)
        );

        console.log(
            "STACK BEFORE CALL_INDIRECT:"
        );


        for (
            let s = 0;
            s < stack.length;
            s++
        ) {

            console.log(
                "   [" + s + "]",
                stack[s]
            );
        }


        console.log(
            "NOTE: final stack item is the table index."
        );
    }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x00008152;
const END   = 0x00008174;

function uleb(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

      return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
      };
    }
  }
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode() {

  let p = START;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNCTION 96 FC OPCODE TRACE");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`RANGE : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  local.get ${x.value}`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  local.set ${x.value}`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  local.tee ${x.value}`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = sleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.const ${x.value}`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.add`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x71) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.and`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x45) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.eqz`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x47) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  i32.ne`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x0f) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  return`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x0b) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  end`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x00) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  unreachable`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x04) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  if`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x02) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  block`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x0d) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  br_if ${x.value}`);
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hex(at)}  call ${x.value}`);
    }

    else if (op === 0xfc) {

      const sub = uleb(p);
      p = sub.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  0xfc subopcode=${sub.value} (0x${sub.value.toString(16)})`
      );

      // Decode common bulk-memory/table instructions.
      if (
        sub.value === 8 ||
        sub.value === 9 ||
        sub.value === 11 ||
        sub.value === 13
      ) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        console.log(
          `           immediate=${x.value}`
        );
      }

      else if (
        sub.value === 10 ||
        sub.value === 12 ||
        sub.value === 14
      ) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        const y = uleb(p);
        p = y.next;

        console.log(
          `           immediate=${x.value}, ${y.value}`
        );
      }

      else {

        console.log(
          "           UNKNOWN 0xfc SUBOPCODE"
        );

        console.log(
          `           RAW : ${bytes.subarray(at, Math.min(END, at + 8)).toString("hex")}`
        );
      }
    }

    else {
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)}  UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("END");
  console.log("============================================================");
}

decode();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeFunctions9129_9133.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const IMPORTED = 83;

const targets = [9129, 9130, 9132, 9133];

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findSection(id) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const sid = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

const code = findSection(10);

if (!code) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

let p = code.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let codeIndex = 0; codeIndex < count.value; codeIndex++) {

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

  const globalIndex = IMPORTED + codeIndex;

  if (targets.includes(globalIndex)) {
    bodies.set(globalIndex, {
      codeIndex,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd
    });
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

function opcodeName(op) {
  const names = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",

    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

    0x30: "i64.load8_s",
    0x31: "i64.load8_u",
    0x32: "i64.load16_s",
    0x33: "i64.load16_u",
    0x34: "i64.load32_s",
    0x35: "i64.load32_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",

    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",
    0x3c: "i64.store8",
    0x3d: "i64.store16",
    0x3e: "i64.store32",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x50: "i64.eqz",
    0x51: "i64.eq",
    0x52: "i64.ne",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x6d: "i32.div_s",
    0x6e: "i32.div_u",
    0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
    0x70: "i32.rem_u",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",

    0x7f: "i64.div_s",
    0x80: "i64.div_u",

    0x92: "f32.add",
    0x93: "f32.sub",
    0x94: "f32.mul",
    0x95: "f32.div",

    0xd0: "ref.null",
    0xd1: "ref.is_null",
    0xd2: "ref.func"
  };

  return names[op] || null;
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  while (true) {
    byte = buf[pos++];

    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(byte & 0x80)) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too long at " + hex(pos));
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value,
    next: pos
  };
}

function readInstruction(pos, end) {

  const start = pos;
  const op = buf[pos++];

  const name = opcodeName(op);

  if (!name) {
    return {
      start,
      end: pos,
      text: `UNKNOWN opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    };
  }

  let text = name;

  try {

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      text += " " + x.value;
    }

    else if (op === 0x42) {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      text += " " + x.value;
    }

    else if (op === 0x20 ||
             op === 0x21 ||
             op === 0x22 ||
             op === 0x23 ||
             op === 0x24) {

      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      text += " " + x.value;
    }

    else if (
      op >= 0x28 &&
      op <= 0x3e
    ) {
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(pos);
      pos = offset.next;

      text +=
        ` align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      text += " " + x.value;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = readULEB(pos);
      pos = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(pos);
      pos = table.next;

      text +=
        ` type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      text += " " + x.value;
    }

    else if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readULEB(pos);
      pos = n.next;

      const labels = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(pos);
        pos = x.next;
        labels.push(x.value);
      }

      text +=
        " " + labels.join(",");
    }

    else if (op === 0x02 ||
             op === 0x03 ||
             op === 0x04) {

      // block type
      const bt = buf[pos++];

      text +=
        ` type=0x${bt.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0xd0) {
      const x = buf[pos++];
      text +=
        ` type=0x${x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    }

    return {
      start,
      end: pos,
      text
    };

  } catch (e) {

    return {
      start,
      end: Math.min(pos + 1, end),
      text: "DECODE ERROR: " + e.message
    };
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTIONS 9129 / 9130 / 9132 / 9133");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

for (const fn of targets) {

  const b = bodies.get(fn);

  if (!b) {
    console.log(`FUNCTION ${fn} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNCTION ${fn}`);
  console.log(`CODE INDEX : ${b.codeIndex}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY       : ${hex(b.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(b.bodyEnd)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `SIZE       : ${b.bodyEnd - b.bodyStart} bytes`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = b.bodyStart;

  // Function body begins with local declarations.
  const localCountInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = localCountInfo.next;

  console.log(
    `LOCAL DECL GROUPS : ${localCountInfo.value}`
  );

  for (let i = 0; i < localCountInfo.value; i++) {

    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    const type = buf[p++];

    console.log(
      `  locals[${i}] count=${n.value} type=0x${type
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let instructions = 0;

  while (p < b.bodyEnd) {

    const ins = readInstruction(p, b.bodyEnd);

    console.log(
      hex(ins.start),
      ins.text
    );

    p = ins.end;
    instructions++;

    if (instructions > 10000) {
      console.log("SAFETY STOP");
      break;
    }
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FINAL : ${hex(p)}`);
  console.log(
    `EXPECTED : ${hex(b.bodyEnd)}`
  );
  console.log("");
}

FILE: scripts\decodeG208ExactV12.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x96600;
const END   = 0x9661c;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

};

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x000964d1;
const END   = 0x00096853;

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x00096653,
  0x0009670d,
  0x000967c8
]);

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);
  return { v, p };
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= ~0 << s;
  return { v, p };
}

function hx(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.p);
  return { align: a.v, off: o.v, p: o.p };
}

const name: Record<number,string> = {
  0x20:"local.get",
  0x21:"local.set",
  0x22:"local.tee",
  0x23:"global.get",
  0x24:"global.set",
  0x28:"i32.load",
  0x29:"i64.load",
  0x2d:"i32.load8_u",
  0x31:"i64.load8_u",
  0x37:"i64.store",
  0x41:"i32.const",
  0x42:"i64.const",
  0x46:"i32.eq",
  0x47:"i32.ne",
  0x6a:"i32.add",
  0x6b:"i32.sub",
  0x6c:"i32.mul",
  0x74:"i32.shl",
  0x7c:"i64.add",
  0x7d:"i64.sub",
  0x83:"i64.and",
  0x84:"i64.or",
  0x85:"i64.xor",
  0x88:"i64.shl",
  0x0b:"end"
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" G-528 PRODUCER RAW DECODER v6");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`Range: ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log("");

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let extra = "";

  try {
    switch (op) {

      case 0x20:
      case 0x21:
      case 0x22:
      case 0x23:
      case 0x24: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.p;
        extra = `${r.v}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28:
      case 0x29:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x37: {
        const m = memarg(p);
        p = m.p;
        extra = `align=${m.align} offset=${m.off}`;

        if (op === 0x37 && TARGETS.has(at)) {
          console.log("");
          console.log("============================================================");
          console.log(`TARGET STORE @ ${hx(at)}`);
          console.log("============================================================");
          console.log(`raw offset = ${m.off}`);
        }
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = sleb(p);
        p = r.p;
        extra = `${r.v}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.p;
        extra = `i64.const/leb(${r.v})`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x02:
      case 0x03:
      case 0x04: {
        extra = `blocktype=0x${b[p].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;
        p++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
      case 0x0b:
      case 0x46:
      case 0x47:
      case 0x6a:
      case 0x6b:
      case 0x6c:
      case 0x74:
      case 0x7c:
      case 0x7d:
      case 0x83:
      case 0x84:
      case 0x85:
      case 0x88:
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.p;
        extra = `function=${r.v}`;
        break;
      }

      default:
        console.log(
          `${hx(at)}  UNKNOWN  0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hx(at)}  ${(name[op] ?? "opcode").padEnd(16)} ${extra}`
    );

  } catch (e) {
    console.log(`${hx(at)}  DECODE ERROR`);
    break;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" V6 COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO NETWORK - NO TRANSACTION");

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let functionStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + z.v;

  if (id === 3) functionStart = start;
  if (id === 10) codeStart = start;

  p = end;
}

const IMPORTS = 83;
const GLOBAL_FN = 2827;
const BODY = GLOBAL_FN - IMPORTS;

// function type
p = functionStart;
const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.p;

let typeIndex = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < fc.v; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  p = x.p;

  if (i === BODY)
    typeIndex = x.v;
}

// code body
p = codeStart;
const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.p;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.v; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  p = x.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + x.v;

  if (i === BODY) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
  }

  p = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGETS = [0x95a47, 0x95d4f];

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

function decodeFrom(start: number, end: number) {
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      const names: Record<number,string> = {
        0x20:"local.get",
        0x21:"local.set",
        0x22:"local.tee",
        0x23:"global.get",
        0x24:"global.set"
      };

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${names[op]} ${r.v}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} i64.const ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(`${hex(at)} call ${r.v}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.p;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.p;

      const kind =
        op >= 0x36 ? "STORE" : "LOAD";

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${kind} op=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
      );

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`
      );

      if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.p;

        const c = uleb(p);
        p = c.p;
      }

      continue;
    }

    // common arithmetic/control
    if (
      op === 0x0b ||
      op === 0x05 ||
      op === 0x1a ||
      op === 0x6a ||
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x71 ||
      op === 0x72 ||
      op === 0x73 ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x75 ||
      op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0x7c ||
      op === 0x7d ||
      op === 0x7e ||
      op === 0x7f ||
      op === 0x80 ||
      op === 0x81 ||
      op === 0x82 ||
      op === 0x83 ||
      op === 0x84 ||
      op === 0x85 ||
      op === 0x86 ||
      op === 0x87 ||
      op === 0x88
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      if (b[p] === 0x40) {
        p++;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} block/loop/if blocktype=0x40`);
      } else {
        const r = sleb(p);
        p = r.p;
        console.log(
          `${hex(at)} block/loop/if blocktype=${r.v}`
        );
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${r.v}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
    );
  }
}

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`TARGET STORE @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  // Show 100 bytes before and 20 after.
  decodeFrom(target - 100, target + 20);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V25");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

function decodeFile(fileName: string, title: string) {
    const filePath = path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        fileName
    );

    const bocBase64 = fs.readFileSync(
        filePath,
        'utf8'
    ).trim();

    const root = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(bocBase64, 'base64')
    )[0];

    const rootSlice = root.beginParse();

    // Jetton Transfer
    const transferOpcode = rootSlice.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = rootSlice.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = rootSlice.loadCoins();
    const destination = rootSlice.loadAddress();
    const responseDestination = rootSlice.loadAddress();
    rootSlice.loadMaybeRef();
    const forwardTonAmount = rootSlice.loadCoins();
    const forwardPayloadExists = rootSlice.loadBit();

    const payload = rootSlice.loadRef();

    const s = payload.beginParse();

    const provideLpOpcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const routerWalletAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const excessesAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const inner = s.loadRef();

    const innerSlice = inner.beginParse();

    const minLpOut = innerSlice.loadCoins();
    const receiverAddress = innerSlice.loadAddress();
    const bothPositive = innerSlice.loadBit();
    const dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount =
        innerSlice.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log('==========================================');
    console.log(title);
    console.log('==========================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('FILE:', fileName);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== JETTON TRANSFER =====');

    console.log(
        'Transfer opcode:',
        '0x' +
            transferOpcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'Query ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Amount:',
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        destination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Response destination:',
        responseDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward TON amount:',
        forwardTonAmount.toString(),
        '=',
        Number(forwardTonAmount) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward payload:',
        forwardPayloadExists
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== PROVIDE LP =====');

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        '0x' +
            provideLpOpcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'Decimal:',
        provideLpOpcode
    );

    console.log(
        'Router wallet address:',
        routerWalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Refund address:',
        refundAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Excesses address:',
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== PROVIDE LP INNER DATA =====');

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Receiver:',
        receiverAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'Custom payload forward gas:',
        dexCustomPayloadForwardGasAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== CELL STRUCTURE =====');

    console.log(
        'Root bits:',
        root.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Root refs:',
        root.refs.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Provide LP payload bits:',
        payload.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Provide LP payload refs:',
        payload.refs.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Inner cell bits:',
        inner.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Inner cell refs:',
        inner.refs.length
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== BOC =====');

    console.log(
        'BOC length:',
        bocBase64.length
    );
}

    'initial_rooster_leg.boc',
    'ROOSTER LEG'
);

    'initial_pton_leg.boc',
    'pTON LEG'
);

console.log('');
console.log('==========================================');
console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');

FILE: scripts\decodeInternalTransferV22.ts

import { Cell, Slice } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BODY_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAVwAAqReNRRkAAAAAAAAAAFIiSmPsCAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDcFbeW8f';

function hex32(value: bigint | number): string {
    return '0x' + BigInt(value).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function printRemaining(slice: Slice) {
    console.log('\nRemaining bits:', slice.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs:', slice.remainingRefs);

    if (slice.remainingBits > 0) {
        try {
            const raw = slice.loadUintBig(slice.remainingBits);
            console.log(
                'Remaining bits as hex:',
                '0x' + raw.toString(16)
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log(
                'Could not read remaining bits as uint'
            );
        }
    }
}

function decode(cell: Cell) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 INTERNAL_TRANSFER 0x178d4519');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('\nCell bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Cell refs:', cell.refs.length);
    console.log('Cell hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // OP
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const op = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('\n1. OP');
    console.log('   hex:', hex32(op));
    console.log('   decimal:', op);

    if (op === 0x178d4519) {
        console.log('   OK: INTERNAL_TRANSFER');
    } else {
        console.log('   WARNING: unexpected opcode');
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // QUERY ID
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('\n2. QUERY_ID');
    console.log('   value:', queryId.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // AMOUNT
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('\n3. AMOUNT');
    console.log('   nano LP:', amount.toString());

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // FROM ADDRESS
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const fromAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('\n4. FROM_ADDRESS');
    console.log(
        '   address:',
        fromAddress?.toString() ?? 'null'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // RESPONSE ADDRESS
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const responseAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('\n5. RESPONSE_ADDRESS');
    console.log(
        '   address:',
        responseAddress?.toString() ?? 'null'
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // FORWARD TON AMOUNT
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('\n6. FORWARD_TON_AMOUNT');
    console.log('   nanoTON:', forwardTonAmount.toString());

    console.log(
        '   TON:',
        Number(forwardTonAmount) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // FORWARD PAYLOAD
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\n7. FORWARD_PAYLOAD');

    const payloadBit = s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        '   Either flag:',
        payloadBit ? 'reference (^Cell)' : 'inline (Cell)'
    );

    if (payloadBit) {
        console.log(
            '   refs remaining:',
            s.remainingRefs
        );

        if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
            const payload = s.loadRef();

            console.log(
                '   payload bits:',
                payload.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                '   payload refs:',
                payload.refs.length
            );

            console.log(
                '   payload BOC:',
                payload
                    .toBoc({ idx: false })
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        }
    } else {
        console.log(
            '   inline payload bits:',
            s.remainingBits
        );

        if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
            const payload = s.loadBits(s.remainingBits);

            console.log(
                '   inline payload:',
                payload.toString()
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('\nRemaining after decode:');
    console.log('   bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('   refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    printRemaining(s);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 INTERNAL TRANSFER DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC ANALYSIS');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(BODY_BOC, 'base64')
    );

    console.log('\nRoot cells:', cells.length);

    decode(cells[0]);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeKaIndirectTableV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';

const WASM =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const TARGETS = [107, 116, 122];

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

function readU32(pos: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32(pos: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[pos.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readU64(pos: { v: number }): bigint {
    let result = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[pos.v++];

        result |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result;
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }
}

function readBytes(pos: { v: number }, n: number): Uint8Array {
    const out = bytes.slice(pos.v, pos.v + n);
    pos.v += n;
    return out;
}

function skipLimits(pos: { v: number }) {
    const flags = bytes[pos.v++];

    readU32(pos);

    if (flags & 0x01) {
        readU32(pos);
    }
}

function readName(pos: { v: number }): string {
    const n = readU32(pos);

    const data = readBytes(pos, n);

    return new TextDecoder().decode(data);
}

interface FnBody {
    index: number;
    offset: number;
    size: number;
    body: Uint8Array;
}

const IMPORTS = 83;

const functions = new Map<number, FnBody>();

interface IndirectCall {
    functionIndex: number;
    offsetInBody: number;
    typeIndex: number;
}

const indirectCalls: IndirectCall[] = [];

function decodeCallIndirect(body: Uint8Array): {
    offset: number;
    typeIndex: number;
}[] {
    const result: {
        offset: number;
        typeIndex: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
        if (body[i] !== 0x11) {
            continue;
        }

        let p = i + 1;

        let typeIndex = 0;
        let shift = 0;

        while (p < body.length) {
            const b = body[p++];

            typeIndex |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

            if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
                break;
            }

            shift += 7;
        }

        // call_indirect has an immediate table index.
        // In the current MVP encoding this is normally 0.
        const tableIndex = body[p];

        result.push({
            offset: i,
            typeIndex: typeIndex >>> 0,
        });

        void tableIndex;
    }

    return result;
}

function parseSections() {
    const pos = { v: 8 };

    while (pos.v < bytes.length) {
        const sectionId = bytes[pos.v++];

        const sectionSize = readU32(pos);

        const payloadStart = pos.v;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

        console.log(
            `section ${sectionId} offset=${payloadStart} size=${sectionSize}`
        );

        // Import section
        if (sectionId === 2) {
            const count = readU32(pos);

            console.log(`  imports=${count}`);

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const module = readName(pos);
                const name = readName(pos);
                const kind = bytes[pos.v++];

                if (kind === 0) {
                    readU32(pos);
                } else if (kind === 1) {
                    const elementType = bytes[pos.v++];

                    void elementType;

                    skipLimits(pos);
                } else if (kind === 2) {
                    skipLimits(pos);
                } else if (kind === 3) {
                    pos.v += 2;
                } else {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Unknown import kind ${kind} at import ${i}`
                    );
                }

                if (i < 90) {
                    console.log(
                        `    #${i} ${module}.${name} kind=${kind}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        // Table section
        if (sectionId === 4) {
            const count = readU32(pos);

            console.log('');
            console.log('  ================= TABLE SECTION =================');

            console.log(`  table count=${count}`);

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const elementType = bytes[pos.v++];

                const before = pos.v;

                const flags = bytes[pos.v++];

                const minimum = readU32(pos);

                let maximum: number | undefined;

                if (flags & 0x01) {
                    maximum = readU32(pos);
                }

                console.log(
                    `  table[${i}] ` +
                    `elementType=0x${elementType.toString(16)} ` +
                    `flags=${flags} ` +
                    `min=${minimum}` +
                    (maximum !== undefined
                        ? ` max=${maximum}`
                        : '')
                );

                console.log(
                    `    descriptorBytes=${pos.v - before}`
                );
            }
        }

        // Element section
        if (sectionId === 9) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('  ================= ELEMENT SECTION =================');

            const count = readU32(pos);

            console.log(`  element segments=${count}`);

            for (let segment = 0; segment < count; segment++) {
                const segmentStart = pos.v;

                const flags = readU32(pos);

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `  segment[${segment}] ` +
                    `flags=${flags} ` +
                    `offset=${segmentStart}`
                );

                /*
                 * We handle the common active-table forms:
                 *
                 * flags 0:
                 *   table 0
                 *   offset expr
                 *   vec(funcidx)
                 *
                 * flags 2:
                 *   tableidx
                 *   offset expr
                 *   elemkind
                 *   vec(funcidx)
                 *
                 * Other forms are reported but not guessed.
                 */

                if (flags === 0) {
                    console.log('    table=0');

                    const opcode = bytes[pos.v++];

                    console.log(
                        `    offset expr first opcode=0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    // Common i32.const expression
                    if (opcode === 0x41) {
                        const value = readS32(pos);

                        console.log(`    offset=${value}`);
                    } else {
                        console.log(
                            '    non-i32.const offset expression'
                        );

                        // We cannot safely skip arbitrary expressions here.
                        // Stop parsing this segment.
                        pos.v = payloadEnd;
                        break;
                    }

                    const endOpcode = bytes[pos.v++];

                    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
                        console.log(
                            `    unexpected offset end=0x${endOpcode.toString(16)}`
                        );
                    }

                    const n = readU32(pos);

                    console.log(`    function count=${n}`);

                    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                        const fn = readU32(pos);

                        console.log(
                            `      table[${j}] = function ${fn}`
                        );
                    }
                } else if (flags === 2) {
                    const tableIndex = readU32(pos);

                    console.log(
                        `    tableIndex=${tableIndex}`
                    );

                    const opcode = bytes[pos.v++];

                    console.log(
                        `    offset expr first opcode=0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    if (opcode === 0x41) {
                        const value = readS32(pos);

                        console.log(`    offset=${value}`);
                    } else {
                        console.log(
                            '    non-i32.const offset expression'
                        );

                        pos.v = payloadEnd;
                        break;
                    }

                    const endOpcode = bytes[pos.v++];

                    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
                        console.log(
                            `    unexpected offset end=0x${endOpcode.toString(16)}`
                        );
                    }

                    const elemKind = bytes[pos.v++];

                    console.log(
                        `    elemkind=0x${elemKind.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    const n = readU32(pos);

                    console.log(`    function count=${n}`);

                    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                        const fn = readU32(pos);

                        console.log(
                            `      table[${j}] = function ${fn}`
                        );
                    }
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        `    unsupported element flags=${flags}`
                    );

                    pos.v = payloadEnd;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        // Code section
        if (sectionId === 10) {
            const count = readU32(pos);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `  code functions=${count}`
            );

            for (let defined = 0; defined < count; defined++) {
                const size = readU32(pos);

                const offset = pos.v;

                const body = readBytes(pos, size);

                const index = IMPORTS + defined;

                functions.set(index, {
                    index,
                    offset,
                    size,
                    body,
                });

                if (TARGETS.includes(index)) {
                    const calls = decodeCallIndirect(body);

                    for (const c of calls) {
                        indirectCalls.push({
                            functionIndex: index,
                            offsetInBody: c.offset,
                            typeIndex: c.typeIndex,
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        pos.v = payloadEnd;
    }
}

function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' WASM TABLE + ELEMENT + CALL_INDIRECT INSPECTOR v22');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(`WASM: ${WASM}`);
    console.log(`size: ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
    console.log('');

    parseSections();

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' TARGET CALL_INDIRECT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    for (const c of indirectCalls) {
        const fn = functions.get(c.functionIndex);

        console.log(
            `F#${c.functionIndex} ` +
            `offset=${fn?.offset ?? '?'} ` +
            `bodyOffset=${c.offsetInBody} ` +
            `typeIndex=${c.typeIndex}`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' DONE');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

main();

const ROOSTER_BODY =
    'te6cckEBAwEA+wABtA+KfqUAAAAAAAAAAGWvMQekAAgBJcKCNcqNEl5nZZFRPVIHIbH+mfdyL0yHcjzn7g37c6MAPGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3IHAOhgQEB4TfAlt+AEkQYMDTZ/VmiNvcexCcb43c5kFbdpMw6Xr9dxAln32QQA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDeAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bgAAAADVcWO/AAgBPUi8zardIAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvBNfZ+HI=';

const PTON_BODY =
    'te6cckEBAwEA0wABZAHzg10AAAAAAAAAAEDuVLkoAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvAQHhN8CW34ATx+W4S2lVi0em9t4WAONLV9rDl23ZaiL4mXY/VqMKbfADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N4AeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhuAAAAANVxY78ACAE9SLzNqt0gB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8E6/avkA==';

function hex(value: number | bigint, bytes = 4) {
    return '0x' + value.toString(16).padStart(bytes * 2, '0');
}

function printCell(cell: Cell, name: string) {
    console.log(`\n===== ${name} =====`);

    console.log('Bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', cell.refs.length);

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log('Opcode:', hex(opcode));
        console.log('Opcode decimal:', opcode.toString());
    }

    console.log('\n===== CELL TREE =====');

    function walk(c: Cell, level: number, path: string) {
        const indent = '  '.repeat(level);

        console.log(
            `${indent}${path}: bits=${c.bits.length}, refs=${c.refs.length}`
        );

        if (c.bits.length >= 32) {
            try {
                const ss = c.beginParse();
                const op = ss.loadUint(32);

                console.log(
                    `${indent}  first_uint32=${hex(op)} (${op.toString()})`
                );
            } catch {
                // ignore
            }
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < c.refs.length; i++) {
            walk(c.refs[i], level + 1, `${path}.ref[${i}]`);
        }
    }

    walk(cell, 0, 'root');
}

function decodeRooster(cell: Cell) {
    console.log('\n===== ROOSTER TRANSACTION FIELDS =====');

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = s.loadCoins();
    const destination = s.loadAddress();
    const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('Opcode:', hex(opcode));
    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());
    console.log('Amount nano:', amount.toString());
    console.log('Amount ROOSTER:', Number(amount) / 1e9);
    console.log('Destination:', destination?.toString());
    console.log('Response destination:', responseDestination?.toString());

    console.log('Remaining bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs:', s.remainingRefs);
}

export async function run() {
    const rooster = Cell.fromBase64(ROOSTER_BODY);
    const pton = Cell.fromBase64(PTON_BODY);

    printCell(rooster, 'ROOSTER BODY');
    printCell(pton, 'PTON BODY');

    decodeRooster(rooster);

    console.log('\n===== DONE =====');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const ROOSTER_BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101030100fb0001b40f8a7ea5000000000000000065af3107a400080125c28235ca8d125e676591513d520721b1fe99f7722f4c87723ce7ee0dfb73a3003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0dc81c03a1810101e137c096df801244183034d9fd59a236f71ec4271be377399056dda4cc3a5ebf5dc40967df641003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1b800000003559da46c002004f522f336ab74801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f0426e06378';

const PTON_BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101030100d300016401f3835d000000000000000043b9aca00801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f0101e137c096df8013c7e5b84b69558b47a6f6de1600e34b57dac3976dd96a22f899763f56a30a6df003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1b800000003559da46c002004f522f336ab74801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f044bf01448';

function inspectCell(
    name: string,
    cell: Cell
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(name);
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log(
        'Bits:',
        cell.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Refs:',
        cell.refs.length
    );

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            'Opcode:',
            '0x' +
            opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'Hash:',
        cell.hash().toString('hex')
    );

    console.log('');

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const ref = cell.refs[i];

        console.log(
            `REF ${i + 1}:`
        );

        console.log(
            '  Bits:',
            ref.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            '  Refs:',
            ref.refs.length
        );

        const refSlice =
            ref.beginParse();

        if (
            refSlice.remainingBits >= 32
        ) {
            const opcode =
                refSlice.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                '  Opcode:',
                '0x' +
                opcode
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(8, '0')
            );
        }

        console.log(
            '  Hash:',
            ref.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');

        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < ref.refs.length;
            j++
        ) {
            const child =
                ref.refs[j];

            console.log(
                `  REF ${i + 1}.${j + 1}:`
            );

            console.log(
                '    Bits:',
                child.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                '    Refs:',
                child.refs.length
            );

            const childSlice =
                child.beginParse();

            if (
                childSlice.remainingBits >= 32
            ) {
                const opcode =
                    childSlice.loadUint(32);

                console.log(
                    '    Opcode:',
                    '0x' +
                    opcode
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, '0')
                );
            }

            console.log(
                '    Hash:',
                child.hash().toString('hex')
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('      ROOSTER LIQUIDITY BODY DEEP DECODER');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    const roosterCell =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(
                ROOSTER_BOC,
                'hex'
            )
        )[0];

    const ptonCell =
        Cell.fromBoc(
            Buffer.from(
                PTON_BOC,
                'hex'
            )
        )[0];

    inspectCell(
        'ROOSTER BODY',
        roosterCell
    );

    inspectCell(
        'pTON BODY',
        ptonCell
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('                    END');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');
}
