import { Address, TonClient, Cell, Slice } from '@ton/ton';

const USER_WALLET =
    '0QDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6R-';

const ROOSTER_WALLET =
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000001';

const client = new TonClient({
    endpoint:
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
});

function addr(slice: Slice): string {
    try {
        return slice.loadAddress().toString();
    } catch {
        return 'ADDRESS_ERROR';
    }
}

function coins(slice: Slice): bigint {
    try {
        return slice.loadCoins();
    } catch {
        return 0n;
    }
}

function readOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);

    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function opcodeText(op: number | null): string {

    if (op === null) {
        return 'NONE';
    }

    const hex =
        '0x' +
        op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');

    let name = 'UNKNOWN';

    switch (hex) {

        case '0x0f8a7ea5':
            name = 'Jetton transfer';
            break;

        case '0x178d4519':
            name = 'Jetton internal_transfer';
            break;

        case '0x37c096df':
            name = 'STON.Fi provide_lp';
            break;

        case '0x01f3835d':
            name = 'pTON operation';
            break;

        case '0x7369676e':
            name = 'Wallet signature';
            break;
    }

    return `${hex}  (${name})`;
}

function dumpCell(
    title: string,
    cell: Cell,
    depth = 0
) {

    const indent =
        '  '.repeat(depth);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `${indent}${title}`
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}--------------------------------`
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}bits : ${cell.bits.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `${indent}refs : ${cell.refs.length}`
    );

    const op =
        readOpcode(cell);

    console.log(
        `${indent}opcode: ${opcodeText(op)}`
    );

    try {

        console.log(
            `${indent}BOC: ${cell
                .toBoc()
                .toString('base64')}`
        );

    } catch {
        console.log(
            `${indent}BOC: ERROR`
        );
    }

    if (depth >= 4) {
        return;
    }

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {

        dumpCell(
            `REF ${i + 1}`,
            cell.refs[i],
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

function decodeJettonTransfer(
    body: Cell
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'JETTON TRANSFER DECODE'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    const s =
        body.beginParse();

    const op =
        s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        `opcode       : ${opcodeText(op)}`
    );

    const queryId =
        s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        `query_id     : ${queryId.toString()}`
    );

    const amount =
        coins(s);

    console.log(
        `jetton amount: ${amount.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `display      : ${Number(amount) / 1e9} ROOSTER`
    );

    const destination =
        addr(s);

    console.log(
        `destination  : ${destination}`
    );

    const responseDestination =
        addr(s);

    console.log(
        `response dest: ${responseDestination}`
    );

    /*
     * custom_payload:
     *
     * Maybe (Either Cell ^Cell)
     */

    const hasCustomPayload =
        s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        `custom payload present: ${hasCustomPayload}`
    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {

        const customIsRef =
            s.loadBit();

        console.log(
            `custom payload is ref: ${customIsRef}`
        );

        if (customIsRef) {

            const custom =
                s.loadRef();

            dumpCell(
                'CUSTOM PAYLOAD',
                custom
            );

        } else {

            const custom =
                s.asCell();

            dumpCell(
                'CUSTOM PAYLOAD INLINE',
                custom
            );
        }
    }

    const forwardTonAmount =
        coins(s);

    console.log(
        `forward TON  : ${forwardTonAmount.toString()} nanotons`
    );

    console.log(
        `forward TON  : ${Number(forwardTonAmount) / 1e9} TON`
    );

    /*
     * forward_payload:
     *
     * Either Cell ^Cell
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'FORWARD PAYLOAD'
    );

    const forwardIsRef =
        s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        `forward payload is ref: ${forwardIsRef}`
    );

    let forwardPayload: Cell;

    if (forwardIsRef) {

        forwardPayload =
            s.loadRef();

    } else {

        forwardPayload =
            s.asCell();
    }

    dumpCell(
        'FORWARD PAYLOAD ROOT',
        forwardPayload
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'END JETTON TRANSFER DECODE'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}

function decodePtonMessage(
    body: Cell
) {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'PTON MESSAGE RAW DECODE'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    dumpCell(
        'PTON ROOT',
        body
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'The complete pTON body is preserved above.'
    );
}

export async function run() {

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        '       STON.FI V2.2 LIQUIDITY PAYLOAD DECODER'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        `TARGET LT: ${TARGET_LT}`
    );

    console.log('');

    const user =
        Address.parse(USER_WALLET);

    console.log(
        `Reading: ${user.toString()}`
    );

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            user,
            {
                limit: 10,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        `Transactions returned: ${transactions.length}`
    );

    const tx: any =
        transactions.find(
            (x: any) =>
                String(x.lt) === TARGET_LT
        );

    if (!tx) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND'
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND'
    );

    console.log(
        `LT: ${tx.lt}`
    );

    console.log(
        `HASH: ${Buffer.from(
            tx.hash()
        ).toString('base64')}`
    );

    if (!tx.outMessages) {

        console.log(
            'No outgoing messages.'
        );

        return;
    }

    let index = 1;

    for (
        const message
        of tx.outMessages.values()
    ) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `OUTGOING MESSAGE ${index}`
        );

        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        try {

            const info =
                message.info;

            const destination =
                info.dest?.toString() ??
                'unknown';

            const value =
                info.value?.coins ??
                0n;

            console.log(
                `destination: ${destination}`
            );

            console.log(
                `value      : ${value.toString()} nanotons`
            );

        } catch {
            // ignore
        }

        if (!message.body) {

            console.log(
                'No body.'
            );

            index++;
            continue;
        }

        const op =
            readOpcode(message.body);

        console.log(
            `opcode     : ${opcodeText(op)}`
        );

        if (
            op === 0x0f8a7ea5
        ) {

            decodeJettonTransfer(
                message.body
            );

        } else if (
            op === 0x01f3835d
        ) {

            decodePtonMessage(
                message.body
            );

        } else {

            dumpCell(
                'MESSAGE BODY',
                message.body
            );
        }

        index++;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'TRACE COMPLETE'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

export async function run() {
    const boc =
        'te6cckEBAwEAzwABZAHzg10AAAAAAAAAAEBfXhAIAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvAQHhN8CW34AEx3gN5WPFrXscgQQeOvvatValdjQXGwd/WUIYGRWYtNADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N4AeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhuAAAAANVpZ2MACAEcQGAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwQFVxRf';

    const root = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(boc, 'base64')
    )[0];

    function decode(cell: Cell, level: number) {
        const prefix = '  '.repeat(level);

        console.log(`${prefix}===== CELL ${level} =====`);
        console.log(`${prefix}Hash: ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`);
        console.log(`${prefix}Bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
        console.log(`${prefix}Refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);

        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
            try {
                console.log(
                    `${prefix}First uint32: 0x${s.loadUint(32).toString(16)}`
                );
            } catch {}
        }

        if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
            try {
                console.log(
                    `${prefix}Next uint64: ${s.loadUintBig(64).toString()}`
                );
            } catch {}
        }

        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            decode(cell.refs[i], level + 1);
        }
    }

    console.log('===== FULL LIQUIDITY TX DECODER =====');

    decode(root, 0);
}

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000014n;

const RPC =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 0x178d4519 FIELD DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LIVE TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: RPC,
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        LP_WALLET,
        { limit: 20 }
    );

    const tx = txs.find(
        x => BigInt(x.lt.toString()) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx || !tx.inMessage) {
        console.log('\nTARGET TX / IN MESSAGE NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    const body: any = tx.inMessage.body;
    const s = body.beginParse();

    console.log('\nLT:');
    console.log(tx.lt.toString());

    console.log('\nOPCODE:');
    console.log(
        '0x' +
        s.loadUint(32)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('\nQUERY ID:');
    console.log(
        s.loadUintBig(64).toString()
    );

    console.log('\nAMOUNT (loadCoins):');
    const amount = s.loadCoins();
    console.log(amount.toString());

    console.log('\nAMOUNT / 1e9:');
    console.log(
        (Number(amount) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
    );

    console.log('\nREMAINING BITS AFTER AMOUNT:');
    console.log(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('\nREMAINING REFS AFTER AMOUNT:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // Try next field as address
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const address1 = s.loadAddress();

        console.log('\nFIELD #1 AFTER AMOUNT: ADDRESS');
        console.log(address1?.toString() ?? 'null');

    } catch (e: any) {

        console.log('\nFIELD #1 AFTER AMOUNT: NOT ADDRESS');
        console.log(e?.message || String(e));
    }

    console.log('\nREMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('\nREMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // Try next field as address
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const address2 = s.loadAddress();

        console.log('\nFIELD #2 AFTER AMOUNT: ADDRESS');
        console.log(address2?.toString() ?? 'null');

    } catch (e: any) {

        console.log('\nFIELD #2 AFTER AMOUNT: NOT ADDRESS');
        console.log(e?.message || String(e));
    }

    console.log('\nREMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('\nREMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // Try remaining data as coins
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    try {

        const value = s.loadCoins();

        console.log('\nNEXT FIELD AS COINS:');
        console.log(value.toString());

    } catch (e: any) {

        console.log('\nNEXT FIELD IS NOT COINS');
        console.log(e?.message || String(e));
    }

    console.log('\nFINAL REMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('\nFINAL REMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(72));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000014n;

const RPC =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LIVE 0x178d4519 DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: RPC,
    });

    console.log('\nFetching target transaction...');

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        LP_WALLET,
        { limit: 20 }
    );

    const tx = txs.find(
        x => BigInt(x.lt.toString()) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    const msg: any = tx.inMessage;

    if (!msg) {
        console.log('\nNO INCOMING MESSAGE');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\nLT:');
    console.log(tx.lt.toString());

    console.log('\nHASH:');
    console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('\nSOURCE:');
    console.log(msg.info.src.toString());

    console.log('\nDESTINATION:');
    console.log(msg.info.dest.toString());

    console.log('\nTON VALUE:');
    console.log(msg.info.value.coins.toString());

    const body = msg.body;

    console.log('\nBODY BITS:');
    console.log(body.bits.length);

    console.log('\nBODY REFS:');
    console.log(body.refs.length);

    console.log('\nBODY BOC HEX FROM LIVE TRANSACTION:');
    console.log(body.toBoc().toString('hex'));

    const s = body.beginParse();

    console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(70));
    console.log('DECODE');
    console.log('-'.repeat(70));

    console.log('\nOPCODE:');
    console.log(
        '0x' +
        s.loadUint(32)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('\nQUERY ID:');
    console.log(
        s.loadUintBig(64).toString()
    );

    console.log('\nNEXT 128 BITS:');
    console.log(
        s.loadUintBig(128).toString()
    );

    console.log('\nREMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('\nREMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('LIVE DECODING COMPLETE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

FILE: scripts\decodeLiveRouterData.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
);

function printCell(name: string, cell: Cell, level = 0) {

    const pad = ' '.repeat(level * 2);

    console.log('');
    console.log(`${pad}===== ${name} =====`);
    console.log(`${pad}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`${pad}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log(`${pad}exotic: ${cell.isExotic}`);
    console.log(`${pad}type: ${cell.type}`);
    console.log(`${pad}hash: ${cell.hash().toString('hex')}`);

    console.log(
        `${pad}boc: ${cell.toBoc({ idx: false }).toString('base64')}`
    );

    try {
        console.log(
            `${pad}bits-string: ${cell.bits.toString()}`
        );
    } catch (e: any) {
        console.log(
            `${pad}bits-string: <unavailable>`
        );
    }

    cell.refs.forEach((ref, i) => {
        printCell(`${name}.ref[${i}]`, ref, level + 1);
    });
}

export async function run(provider: any) {

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('      LIVE ROUTER LIBRARY INSPECTION');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const result = await provider.provider(ROUTER).getState();

    if (result.state.type !== 'active') {
        console.log('Router is not active.');
        return;
    }

    const data = Cell.fromBoc(result.state.data)[0];

    console.log('===== LIVE ROOT DATA =====');
    console.log('bits:', data.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', data.refs.length);
    console.log('hash:', data.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== LIVE DATA TREE =====');

    printCell('ROOT', data);

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' LIBRARY CONTAINER CHECK');
    console.log('========================================');

    if (data.refs.length < 2) {
        console.log('ERROR: Router data has fewer than 2 refs.');
        return;
    }

    const container = data.refs[1];

    console.log('Container bits:', container.bits.length);
    console.log('Container refs:', container.refs.length);

    for (let i = 0; i < container.refs.length; i++) {

        const lib = container.refs[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(`===== LIVE LIBRARY ${i + 1} =====`);
        console.log('Cell hash:', lib.hash().toString('hex'));
        console.log('bits:', lib.bits.length);
        console.log('refs:', lib.refs.length);
        console.log('exotic:', lib.isExotic);
        console.log('type:', lib.type);

        console.log(
            'BOC:',
            lib.toBoc({ idx: false }).toString('base64')
        );

        try {
            console.log(
                'BITS:',
                lib.bits.toString()
            );
        } catch {
            console.log(
                'BITS: <unavailable>'
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('========================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeLocal13ExactV15.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x9652b;
const END   = 0x96566;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;

    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function sleb64(p: number) {
  let v = 0n;
  let s = 0n;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= BigInt(x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7n;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 64n && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (-1n) << s;

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

function blocktype(p: number) {
  const x = b[p];

  if (x === 0x40)
    return { text: "void", next: p + 1 };

  const r = sleb32(p);

  return {
    text: String(r.value),
    next: r.next
  };
}

const names: Record<number,string> = {

  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",

  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x42: "i64.const",

  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x29: "i64.load",

  0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

  0x46: "i32.eq",
  0x47: "i32.ne",

  0x49: "i32.lt_u",

  0x1b: "select",

  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",

  0x71: "i32.and",
  0x72: "i32.or",
  0x73: "i32.xor",

  0x74: "i32.shl",
  0x75: "i32.shr_s",
  0x76: "i32.shr_u",
  0x77: "i32.rotl",
  0x78: "i32.rotr",

  0x7c: "i64.add",
  0x7d: "i64.sub",
  0x7e: "i64.mul",

  0x83: "i64.and",
  0x85: "i64.xor",

  0xac: "i64.extend_i32_s",
  0xad: "i64.extend_i32_u"
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LOCAL13 EXACT PRODUCER V15");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Range: ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let name =
    names[op] ??
    `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")})`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb64(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d
  ) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra =
      ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x03) {
    const r = blocktype(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` blocktype=${r.text}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0xfc) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;

    const sub = r.value;

    if (sub === 10) {

      const dst = uleb(p);
      p = dst.next;

      const src = uleb(p);
      p = src.next;

      extra =
        ` memory.copy dst=${dst.value} src=${src.value}`;

      name = "memory.copy";
    }
    else {
      name += ` sub=${sub}`;
    }
  }

  console.log(
    `${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RAW BYTES");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = START; i < END; i += 16) {

  const e = Math.min(i + 16, END);

  const bytes: string[] = [];

  for (let j = i; j < e; j++)
    bytes.push(
      b[j].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")
    );

  console.log(`${hx(i)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log("Producer:");
console.log("  local13 = value generated immediately before");
console.log("  0x96565 local.set 13");
console.log();

console.log("Consumer:");
console.log("  0x965fa -> MEM[G-8] = local13");
console.log();

console.log("Then:");
console.log("  G208[i] =");
console.log("    (old_G208[i] XOR local13)");
console.log("    XOR");
console.log("    (G336[i] AND");
console.log("      (old_G208[i] XOR local13))");
console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V15");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeLpAccountAddLiquidityV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;

function formatInteger(value: bigint): string {
    return value
        .toString()
        .replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

function formatToken9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    if (fraction.length === 0) {
        return whole.toLocaleString();
    }

    return `${whole.toLocaleString()}.${fraction}`;
}

function getOpcode(body: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function decodeAddLiquidity(
    body: Cell,
    txLt: string
) {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        if (opcode !== OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
            return false;
        }

        /*
         * STON.fi v2.2 LP Account add_liquidity body:
         *
         * opcode
         * query_id
         * new_amount0
         * new_amount1
         * min_lp_out
         * fwd_amount
         * both_positive
         * to_user
         * custom_payload
         * additional_fields:
         *   refund_address
         *   excess_address
         */

        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

        const newAmount0 = s.loadCoins();

        const newAmount1 = s.loadCoins();

        const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();

        const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();

        const bothPositive = s.loadBit();

        const toUser = s.loadAddress();

        const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

        if (hasCustomPayload) {
            s.loadRef();
        }

        const additionalFields = s.loadRef();

        const a = additionalFields.beginParse();

        const refundAddress = a.loadAddress();

        const excessAddress = a.loadAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );
        console.log(
            ` ADD_LIQUIDITY DECODE — LT ${txLt}`
        );
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('OPCODE:');
        console.log(
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('QUERY ID:');
        console.log(queryId.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('NEW AMOUNT0 RAW:');
        console.log(formatInteger(newAmount0));

        console.log('');
        console.log('NEW AMOUNT0 TOKEN:');
        console.log(formatToken9(newAmount0));

        console.log('');
        console.log('NEW AMOUNT1 RAW:');
        console.log(formatInteger(newAmount1));

        console.log('');
        console.log('NEW AMOUNT1 TOKEN:');
        console.log(formatToken9(newAmount1));

        console.log('');
        console.log('MIN LP OUT:');
        console.log(formatInteger(minLpOut));

        console.log('');
        console.log('FWD AMOUNT:');
        console.log(formatInteger(fwdAmount));

        console.log('');
        console.log('BOTH POSITIVE:');
        console.log(
            bothPositive ? 'YES' : 'NO'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TO USER:');
        console.log(
            toUser
                ? toUser.toString()
                : 'NULL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('CUSTOM PAYLOAD:');
        console.log(
            hasCustomPayload ? 'YES' : 'NO'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('REFUND ADDRESS:');
        console.log(
            refundAddress
                ? refundAddress.toString()
                : 'NULL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('EXCESS ADDRESS:');
        console.log(
            excessAddress
                ? excessAddress.toString()
                : 'NULL'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('REMAINING ROOT BITS:');
        console.log(s.remainingBits);

        console.log('');
        console.log('REMAINING ROOT REFS:');
        console.log(s.remainingRefs);

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');

        return {
            newAmount0,
            newAmount1,
            minLpOut,
        };

    } catch (error) {

        console.log('');
        console.log('ADD_LIQUIDITY DECODE FAILED');

        console.log(
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(error)
        );

        return false;
    }
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' STON.fi v2.2 LP ACCOUNT ADD_LIQUIDITY DECODER'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT'
    );
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(LP_ACCOUNT.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching LP Account transactions...');

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            LP_ACCOUNT,
            {
                limit: 100,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRANSACTIONS FETCHED:');
    console.log(transactions.length);

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        if (!tx.inMessage) {
            continue;
        }

        if (
            tx.inMessage.info.type !==
            'internal'
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        const body =
            tx.inMessage.body;

        const opcode =
            getOpcode(body);

        if (opcode !== OP_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
            continue;
        }

        found++;

        decodeAddLiquidity(
            body,
            tx.lt.toString()
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log('ADD_LIQUIDITY MESSAGES FOUND:');
    console.log(found);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'DECODER COMPLETE'
    );

    console.log(
        'READ ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
}

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';

const LP_ACCOUNT = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000011n;

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';

    return (
        '0x' +
        op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );
}

function getOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function formatNano(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const fraction = (
        value % 1_000_000_000n
    )
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return fraction
        ? `${whole}.${fraction}`
        : whole.toString();
}

async function main() {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 LP ACCOUNT TX DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP ACCOUNT:');
    console.log(LP_ACCOUNT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching transactions...');

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            LP_ACCOUNT,
            {
                limit: 100,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        'Fetched:',
        transactions.length
    );

    for (const tx of transactions) {
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '================================================'
        );
        console.log(' TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
        console.log(
            '================================================'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(
            tx.hash().toString('base64')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
        console.log(tx.now.toString());

        // INPUT
        const input = tx.inMessage;

        if (input) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '================ INPUT ================'
            );

            console.log(
                'TYPE:',
                input.info.type
            );

            if (
                input.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'SOURCE:',
                    input.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'DESTINATION:',
                    input.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'VALUE:',
                    formatNano(
                        input.info.value.coins
                    ),
                    'TON'
                );
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log('BODY:');
            console.log(
                input.body.bits.length,
                'bits /',
                input.body.refs.length,
                'refs'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('BOC:');
            console.log(
                input.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );

            const op =
                getOpcode(input.body);

            console.log('');
            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(opcodeHex(op));
        }

        // OUTPUTS
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '================ OUTPUTS ================'
        );

        let index = 0;

        for (
            const output
            of tx.outMessages.values()
        ) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `---------- OUT #${index} ----------`
            );

            console.log(
                'TYPE:',
                output.info.type
            );

            if (
                output.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log(
                    'SOURCE:',
                    output.info.src.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'DESTINATION:',
                    output.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log(
                    'VALUE:',
                    formatNano(
                        output.info.value.coins
                    ),
                    'TON'
                );
            }

            const body =
                output.body;

            console.log('');
            console.log('BODY:');

            console.log(
                body.bits.length,
                'bits /',
                body.refs.length,
                'refs'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('BOC:');

            console.log(
                body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );

            const op =
                getOpcode(body);

            console.log('');
            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(
                opcodeHex(op)
            );

            index++;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '================================================'
        );
        console.log(
            ' READ ONLY COMPLETE'
        );
        console.log(
            ' NO TRANSACTION SENT'
        );
        console.log(
            '================================================'
        );

        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TARGET LT NOT FOUND'
    );
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error(
        'ERROR:',
        error
    );
});

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAgQABcVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAFF0h26AAECPJ8UQwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDdAEAhYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N6B+o0s';

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER =
    100000000000n;

const EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT =
    150109265n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 ADD LIQUIDITY BOC DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');

    const cell =
        Cell.fromBase64(BOC);

    const s =
        cell.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== HEADER =====');

    const opcode =
        s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' +
        opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    const queryId =
        s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log(
        'QUERY ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== AMOUNTS =====');

    const newAmount0 =
        s.loadCoins();

    const newAmount1 =
        s.loadCoins();

    const minLpOut =
        s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'NEW AMOUNT 0 RAW:',
        newAmount0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'NEW AMOUNT 1 RAW:',
        newAmount1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT RAW:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== NEXT FIELDS =====');

    console.log(
        'REMAINING BITS BEFORE NEXT:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING REFS BEFORE NEXT:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    /*
     * Read the next fields one by one.
     * If the exact STON.fi layout differs,
     * the script will stop here instead of
     * inventing a decode.
     */

    const fwdAmount =
        s.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT RAW:',
        fwdAmount.toString()
    );

    const bothPositive =
        s.loadBit();

    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        bothPositive
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== TO USER =====');

    const toUser =
        s.loadAddress();

    console.log(
        'TO USER:',
        toUser?.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== STATE AFTER TO USER =====');

    console.log(
        'REMAINING BITS:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REMAINING REFS:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== GUARDS =====');

    console.log(
        'OPCODE CORRECT:',
        opcode === 0x50c6a654
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT0 = 100 ROOSTER:',
        newAmount0 === EXPECTED_ROOSTER
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT1 = 0 pTON:',
        newAmount1 === 0n
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT = 150109265:',
        minLpOut === EXPECTED_MIN_LP_OUT
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DISPLAY =====');

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT0:',
        Number(newAmount0) / 1e9,
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT1:',
        Number(newAmount1) / 1e6,
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log(
        'FWD:',
        Number(fwdAmount) / 1e9,
        'TON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log(' TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
    console.log(' pTON LEG SENT: NO');
    console.log('================================================');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TARGET = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const EXPECTED_OWNER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const DATA_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEASwAAkYAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvAD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B/teWN17MZq1F0h26AAJ1XV7e';

function sameAddress(a: Address | null, b: Address): boolean {
    return !!a && a.equals(b);
}

async function main() {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP DESTINATION DATA SEQUENTIAL DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log();
    console.log('TARGET:');
    console.log(TARGET.toString());

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(DATA_BOC);
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('CELL');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('total bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // FIELD 1
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('FIELD 1: ADDRESS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const field1Start = 0;
    const field1 = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('start bit:', field1Start);
    console.log('end bit:', 267);
    console.log('address:', field1?.toString());

    if (sameAddress(field1, EXPECTED_OWNER)) {
        console.log('*** MATCH: KNOWN OWNER ***');
    } else {
        console.log('*** NOT THE EXPECTED OWNER ***');
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // FIELD 2
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('FIELD 2: ADDRESS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const field2Start = 267;
    const field2 = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('start bit:', field2Start);
    console.log('end bit:', 534);
    console.log('address:', field2?.toString());

    if (sameAddress(field2, EXPECTED_POOL)) {
        console.log('*** MATCH: KNOWN POOL ***');
    } else {
        console.log('*** NOT THE EXPECTED POOL ***');
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // FIELD 3
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('FIELD 3: REMAINING 48 BITS');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('bits before field 3:', s.remainingBits);

    if (s.remainingBits !== 48) {
        console.log(
            `WARNING: expected 48 remaining bits, got ${s.remainingBits}`
        );
    }

    const field3 = s.loadUintBig(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('field3 decimal:', field3.toString());
    console.log('field3 hex:', '0x' + field3.toString(16));

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // Interpret field 3 as possible amounts
    // ---------------------------------------------------------

    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('FIELD 3 INTERPRETATION');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(
        'as nano ROOSTER:',
        field3.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'as ROOSTER:',
        Number(field3) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    console.log(
        'target 7.219004800 nano:',
        7219004800n.toString()
    );

    if (field3 === 7219004800n) {
        console.log(
            '*** EXACT MATCH: 7.219004800 ROOSTER ***'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'field3 is NOT 7.219004800 ROOSTER'
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('FINAL STRUCTURE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log(
        'FIELD 1 [0..266]   =',
        field1?.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'FIELD 2 [267..533] =',
        field2?.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'FIELD 3 [534..581] =',
        field3.toString()
    );

    console.log();
    console.log('remaining bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('remaining refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    if (s.remainingBits === 0 && s.remainingRefs === 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(
            '*** COMPLETE CELL CONSUMED EXACTLY ***'
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main();
}

FILE: scripts\decodeLpInternalTransferV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101010100570000a9178d4519000000000000000052224a63ec0801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0dc15b796f1f';

function formatAmount(value: bigint, decimals = 9): string {
    const base = 10n ** BigInt(decimals);
    const whole = value / base;
    const fraction = (value % base)
        .toString()
        .padStart(decimals, '0');

    return `${whole}.${fraction}`;
}

function formatAddress(address: Address | null): string {
    if (!address) return 'null';

    return address.toString({
        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    });
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET INTERNAL TRANSFER DECODER');
    console.log(' opcode: 0x178d4519');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC DECODING');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    // IMPORTANT:
    // The supplied BOC is HEX, not Base64.
    const bocBytes = Buffer.from(BOC, 'hex');

    console.log('\nBOC HEX LENGTH:', BOC.length);
    console.log('BOC BYTE LENGTH:', bocBytes.length);
    console.log('BOC MAGIC:', bocBytes.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex'));

    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(bocBytes);

    console.log('BOC ROOT COUNT:', roots.length);

    const cell = roots[0];
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // HEADER
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('\nHEADER');
    console.log('Opcode :', '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'));
    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());

    if (opcode !== 0x178d4519) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected opcode: 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // amount
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('\nLP AMOUNT');
    console.log('Raw:', amount.toString());
    console.log('LP :', formatAmount(amount));

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // from_address
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const fromAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('\nFROM ADDRESS');
    console.log(formatAddress(fromAddress));

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // response_address
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const responseAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('\nRESPONSE ADDRESS');
    console.log(formatAddress(responseAddress));

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // forward_ton_amount
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('\nFORWARD TON AMOUNT');
    console.log('Raw:', forwardTonAmount.toString());
    console.log('TON:', formatAmount(forwardTonAmount));

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // remaining payload
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('\nFORWARD PAYLOAD');
    console.log('Remaining bits :', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Remaining refs :', s.remainingRefs);

    if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
        const bits = s.loadBits(s.remainingBits);

        console.log('Payload bits length:', bits.length);
        console.log('Payload bits:', bits.toString());
    }

    if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        let index = 0;

        while (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
            const ref = s.loadRef();

            console.log(`Payload ref [${index}] BOC:`);
            console.log(ref.toBoc().toString('base64'));

            index++;
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' DECODER COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeLpMetadataV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const CONTENT_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAZQAAxgFodHRwczovL21ldGEuc3Rvbi5maS9scC92MS8wOkY5MDBCNjgyQjg0QTg5RUNDOEJEQUE1NUNFMDNCMDQ3MzJCNEVBRjFFRDlGNjc5MDdGQjVFNThERDdCMzE5QUIuanNvblMBP1g=';

function readSnakeString(cell: Cell): string {
    let current = cell;
    let result = '';

    while (true) {
        const slice = current.beginParse();

        while (slice.remainingBits >= 8) {
            result += String.fromCharCode(slice.loadUint(8));
        }

        if (slice.remainingRefs === 0) {
            break;
        }

        current = slice.loadRef();
    }

    return result;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP METADATA DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(CONTENT_BOC, 'base64')
    )[0];

    console.log('CONTENT CELL:');
    console.log(CONTENT_BOC);
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOT CELL BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT CELL REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    );

FILE: scripts\decodeLpWalletGetterV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const OWNER_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAJAAAQ4AeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhvDrj0L0';

const MASTER_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAJAAAQ4AfIBbQVwlRPZkXtUq5wHYI5ladXj2z7PIP9ryxuvZjNXCskaCA';

function decodeAddress(boc: string): string {
    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc);
    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    const address = slice.loadAddress();

    if (address === null) {
        throw new Error('Address is null');
    }

    return address.toString({
        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    });
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET GETTER ADDRESS DECODER');

    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const balanceHex = 'fee15dd408';
    const balance = BigInt('0x' + balanceHex);

    console.log('\nLP BALANCE:');
    console.log('RAW:', balance.toString());
    console.log(
        'LP:',
        `${balance / 1_000_000_000n}.${(balance % 1_000_000_000n)
            .toString()
            .padStart(9, '0')}`
    );

    console.log('\n[1] DECODING OWNER...');
    const owner = decodeAddress(OWNER_BOC);
    console.log('OWNER:', owner);

    console.log('\n[2] DECODING MASTER / POOL...');
    const master = decodeAddress(MASTER_BOC);
    console.log('MASTER / POOL:', master);

    console.log('\nEXPECTED OWNER:');
    console.log(
        'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
    );

    console.log('\nEXPECTED POOL:');
    console.log(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' DECODER COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const LP_WALLET =
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SDOX';

const INIT_STATE_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAwEAbwACATQCAQGHCAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwA+QC2grhKiezIvapVzgOwRzK06vHtn2eQf7XljdezGauACCEICwAg2RA0ITkT7lDFhMqxaIUF+9PQp7gm1VgtWeLM0w+iLpNrD';

function readAddress(slice: any, label: string) {
    try {
        const addr = slice.loadAddress();

        console.log(`${label}:`);
        console.log('  raw     =', addr.toRawString());
        console.log(
            '  friendly =',
            addr.toString({
                urlSafe: true,
                bounceable: true,
                testOnly: true,
            })
        );

        return addr;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(`${label}: FAILED`);
        console.log(e);
        return null;
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP JETTON WALLET INIT DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP WALLET:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET);

    console.log('');
    console.log('INIT STATE BOC:');
    console.log(INIT_STATE_BOC);

    const root = Cell.fromBase64(INIT_STATE_BOC);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT CELL:');
    console.log('bits =', root.bits.length);
    console.log('refs =', root.refs.length);

    const s = root.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log('STATE INIT HEADER:');

    const splitDepth = s.loadMaybeRef();
    console.log(
        'split_depth ref:',
        splitDepth ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    const special = s.loadMaybeRef();
    console.log(
        'special ref:',
        special ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    const code = s.loadMaybeRef();
    const data = s.loadMaybeRef();

    console.log(
        'code ref:',
        code ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'data ref:',
        data ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    if (!data) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('ERROR: No data cell found.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('CODE CELL:');

    if (code) {
        console.log('code bits =', code.bits.length);
        console.log('code refs =', code.refs.length);
        console.log(
            'code hash =',
            code.hash().toString('hex')
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('DATA CELL:');
    console.log('data bits =', data.bits.length);
    console.log('data refs =', data.refs.length);

    console.log('');
    console.log('DATA HEX:');
    console.log(data.bits.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRY STANDARD JETTON WALLET DATA LAYOUT');
    console.log('(balance, owner, master, wallet_code)');

    try {
        const ds = data.beginParse();

        const balance = ds.loadCoins();

        console.log('');
        console.log('balance =', balance.toString());

        readAddress(ds, 'owner');

        readAddress(ds, 'master');

        if (ds.remainingRefs > 0) {
            const walletCode = ds.loadRef();

            console.log('');
            console.log('wallet_code:');
            console.log('  bits =', walletCode.bits.length);
            console.log('  refs =', walletCode.refs.length);
            console.log(
                '  hash =',
                walletCode.hash().toString('hex')
            );
        } else {
            console.log('');
            console.log('wallet_code ref: NONE');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('REMAINING:');
        console.log('bits =', ds.remainingBits);
        console.log('refs =', ds.remainingRefs);

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('STANDARD JETTON LAYOUT DECODE FAILED');
        console.log(e);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' END OF READ-ONLY LP WALLET DECODING');
    console.log('======================================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodeLpWalletMessageV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BODY_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEAVwAAqReNRRkAAAAAAAAAAFIiSmPsCAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDcFbeW8f';

function scanCell(cell: Cell, path: string) {
    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(`CELL ${path}`);
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', cell.refs.length);
    console.log('hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    if (cell.bits.length >= 32) {
        const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            '\nFIRST 32-BIT VALUE:',
            `0x${opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`,
            `(${opcode})`
        );

        if (opcode === 0x178d4519) {
            console.log('\n*** FOUND TARGET OPCODE 0x178d4519 ***');
        }

        if (cell.bits.length >= 96) {
            const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                'NEXT 64-BIT VALUE:',
                queryId.toString()
            );
        }
    }

    // Scan every possible 32-bit offset.
    // We reconstruct the bitstream using loadUint(1), avoiding
    // assumptions about byte alignment.

    const reader = cell.beginParse();
    const bits: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.bits.length; i++) {
        bits.push(reader.loadUint(1));
    }

    const target = 0x178d4519;

    let found = false;

    for (let offset = 0; offset <= bits.length - 32; offset++) {
        let value = 0;

        for (let j = 0; j < 32; j++) {
            value = value * 2 + bits[offset + j];
        }

        if (value === target) {
            console.log(
                `FOUND 0x178d4519 at bit offset ${offset}`
            );
            found = true;
        }
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('0x178d4519 not found in this cell.');
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        scanCell(cell.refs[i], `${path}.ref${i}`);
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET MESSAGE DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC ANALYSIS');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(BODY_BOC, 'base64')
    );

    console.log('\nBOC root cells:', cells.length);

    scanCell(cells[0], 'root');

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\decodeLpWalletOutgoingV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC =
    'te6cckEBAQEADgAAGNUydtsAAAAAAAAAAPfBmNw=';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP WALLET OUTGOING MESSAGE DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(BOC);
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('\nCell bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Cell refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('\nOPCODE');
    console.log('hex:', '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'));
    console.log('decimal:', opcode);

    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
        const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log('\nQUERY_ID');
        console.log('value:', queryId.toString());
    }

    console.log('\nREMAINING');
    console.log('bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
        console.log('\nRemaining bits as hex:');

        let value = 0n;
        const count = Math.min(s.remainingBits, 256);

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            value = (value << 1n) | BigInt(s.loadBit() ? 1 : 0);
        }

        console.log(
            value.toString(16).padStart(Math.ceil(count / 4), '0')
        );
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000010';

const URL =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 EXACT POOL ADD_LIQUIDITY MESSAGE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('\nPOOL:');
    console.log(POOL);

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT);

    const url =
        `${URL}?account=${encodeURIComponent(POOL)}&limit=20`;

    console.log('\nFETCHING V3 TRANSACTIONS...');

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        console.log('\nHTTP ERROR:', response.status);
        console.log(await response.text());
        return;
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    const transactions =
        json.transactions ?? [];

    console.log(
        `Transactions returned: ${transactions.length}`
    );

    const tx = transactions.find(
        (x: any) =>
            String(x.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log(
            '\nTARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND'
        );
        return;
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('LT:', tx.lt);
    console.log('HASH:', tx.hash);
    console.log('ABORTED:', tx.aborted);
    console.log('COMPUTE SUCCESS:', tx.compute_success);
    console.log('COMPUTE EXIT:', tx.compute_exit_code);
    console.log('ACTION SUCCESS:', tx.action_success);

    console.log('\nINCOMING MESSAGE');

    const incoming =
        tx.in_msg;

    if (!incoming) {
        console.log('No incoming message.');
    } else {
        console.log('Source:', incoming.source);
        console.log('Destination:', incoming.destination);
        console.log('Value:', incoming.value);
        console.log('Created LT:', incoming.created_lt);
        console.log('Opcode:', incoming.opcode);
        console.log('Decoded:', incoming.decoded);

        console.log('\nFULL INCOMING MESSAGE JSON:');
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(incoming, null, 2)
        );
    }

    console.log('\nOUTGOING MESSAGES');

    const outgoing =
        tx.out_msgs ?? [];

    console.log(
        'Count:',
        outgoing.length
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < outgoing.length; i++) {
        const msg = outgoing[i];

        console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(80));
        console.log(`MESSAGE #${i + 1}`);
        console.log('-'.repeat(80));

        console.log('Source:', msg.source);
        console.log('Destination:', msg.destination);
        console.log('Value:', msg.value);
        console.log('Created LT:', msg.created_lt);
        console.log('Opcode:', msg.opcode);
        console.log('Decoded:', msg.decoded);

        console.log('\nFULL MESSAGE JSON:');
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(msg, null, 2)
        );
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolAddLiquidityV22.ts
