import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAfwABbVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAAMD0DRAjyfFEMAPGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3QBAIWAHjPyYW2Lkr1x78cDfEhWZTyVUtGMOargKZ0Mm80XL4bwA8Z+TC2xcleuPfjgb4kKzKeSqloxhzVcBTOhk3mi5fDeJFURJA==';

function readCoins(slice: ReturnType<Cell['beginParse']>): bigint {
    return slice.loadCoins();
}

function readAddress(
    slice: ReturnType<Cell['beginParse']>
): string {

    const address = slice.loadAddress();

    return address
        ? address.toString()
        : 'null';
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ADD_LIQUIDITY EXACT TL-B DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Pool:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Opcode: 0x50c6a654');
    console.log('');

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(ADD_LIQUIDITY_BOC);

    console.log('ROOT CELL');
    console.log('Bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', cell.refs.length);

    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. opcode
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const opcode = slice.loadUint(32);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('1. OPCODE');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'Opcode:',
        `0x${opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. query_id
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('2. QUERY ID');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'query_id:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. new_amount0
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const newAmount0 = readCoins(slice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('3. NEW AMOUNT 0');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'new_amount0:',
        newAmount0.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. new_amount1
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const newAmount1 = readCoins(slice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('4. NEW AMOUNT 1');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'new_amount1:',
        newAmount1.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. min_lp_out
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const minLpOut = readCoins(slice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('5. MIN LP OUT');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'min_lp_out:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. fwd_amount
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const fwdAmount = readCoins(slice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('6. FORWARD AMOUNT');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'fwd_amount:',
        fwdAmount.toString()
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. both_positive
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const bothPositive = slice.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('7. BOTH POSITIVE');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'both_positive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 8. to_user
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const toUser = readAddress(slice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('8. TO USER');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'to_user:',
        toUser
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 9. custom_payload
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const hasCustomPayload = slice.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('9. CUSTOM PAYLOAD');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'present:',
        hasCustomPayload
    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {

        if (slice.remainingRefs === 0) {
            throw new Error(
                'custom_payload flag is true but no reference exists'
            );
        }

        const customPayload = slice.loadRef();

        console.log(
            'custom_payload bits:',
            customPayload.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'custom_payload refs:',
            customPayload.refs.length
        );

        console.log(
            'custom_payload BOC:',
            customPayload.toBoc({
                idx: false,
            }).toString('base64')
        );
    } else {

        console.log(
            'custom_payload: null'
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 10. additional_fields reference
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('10. ADDITIONAL FIELDS');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'remaining refs in root:',
        slice.remainingRefs
    );

    if (slice.remainingRefs === 0) {
        throw new Error(
            'Expected additional_fields reference but none exists'
        );
    }

    const additional = slice.loadRef();

    console.log(
        'additional bits:',
        additional.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'additional refs:',
        additional.refs.length
    );

    const additionalSlice = additional.beginParse();

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 11. refund_address
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const refundAddress = readAddress(additionalSlice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('11. REFUND ADDRESS');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'refund_address:',
        refundAddress
    );

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // 12. excess_address
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const excessAddress = readAddress(additionalSlice);

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('12. EXCESS ADDRESS');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'excess_address:',
        excessAddress
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('FINAL');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'Remaining root bits:',
        slice.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining root refs:',
        slice.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining additional bits:',
        additionalSlice.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining additional refs:',
        additionalSlice.remainingRefs
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolAddressesV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

function decodeAddress(label: string, boc: string) {

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(boc);

    console.log(label);
    console.log('bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('refs:', cell.refs.length);

    try {

        const slice = cell.beginParse();
        const address = slice.loadAddress();

        if (address) {
            console.log('Address:', address.toString());
        } else {
            console.log('Address: null');
        }

    } catch (error: any) {

        console.log(
            'Decode failed:',
            error?.message ?? error
        );
    }

    console.log('');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('===== DECODE POOL ADDRESSES =====');
    console.log('');

    decodeAddress(
        'ITEM 1',
        'te6cckEBAQEAJAAAQ4ASXCgjXKjRJeZ2WRUT1SByGx/pn3ci9Mh3I85+4N+3OjCGupPl'
    );

    decodeAddress(
        'ITEM 5',
        'te6cckEBAQEAJAAAQ4ATx+W4S2lVi0em9t4WAONLV9rDl23ZaiL4mXY/VqMKbfApHXAa'
    );

    decodeAddress(
        'ITEM 6',
        'te6cckEBAQEAJAAAQ4ASRBgwNNn9WaI29x7EJxvjdzmQVt2kzDpev13ECWffZBDChq0g'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolCallbackFieldsV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

function nanoToTon(value: bigint): string {
    return (Number(value) / 1e9).toFixed(9);
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY FIELD DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
            archival: true,
        },
    );

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        const msg: any = tx.inMessage;

        if (!msg) {
            console.log('No incoming message.');
            return;
        }

        const body = msg.body;

        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION');
        console.log('LT:', lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

        console.log('');
        console.log('SOURCE:');
        console.log(msg.info.src.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('DESTINATION:');
        console.log(msg.info.dest.toString());

        // ============================================================
        // ROOT
        // ============================================================

        const root = body.beginParse();

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 1. OPCODE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const opcode = root.loadUint(32);

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('1. OPCODE');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log(
            '0x' +
            opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 2. QUERY ID
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const queryId = root.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('2. QUERY ID');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log(queryId.toString());

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 3. TOTAL AMOUNT 0
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const totAm0 = root.loadCoins();

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('3. TOT_AM0');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log('raw:', totAm0.toString());
        console.log('TON equivalent:', nanoToTon(totAm0));

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 4. TOTAL AMOUNT 1
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const totAm1 = root.loadCoins();

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('4. TOT_AM1');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log('raw:', totAm1.toString());
        console.log('TON equivalent:', nanoToTon(totAm1));

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 5. USER ADDRESS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const userAddress = root.loadAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('5. USER_ADDRESS');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log(
            userAddress?.toString() ?? 'NONE'
        );

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 6. MIN LP OUT
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const minLpOut = root.loadCoins();

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('6. MIN_LP_OUT');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log('raw:', minLpOut.toString());
        console.log(
            'LP units:',
            (Number(minLpOut) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
        );

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 7. FWD AMOUNT
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const fwdAmount = root.loadCoins();

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('7. FWD_AMOUNT');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log('raw:', fwdAmount.toString());
        console.log('TON:', nanoToTon(fwdAmount));

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 8. CUSTOM PAYLOAD
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const hasCustomPayload = root.loadBit();

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('8. CUSTOM_PAYLOAD');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log(
            hasCustomPayload
                ? 'PRESENT'
                : 'NONE'
        );

        if (hasCustomPayload) {
            console.log(
                'A custom payload reference exists.'
            );
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // ROOT CHECK
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('ROOT AFTER DECODE');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log(
            'remaining bits:',
            root.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'remaining refs:',
            root.remainingRefs
        );

        // ============================================================
        // ADDITIONAL FIELDS REF
        // ============================================================

        const additional = root.loadRef();

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('9. ADDITIONAL FIELDS');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        console.log('');
        console.log('bits:', additional.bits.length);
        console.log('refs:', additional.refs.length);

        const additionalSlice = additional.beginParse();

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 9.1 TO USER ADDRESS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const toUserAddress =
            additionalSlice.loadAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('9.1 TO_USER_ADDRESS:');

        console.log(
            toUserAddress?.toString() ?? 'NONE'
        );

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 9.2 REFUND ADDRESS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const refundAddress =
            additionalSlice.loadAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('9.2 REFUND_ADDRESS:');

        console.log(
            refundAddress?.toString() ?? 'NONE'
        );

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // 9.3 EXCESS ADDRESS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const excessAddress =
            additionalSlice.loadAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('9.3 EXCESS_ADDRESS:');

        console.log(
            excessAddress?.toString() ?? 'NONE'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));
        console.log('ADDITIONAL REF CHECK');
        console.log('-'.repeat(78));

        console.log(
            'remaining bits:',
            additionalSlice.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'remaining refs:',
            additionalSlice.remainingRefs
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('CALLBACK DECODE COMPLETE');
        console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        return;
    }

    console.log(
        'Target LT not found:',
        TARGET_LT.toString()
    );
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolCallbackLiveV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LIVE CALLBACK DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
            archival: true,
        },
    );

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        found = true;

        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET FOUND');
        console.log('LT:', lt.toString());
        console.log(
            'HASH:',
            tx.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        const msg: any = tx.inMessage;

        if (!msg) {
            console.log('No incoming message.');
            return;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('SOURCE:');
        console.log(msg.info.src.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('DESTINATION:');
        console.log(msg.info.dest.toString());

        const body = msg.body;

        console.log('');
        console.log('BODY OPCODE:');

        const opcodeSlice = body.beginParse();

        const opcode = opcodeSlice.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            '0x' +
            opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOT BITS:');
        console.log(body.bits.length);

        console.log('');
        console.log('ROOT REFS:');
        console.log(body.refs.length);

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // READ QUERY ID
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const slice = body.beginParse();

        const op = slice.loadUint(32);
        const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);

        console.log('');
        console.log('OPCODE DECIMAL:');
        console.log(op.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('QUERY ID:');
        console.log(queryId.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('REMAINING ROOT BITS:');
        console.log(slice.remainingBits);

        console.log('');
        console.log('REMAINING ROOT REFS:');
        console.log(slice.remainingRefs);

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // ROOT BITS - READ ONLY
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (slice.remainingBits > 0) {

            const bits = slice.loadBits(
                slice.remainingBits
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('ROOT REMAINING BITS STRING:');
            console.log(bits.toString());
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // REF CELLS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('REFERENCE CELLS');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        body.refs.forEach((ref: any, index: number) => {

            console.log('');
            console.log(`REF[${index}]`);

            console.log(
                'bits:',
                ref.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                'refs:',
                ref.refs.length
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('BITS:');

            console.log(
                ref.bits.toString()
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('BOC BASE64:');

            console.log(
                ref.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );
        });

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LIVE BOC
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('LIVE BODY BOC');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        console.log(
            body.toBoc().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('LIVE BODY BOC LENGTH:');

        console.log(
            body.toBoc().length
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log('DONE - READ ONLY');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        return;
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Target LT not found:',
            TARGET_LT.toString()
        );
    }
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolCallbackV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC =
    'b5ee9c724101020100a700017906ecd52700000000000000006b60e887f16c4303d034801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86e811e4f8a2080100c9801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18f003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1bc049d92b3f';

function amount9(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;
    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${frac}`;
}

function addr(a: Address | null): string {
    if (!a) return 'null';

    return a.toString({
        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    });
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL cb_add_liquidity DECODER');
    console.log(' opcode: 0x06ecd527');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC DECODING');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const bytes = Buffer.from(BOC, 'hex');

    console.log('\nBOC MAGIC:', bytes.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex'));

    const cell = Cell.fromBoc(bytes)[0];
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('\nHEADER');
    console.log('Opcode :', '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'));
    console.log('Query ID:', queryId.toString());

    if (opcode !== 0x06ecd527) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected opcode: 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    // excess_address
    const excessAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('\nEXCESS ADDRESS');
    console.log(addr(excessAddress));

    // The callback contains the liquidity amounts
    // in the referenced payload structure.
    console.log('\nREMAINING CELL');
    console.log('Bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    while (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
        const ref = s.loadRef();

        console.log('\nREFERENCE BOC:');
        console.log(ref.toBoc().toString('base64'));

        console.log('Reference bits:', ref.bits.length);
        console.log('Reference refs:', ref.refs.length);
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' DECODER COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolCbAddLiquidityV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC_HEX =
    'b5ee9c724101030100a700017906ecd52700000000000000006b60e887f16c4303d034801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86e811e4f8a2080100c9801e33f2616d8b92bd71efc7037c4856653c9552d18c39aae0299d0c9bcd172f86f003c67e4c2db17257ae3df8e06f890acca792aa5a3187355c0533a19379a2e5f0de0078cfc985b62e4af5c7bf1c0df1215994f2554b4630e6ab80a674326f345cbe1bc049d92b3f';

const EXPECTED_OPCODE = 0x06ecd527;

function readCoins(slice: any): bigint {
    return slice.loadCoins();
}

function readMaybeRef(slice: any): any | null {
    const exists = slice.loadBit();
    return exists ? slice.loadRef() : null;
}

function readAddress(slice: any): Address {
    return slice.loadAddress();
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 CB_ADD_LIQUIDITY EXACT TL-B DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC DECODING');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Opcode:');
    console.log('0x' + EXPECTED_OPCODE.toString(16));

    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC SIZE:');
    console.log(`${Buffer.from(BOC_HEX, 'hex').length} bytes`);

    const cell = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(BOC_HEX, 'hex')
    )[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT CELL');
    console.log(`Bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    console.log(`Refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log('');
    console.log('--------------------------------------');
    console.log('HEADER');
    console.log('--------------------------------------');

    console.log('opcode:');
    console.log('0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'));

    if (opcode !== EXPECTED_OPCODE) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected opcode: 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('query_id:');
    console.log(queryId.toString());

    const amount0 = readCoins(s);

    console.log('amount0:');
    console.log(amount0.toString());

    const amount1 = readCoins(s);

    console.log('amount1:');
    console.log(amount1.toString());

    const userAddress = readAddress(s);

    console.log('user_address:');
    console.log(userAddress.toString());

    const minLpOut = readCoins(s);

    console.log('min_lp_out:');
    console.log(minLpOut.toString());

    const fwdAmount = readCoins(s);

    console.log('fwd_amount:');
    console.log(fwdAmount.toString());

    const customPayload = readMaybeRef(s);

    console.log('custom_payload:');
    console.log(customPayload ? 'present' : 'absent');

    console.log('');
    console.log('--------------------------------------');
    console.log('ADDITIONAL DATA REF');
    console.log('--------------------------------------');

    const additional = s.loadRef().beginParse();

    const toAddress = readAddress(additional);

    console.log('to_user_address:');
    console.log(toAddress.toString());

    const refundAddress = readAddress(additional);

    console.log('refund_address:');
    console.log(refundAddress.toString());

    const excessAddress = readAddress(additional);

    console.log('excess_address:');
    console.log(excessAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('--------------------------------------');
    console.log('REMAINING ROOT');
    console.log('--------------------------------------');

    console.log(`Remaining bits: ${s.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`Remaining refs: ${s.remainingRefs}`);

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log('DECODING COMPLETE');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolDataV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    const poolAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    const result = await provider.provider(poolAddress).get(
        'get_pool_data',
        []
    );

    console.log('===== DECODE V2.2 POOL DATA =====');
    console.log('Pool:', poolAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const stack = result.stack;

    // ITEM 0
    const item0 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 0:', item0.toString());

    // ITEM 1
    const item1 = stack.readCell();
    console.log('ITEM 1 CELL BOC:');
    console.log(item1.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    // ITEM 2
    const item2 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 2:', item2.toString());

    // ITEM 3
    const item3 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 3:', item3.toString());

    // ITEM 4
    const item4 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 4:', item4.toString());

    // ITEM 5
    const item5 = stack.readCell();
    console.log('ITEM 5 CELL BOC:');
    console.log(item5.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    // ITEM 6
    const item6 = stack.readCell();
    console.log('ITEM 6 CELL BOC:');
    console.log(item6.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    // ITEM 7
    const item7 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 7:', item7.toString());

    // ITEM 8
    const item8 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 8:', item8.toString());

    // ITEM 9
    const item9 = stack.readCell();
    console.log('ITEM 9 CELL BOC:');
    console.log(item9.toBoc().toString('base64'));
    console.log('');

    // ITEM 10
    const item10 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 10:', item10.toString());

    // ITEM 11
    const item11 = stack.readBigNumber();
    console.log('ITEM 11:', item11.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== DECODE FINISHED =====');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000012';

const OP_INTERNAL_TRANSFER = 0x178d4519;

function fmt(value: bigint): string {
    return value
        .toString()
        .replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

function getOpcode(body: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function decodeInternalTransfer(body: Cell) {
    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    if (opcode !== OP_INTERNAL_TRANSFER) {
        return;
    }

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    const fromAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const responseAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    let forwardPayload = 'NONE';

    if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
        const isRef = s.loadBit();

        if (isRef) {
            if (s.remainingRefs > 0) {
                const ref = s.loadRef();

                forwardPayload =
                    `REF: ${ref.bits.length} bits / ${ref.refs.length} refs`;
            }
        } else {
            forwardPayload = 'INLINE';
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' JETTON INTERNAL_TRANSFER DECODE');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('OPCODE:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('QUERY ID:');
    console.log(queryId.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP AMOUNT RAW:');
    console.log(fmt(amount));

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP AMOUNT:');
    console.log(
        fmt(amount)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('FROM:');
    console.log(
        fromAddress
            ? fromAddress.toString()
            : 'NULL'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('RESPONSE ADDRESS:');
    console.log(
        responseAddress
            ? responseAddress.toString()
            : 'NULL'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('FORWARD TON AMOUNT:');
    console.log(
        fmt(forwardTonAmount)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('FORWARD PAYLOAD:');
    console.log(forwardPayload);

    console.log('');
    console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
    console.log(s.remainingBits);

    console.log('');
    console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
    console.log(s.remainingRefs);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 POOL LP MINT DECODER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT);

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions =
        await client.getTransactions(
            POOL,
            {
                limit: 30,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('TRANSACTIONS FETCHED:');
    console.log(transactions.length);

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        if (
            tx.lt.toString() !==
            TARGET_LT
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');

        console.log('');
        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(
            tx.hash().toString('base64')
        );

        let index = 0;

        for (
            const outMessage
            of tx.outMessages.values()
        ) {

            index++;

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `OUTGOING MESSAGE #${index}`
            );

            if (
                outMessage.info.type ===
                'internal'
            ) {
                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(
                    outMessage.info.dest.toString()
                );
            }

            const opcode =
                getOpcode(
                    outMessage.body
                );

            console.log('');
            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(
                opcode === null
                    ? 'UNKNOWN'
                    : '0x' +
                      opcode
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, '0')
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('BODY:');
            console.log(
                `${outMessage.body.bits.length} bits / ${outMessage.body.refs.length} refs`
            );

            if (
                opcode ===
                OP_INTERNAL_TRANSFER
            ) {
                decodeInternalTransfer(
                    outMessage.body
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('DECODER COMPLETE');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Cell } from '@ton/core';

const BOC =
    'te6cckEBAwEA1gABaTfAlt8AAAAAAAAAAIAeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhu1r7oAQXYgQAQHhN8CW34ASRBgwNNn9WaI29x7EJxvjdzmQVt2kzDpev13ECWffZBADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N4AeM/JhbYuSvXHvxwN8SFZlPJVS0Yw5quApnQybzRcvhuAAAAANV0aUkACAE9UXtAar2gB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8EJqERng==';

function main() {
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 OFFLINE POOL MESSAGE DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO NETWORK - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('');

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(BOC);

    console.log('ROOT BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOT DATA:');

    if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            'OPCODE HEX:',
            '0x' + opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
        );

        console.log(
            'OPCODE DECIMAL:',
            opcode.toString()
        );
    }

    console.log('REMAINING BITS:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('REMAINING REFS:', s.remainingRefs);

    console.log('');
    console.log('RAW ROOT REFS:');

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = cell.refs[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(`REF ${i + 1}`);
        console.log('BITS:', ref.bits.length);
        console.log('REFS:', ref.refs.length);
        console.log('BOC:', ref.toBoc().toString('base64'));

        const rs = ref.beginParse();

        if (rs.remainingBits >= 32) {
            const op = rs.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                'FIRST 32 BITS:',
                '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
            );
        }

        console.log(
            'REMAINING BITS:',
            rs.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'REMAINING REFS:',
            rs.remainingRefs
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log(' END');
    console.log('==============================================');
}

main();

FILE: scripts\decodePoolMessageSchemaV22.ts

import { Address, Cell, Slice } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

function hex(n: bigint, bits: number): string {
    const width = Math.ceil(bits / 4);
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, '0');
}

function inspectSlice(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    depth = 0
) {
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));
    console.log(`CELL ${path}`);
    console.log(`Depth: ${depth}`);
    console.log(`Bits: ${s.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`Refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
    console.log('-'.repeat(78));

    /*
     * First 32 bits:
     * Almost all TON message bodies start with
     * an opcode / constructor identifier.
     */
    if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
        const op = s.loadUint(32);

        console.log(
            `opcode uint32 : ${op}`
        );

        console.log(
            `opcode hex    : 0x${hex(BigInt(op), 32)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `opcode signed : ${
                op > 0x7fffffff
                    ? op - 0x100000000
                    : op
            }`
        );
    }

    /*
     * Show the next standard-sized fields.
     *
     * We use independent slices so this diagnostic
     * never mutates the slice used by another test.
     */

    const tests = [
        { bits: 32, name: 'uint32' },
        { bits: 64, name: 'uint64' },
        { bits: 128, name: 'uint128' },
        { bits: 256, name: 'uint256' }
    ];

    for (const test of tests) {
        if (s.remainingBits < test.bits) {
            continue;
        }

        try {
            const x = cell.beginParse();

            /*
             * Skip opcode.
             */
            x.skip(32);

            if (x.remainingBits >= test.bits) {
                const value =
                    x.loadUintBig(test.bits);

                console.log(
                    `after opcode ${test.name}: ${value.toString()}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `after opcode ${test.name} hex: 0x${hex(
                        value,
                        test.bits
                    )}`
                );
            }
        } catch {
            // Diagnostic only.
        }
    }

    /*
     * Print byte-aligned windows after the opcode.
     * This is useful when fields are not the type
     * we initially assumed.
     */
    const offsets = [
        32, 40, 48, 56, 64, 72,
        80, 88, 96, 104, 112, 120,
        128, 136, 144, 152, 160,
        168, 176, 184, 192, 200, 208,
        216, 224, 232, 240, 248, 256,
        264, 272, 280, 288, 296,
        304, 312, 320
    ];

    console.log('');
    console.log('64-bit windows:');

    for (const offset of offsets) {
        if (offset + 64 > cell.bits.length) {
            continue;
        }

        try {
            const x = cell.beginParse();
            x.skip(offset);

            const value =
                x.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                `  @${offset}: ${value.toString()} ` +
                `(0x${hex(value, 64)})`
            );
        } catch {
            // Ignore invalid window.
        }
    }

    /*
     * Recursive refs.
     */
    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        inspectSlice(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`,
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

function tryCommonSchemas(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `SCHEMA TESTS FOR ${path}`
    );

    /*
     * Schema A:
     *
     * opcode:uint32
     * query_id:uint64
     */
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits >= 96) {
            const op = s.loadUint(32);
            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Schema A: opcode:uint32 + query_id:uint64'
            );
            console.log(
                `  opcode   = 0x${hex(BigInt(op), 32)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  query_id = ${queryId.toString()}`
            );
        }
    } catch {
        // Ignore.
    }

    /*
     * Schema B:
     *
     * opcode:uint32
     * query_id:uint64
     * amount:uint64
     */
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits >= 160) {
            const op = s.loadUint(32);
            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
            const amount = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Schema B: opcode:uint32 + query_id:uint64 + amount:uint64'
            );
            console.log(
                `  opcode   = 0x${hex(BigInt(op), 32)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  query_id = ${queryId.toString()}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  amount   = ${amount.toString()}`
            );
        }
    } catch {
        // Ignore.
    }

    /*
     * Schema C:
     *
     * opcode:uint32
     * query_id:uint64
     * amount:uint128
     */
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits >= 224) {
            const op = s.loadUint(32);
            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
            const amount = s.loadUintBig(128);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Schema C: opcode:uint32 + query_id:uint64 + amount:uint128'
            );
            console.log(
                `  opcode   = 0x${hex(BigInt(op), 32)}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  query_id = ${queryId.toString()}`
            );
            console.log(
                `  amount   = ${amount.toString()}`
            );
        }
    } catch {
        // Ignore.
    }
}

async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 POOL MESSAGE SCHEMA DECODER'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:', POOL.toString());

    const url =
        `${TONCENTER}` +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(POOL.toString())}` +
        `&limit=20`;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Requesting Pool transactions...'
    );

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter API error: ${JSON.stringify(json)}`
        );
    }

    const transactions = json.result ?? [];

    console.log(
        'Transactions returned:',
        transactions.length
    );

    /*
     * Only inspect a manageable number.
     *
     * The purpose here is schema identification,
     * not exhaustive historical scanning.
     */
    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx = transactions[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log(
            `TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`
        );
        console.log(
            'Hash:',
            tx.transaction_id?.hash
        );
        console.log(
            'UTIME:',
            tx.utime
        );
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        const inBody =
            tx.in_msg?.msg_data?.body;

        if (inBody) {
            try {
                const cell =
                    Cell.fromBase64(inBody);

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '--- IN MESSAGE ---'
                );

                inspectSlice(
                    cell,
                    `tx${i + 1}.in`
                );

                tryCommonSchemas(
                    cell,
                    `tx${i + 1}.in`
                );
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'IN decode error:',
                    String(e)
                );
            }
        }

        const outMsgs =
            tx.out_msgs ?? [];

        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < outMsgs.length;
            j++
        ) {
            const body =
                outMsgs[j]?.msg_data?.body;

            if (!body) {
                continue;
            }

            try {
                const cell =
                    Cell.fromBase64(body);

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `--- OUT MESSAGE #${j + 1} ---`
                );

                inspectSlice(
                    cell,
                    `tx${i + 1}.out${j + 1}`
                );

                tryCommonSchemas(
                    cell,
                    `tx${i + 1}.out${j + 1}`
                );
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'OUT decode error:',
                    String(e)
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(
        'SCHEMA DECODING FINISHED'
    );
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
}

export { run };

FILE: scripts\decodePoolOutgoingLT99809465000011V22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const POOL_TX_LT = 99809465000010n;
const TARGET_MESSAGE_LT = 99809465000011n;

const TONCENTER = 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function hex(n: bigint, bytes = 4) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(bytes * 2, '0');
}

function addr(a: any): string {
    try {
        return a?.toString?.() ?? String(a);
    } catch {
        return String(a);
    }
}

function dumpCell(cell: any, name: string, depth = 0) {
    const pad = ' '.repeat(depth * 2);

    console.log(`${pad}${name}`);

    try {
        console.log(`${pad}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(`${pad}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        const boc = cell.toBoc().toString('base64');
        console.log(`${pad}BOC(base64):`);
        console.log(`${pad}${boc}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        const slice = cell.beginParse();

        console.log(
            `${pad}remainingBits: ${slice.remainingBits}`
        );

        console.log(
            `${pad}remainingRefs: ${slice.remainingRefs}`
        );

        if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
            const op = slice.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                `${pad}uint32 opcode: ${hex(BigInt(op), 4)}`
            );

            if (slice.remainingBits >= 64) {
                const qid = slice.loadUintBig(64);

                console.log(
                    `${pad}uint64/query_id: ${qid}`
                );
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            `${pad}slice decode error: ${e}`
        );
    }

    try {
        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            dumpCell(
                cell.refs[i],
                `ref[${i}]`,
                depth + 1
            );
        }
    } catch {}
}

async function main(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL OUTGOING MESSAGE DECODER');
    console.log(' POOL TX LT: 99809465000010');
    console.log(' TARGET MESSAGE LT: 99809465000011');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TONCENTER,
    });

    console.log(`Pool: ${POOL.toString()}`);
    console.log(`Pool transaction LT: ${POOL_TX_LT}`);
    console.log(`Target message LT: ${TARGET_MESSAGE_LT}`);
    console.log('');

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(POOL, {
        limit: 20,
    });

    const tx = txs.find((t: any) => {
        try {
            return BigInt(t.lt) === POOL_TX_LT;
        } catch {
            return false;
        }
    });

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('POOL TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('POOL TRANSACTION FOUND');

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('POOL TRANSACTION');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(`LT: ${tx.lt}`);

    try {
        console.log(
            `hash: ${Buffer.from(tx.hash()).toString('base64')}`
        );
    } catch {}

    console.log('');

    const outgoing = tx.outMessages;

    if (!outgoing || outgoing.size === 0) {
        console.log('No outgoing messages.');
        return;
    }

    console.log(
        `Outgoing message count: ${outgoing.size}`
    );

    let index = 0;
    let found = false;

    for (const [, message] of outgoing) {
        const info: any = message.info;

        let createdLt: bigint | null = null;

        try {
            createdLt = BigInt(info.createdLt);
        } catch {}

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `OUTGOING MESSAGE #${index}`
        );

        console.log(
            `createdLt: ${createdLt ?? '<unavailable>'}`
        );

        if (createdLt !== TARGET_MESSAGE_LT) {
            console.log('Not target message.');
            index++;
            continue;
        }

        found = true;

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );
        console.log(
            'TARGET OUTGOING MESSAGE FOUND'
        );
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        try {
            console.log(`src: ${addr(info.src)}`);
        } catch {}

        try {
            console.log(`dest: ${addr(info.dest)}`);
        } catch {}

        try {
            console.log(
                `value: ${info.value.coins} nanoTON`
            );

            console.log(
                `value: ${(Number(info.value.coins) / 1e9).toFixed(9)} TON`
            );
        } catch {}

        try {
            console.log(
                `createdLt: ${info.createdLt}`
            );
        } catch {}

        try {
            console.log(
                `createdAt: ${info.createdAt}`
            );
        } catch {}

        console.log('');

        if (message.body) {
            dumpCell(
                message.body,
                'BODY'
            );
        } else {
            console.log('BODY: empty');
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '============================================================'
        );

        break;
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TARGET MESSAGE LT NOT FOUND INSIDE POOL TRANSACTION.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' DECODING COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main(provider);
}

FILE: scripts\decodePoolOutJettonV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const BOC =
    'te6cckEBAgEAgwABdVDGplQAAAAAAAAAAGtfdACC7EBUXtAar2DADxn5MLbFyV649+OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0AQCFgB4z8mFti5K9ce/HA3xIVmU8lVLRjDmq4CmdDJvNFy+G8APGfkwtsXJXrj344G+JCsynkqpaMYc1XAUzoZN5ouXw3rRLvn0=';

const ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x50c6a654;

function formatNano(value: bigint): string {
    return `${value.toString()} (${Number(value) / 1e9})`;
}

function printAddress(
    label: string,
    address: Address | null
) {
    console.log(
        `${label}:`,
        address?.toString() ?? 'null'
    );
}

export async function run(
    _provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 POOL OUT MESSAGE DECODER');
    console.log(' FULL ADD_LIQUIDITY DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
    console.log(' NO RPC');
    console.log(' NO WALLET');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('BOC LENGTH:', BOC.length);

    const cell = Cell.fromBase64(BOC);

    console.log('ROOT BITS:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('ROOT REFS:', cell.refs.length);

    console.log('');

    const s = cell.beginParse();

    if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
        throw new Error(
            'Not enough bits for opcode'
        );
    }

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        '0x' +
            opcode
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, '0')
    );

    console.log(
        'OPCODE DECIMAL:',
        opcode.toString()
    );

    if (opcode !== ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'WARNING: Opcode is not expected ADD_LIQUIDITY'
        );
        console.log(
            'EXPECTED:',
            '0x50c6a654'
        );
    } else {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'MESSAGE TYPE: STON.fi ADD_LIQUIDITY'
        );
    }

    if (s.remainingBits < 64) {
        throw new Error(
            'Not enough bits for query_id'
        );
    }

    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'QUERY ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('========== ROOT PAYLOAD ==========');

    /*
     * Based on the v2.2 ADD_LIQUIDITY layout:
     *
     * opcode
     * query_id
     * amount0
     * amount1
     * min_lp_out
     * fwd_amount
     * both_positive
     * to_address
     * custom_payload
     *
     * Additional fields are stored in a reference.
     */

    const amount0 = s.loadCoins();
    const amount1 = s.loadCoins();
    const minLpOut = s.loadCoins();
    const fwdAmount = s.loadCoins();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'AMOUNT0 RAW:',
        amount0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT0:',
        formatNano(amount0),
        'ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'AMOUNT1 RAW:',
        amount1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'AMOUNT1:',
        formatNano(amount1),
        'pTON'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT RAW:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'MIN LP OUT:',
        formatNano(minLpOut),
        'LP'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT RAW:',
        fwdAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'FWD AMOUNT:',
        formatNano(fwdAmount),
        'TON'
    );

    if (s.remainingBits < 1) {
        throw new Error(
            'Missing both_positive bit'
        );
    }

    const bothPositive = s.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'BOTH POSITIVE:',
        bothPositive
    );

    const toAddress = s.loadAddress();

    console.log('');
    printAddress(
        'TO ADDRESS',
        toAddress
    );

    if (s.remainingBits < 1) {
        throw new Error(
            'Missing custom_payload flag'
        );
    }

    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'CUSTOM PAYLOAD PRESENT:',
        hasCustomPayload
    );

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        console.log(
            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
            'PRESENT'
        );

        if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {
            throw new Error(
                'Custom payload flag is true but reference is missing'
            );
        }

        const customPayload = s.loadRef();

        console.log(
            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD BITS:',
            customPayload.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD REFS:',
            customPayload.refs.length
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'CUSTOM PAYLOAD:',
            'NONE'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOT REMAINING BITS:',
        s.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOT REMAINING REFS:',
        s.remainingRefs
    );

    /*
     * Additional fields
     */

    if (s.remainingRefs < 1) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'WARNING: additional_fields reference is missing'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
        console.log('================================================');

        return;
    }

    const additional = s.loadRef();

    console.log('');
    console.log('========== ADDITIONAL FIELDS ==========');

    console.log(
        'REF BITS:',
        additional.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'REF REFS:',
        additional.refs.length
    );

    const a = additional.beginParse();

    if (a.remainingBits < 267) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'WARNING: additional_fields reference is shorter than expected'
        );
    }

    let refundAddress: Address | null = null;
    let excessAddress: Address | null = null;

    try {
        refundAddress = a.loadAddress();
    } catch {
        console.log(
            'REFUND ADDRESS: decode failed'
        );
    }

    try {
        excessAddress = a.loadAddress();
    } catch {
        console.log(
            'EXCESS ADDRESS: decode failed'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    printAddress(
        'REFUND ADDRESS',
        refundAddress
    );

    printAddress(
        'EXCESS ADDRESS',
        excessAddress
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'REF REMAINING BITS:',
        a.remainingBits
    );

    console.log(
        'REF REMAINING REFS:',
        a.remainingRefs
    );

    /*
     * Root reference inspection
     *
     * This is useful because the currently supplied BOC
     * has exactly one root reference.
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('========== ROOT REFERENCE MAP ==========');

    console.log(
        'ROOT REFERENCE COUNT:',
        cell.refs.length
    );

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const ref = cell.refs[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `REF ${i + 1}`
        );

        console.log(
            'BITS:',
            ref.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'REFS:',
            ref.refs.length
        );

        const rs = ref.beginParse();

        if (rs.remainingBits >= 32) {
            const refOpcode =
                rs.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                'FIRST 32 BITS:',
                '0x' +
                    refOpcode
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, '0')
            );

            console.log(
                'FIRST 32 DECIMAL:',
                refOpcode.toString()
            );
        }

        if (rs.remainingBits >= 64) {
            const refQueryId =
                rs.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                'NEXT 64 BITS:',
                refQueryId.toString()
            );
        }

        console.log(
            'REMAINING BITS:',
            rs.remainingBits
        );

        console.log(
            'REMAINING REFS:',
            rs.remainingRefs
        );

        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < ref.refs.length;
            j++
        ) {
            const child = ref.refs[j];

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `  CHILD REF ${j + 1}`
            );

            console.log(
                '  BITS:',
                child.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                '  REFS:',
                child.refs.length
            );

            const cs = child.beginParse();

            if (cs.remainingBits >= 32) {
                const childOpcode =
                    cs.loadUint(32);

                console.log(
                    '  FIRST 32 BITS:',
                    '0x' +
                        childOpcode
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(8, '0')
                );
            }

            console.log(
                '  REMAINING BITS:',
                cs.remainingBits
            );

            console.log(
                '  REMAINING REFS:',
                cs.remainingRefs
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' END - READ ONLY');
    console.log('================================================');
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\decodePoolSchemaTreeV22.ts
