import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET = 7219004800n;
const TARGET_HEX = '00000001ae494580';

const OPS: Record<number, string> = {
    0x37c096df: 'PROVIDE_LP',
    0x50c6a654: 'ADD_LIQUIDITY',
    0x178d4519: 'LP/CALLBACK',
    0x0801e33f: 'ADDRESS/DATA',
    0x801e33f2: 'ADDRESS/DATA'
};

function indent(depth: number): string {
    return '  '.repeat(depth);
}

function toHex(value: bigint, bits: number): string {
    const digits = Math.ceil(bits / 4);
    return value.toString(16).padStart(digits, '0');
}

function scanLogicalFields(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    depth: number
) {
    const p = indent(depth);

    console.log(`${p}CELL ${path}`);
    console.log(
        `${p}bits=${cell.bits.length} refs=${cell.refs.length}`
    );

    /*
     * Root opcode
     */
    if (cell.bits.length >= 32) {
        const opcode = cell.beginParse().preloadUint(32);
        const opHex = opcode
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, '0');

        console.log(
            `${p}opcode=0x${opHex} ${
                OPS[opcode] ?? 'UNKNOWN'
            }`
        );
    }

    /*
     * Scan only byte-aligned positions.
     *
     * Important:
     * loadUintBig() is used here because TARGET is bigint.
     */
    const widths = [32, 64, 128, 256];

    for (const width of widths) {
        if (cell.bits.length < width) {
            continue;
        }

        for (
            let offset = 0;
            offset + width <= cell.bits.length;
            offset += 8
        ) {
            const s = cell.beginParse();

            if (offset > 0) {
                s.skip(offset);
            }

            const value = s.loadUintBig(width);

            /*
             * Exact target
             */
            if (value === TARGET) {
                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    `${p}*** TARGET FOUND ***`
                );
                console.log(
                    `${p}path   = ${path}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `${p}offset = ${offset} bits`
                );
                console.log(
                    `${p}width  = ${width}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `${p}decimal= ${value}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `${p}hex    = 0x${toHex(value, width)}`
                );
            }

            /*
             * Target bytes inside larger fields
             */
            if (width > 64) {
                const valueHex = toHex(
                    value,
                    width
                );

                if (
                    valueHex.includes(TARGET_HEX)
                ) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log(
                        `${p}*** TARGET BYTES INSIDE FIELD ***`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `${p}path   = ${path}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `${p}offset = ${offset} bits`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `${p}width  = ${width}`
                    );
                    console.log(
                        `${p}value  = 0x${valueHex}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /*
     * Follow every reference recursively.
     */
    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < cell.refs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        scanLogicalFields(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`,
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

function decodeKnownRoot(
    cell: Cell,
    label: string
) {
    if (cell.bits.length < 32) {
        return;
    }

    const s = cell.beginParse();
    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const opHex = opcode
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, '0');

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(label);
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log(`opcode: 0x${opHex}`);
    console.log(
        `name  : ${OPS[opcode] ?? 'UNKNOWN'}`
    );
    console.log(
        `bits  : ${cell.bits.length}`
    );
    console.log(
        `refs  : ${cell.refs.length}`
    );

    /*
     * We are NOT assigning semantic names to these fields yet.
     * They are only positional observations.
     */
    if (opcode === 0x50c6a654) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'ADD_LIQUIDITY ROOT'
        );

        console.log(
            `remaining bits after opcode: ${s.remainingBits}`
        );

        if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
            const field0 = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                `field[0] uint64 = ${field0}`
            );

            console.log(
                `field[0] hex     = 0x${toHex(
                    field0,
                    64
                )}`
            );
        }

        if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
            const field1 = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                `field[1] uint64 = ${field1}`
            );

            console.log(
                `field[1] hex     = 0x${toHex(
                    field1,
                    64
                )}`
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `refs remaining: ${s.remainingRefs}`
        );
    }

    if (opcode === 0x37c096df) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'PROVIDE_LP ROOT'
        );

        console.log(
            `remaining bits after opcode: ${s.remainingBits}`
        );

        if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
            const field0 = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                `field[0] uint64 = ${field0}`
            );

            console.log(
                `field[0] hex     = 0x${toHex(
                    field0,
                    64
                )}`
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `refs remaining: ${s.remainingRefs}`
        );
    }
}

async function getTransactions(
    address: string,
    limit = 20
) {
    const url =
        'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions' +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(address)}` +
        `&limit=${limit}`;

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}`
        );
    }

    const json = await response.json();

    if (
        !json.ok ||
        !Array.isArray(json.result)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'Invalid Toncenter response'
        );
    }

    return json.result;
}

function bodyToCell(
    body: any
): Cell | null {
    if (!body) {
        return null;
    }

    try {
        if (
            body['@type'] === 'tvm.cell'
        ) {
            return Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(
                    body.bytes,
                    'base64'
                )
            )[0];
        }

        if (
            typeof body === 'string'
        ) {
            return Cell.fromBoc(
                Buffer.from(
                    body,
                    'base64'
                )
            )[0];
        }
    } catch {
        return null;
    }

    return null;
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 POOL SCHEMA TREE DECODER'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Pool   : ${POOL.toString()}`
    );
    console.log(
        `Target : ${TARGET}`
    );
    console.log(
        `Hex    : 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Fetching Pool transactions from TON Center testnet...'
    );

    const txs = await getTransactions(
        POOL.toString(),
        20
    );

    console.log(
        `Transactions: ${txs.length}`
    );

    let interesting = 0;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < txs.length;
        i++
    ) {
        const tx = txs[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '#'.repeat(80)
        );

        console.log(
            `TRANSACTION ${i + 1}/${txs.length}`
        );

        console.log(
            '#'.repeat(80)
        );

        console.log(
            `hash : ${
                tx.transaction_id?.hash ?? '?'
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `lt   : ${
                tx.transaction_id?.lt ?? '?'
            }`
        );

        console.log(
            `utime: ${tx.utime ?? '?'}`
        );

        /*
         * IN MESSAGE
         */
        const inMsg = tx.in_msg;

        if (
            inMsg &&
            inMsg.body
        ) {
            const cell =
                bodyToCell(inMsg.body);

            if (cell) {
                const s =
                    cell.beginParse();

                if (
                    cell.bits.length >= 32
                ) {
                    const op =
                        s.preloadUint(32);

                    if (OPS[op]) {
                        interesting++;

                        decodeKnownRoot(
                            cell,
                            'IN MESSAGE'
                        );

                        console.log('');
                        console.log(
                            'REF TREE'
                        );

                        scanLogicalFields(
                            cell,
                            'in',
                            0
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        /*
         * OUT MESSAGES
         */
        const outMsgs =
            tx.out_msgs || [];

        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < outMsgs.length;
            j++
        ) {
            const msg =
                outMsgs[j];

            if (!msg?.body) {
                continue;
            }

            const cell =
                bodyToCell(msg.body);

            if (!cell) {
                continue;
            }

            const s =
                cell.beginParse();

            if (
                cell.bits.length < 32
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            const op =
                s.preloadUint(32);

            if (!OPS[op]) {
                continue;
            }

            interesting++;

            decodeKnownRoot(
                cell,
                `OUT MESSAGE #${j + 1}`
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'REF TREE'
            );

            scanLogicalFields(
                cell,
                `out[${j + 1}]`,
                0
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '='.repeat(80)
    );

    console.log(
        'FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        '='.repeat(80)
    );

    console.log(
        `Interesting messages: ${interesting}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target decimal       : ${TARGET}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target hex           : 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const DATA_BOC =
    'te6ccgEBBAEA0QABPTgWvT+kQO+iLLHK0C/3Cu6/iox7ajxoGENMoAAoABUBAsmAElwoI1yo0SXmdlkVE9Ugchsf6Z93IvTIdyPOfuDftzowAnj8twltKrFo9N7bwsAcaWr7WHLtuy1EXxMux+rUYU2+AEkQYMDTZ/VmiNvcexCcb43c5kFbdpMw6Xr9dxAln32QQAIDCEICwAg2RA0ITkT7lDFhMqxaIUF+9PQp7gm1VgtWeLM0w+gIQgLJWi7SKrUW93+dSJjcRXjnLxiiRI6PaDIzSwtL9QG8eQ==';

const TARGET = 7219004800n;

function printBits(cell: Cell) {
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('Bits:', s.remainingBits);
    console.log('Refs:', s.remainingRefs);

    if (s.remainingBits > 0) {
        const bits = s.loadBits(s.remainingBits);
        console.log('BITS HEX:');
        console.log(bits.toString());
    }
}

function scanUint(
    cell: Cell,
    maxBits: number
) {
    const s = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('UINT SCAN');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    for (
        let bits = 8;
        bits <= maxBits && bits <= s.remainingBits;
        bits += 8
    ) {
        try {
            const x = cell.beginParse().loadUintBig(bits);

            if (x === TARGET) {
                console.log(
                    `FOUND TARGET at ${bits} bits:`,
                    x.toString()
                );
            }

            if (
                x > 0n &&
                x < 10_000_000_000_000_000n
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `${bits} bits -> ${x.toString()}`
                );
            }
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
    }
}

function walk(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string
) {
    console.log('\n' + '-'.repeat(80));
    console.log('CELL:', path);
    console.log('-'.repeat(80));

    console.log('Bits:', cell.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', cell.refs.length);
    console.log('Hash:', cell.hash().toString('hex'));

    printBits(cell);

    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        walk(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`
        );
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL STATE DATA BOC DECODER');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE BOC');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('\nTARGET RAW:');
    console.log(TARGET.toString());

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(
        Buffer.from(DATA_BOC, 'base64')
    );

    console.log('\nROOT CELLS:', cells.length);

    const root = cells[0];

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('ROOT CELL');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('Bits:', root.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', root.refs.length);
    console.log(
        'Hash:',
        root.hash().toString('hex')
    );

    console.log('\nROOT BITS HEX:');

    const slice = root.beginParse();

    if (slice.remainingBits > 0) {
        const bits =
            slice.loadBits(slice.remainingBits);

        console.log(bits.toString());
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('COMMON UINT SCAN');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    scanUint(
        root,
        Math.min(root.bits.length, 256)
    );

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('CELL TREE');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    walk(root, 'root');

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET = 7219004800n;
const TARGET_HEX = '00000001ae494580';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/getTransactions';

function hexFromBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    return Array.from(bytes)
        .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join('');
}

function scanCell(
    cell: Cell,
    path: string,
    hits: string[],
    depth = 0
) {
    const slice = cell.beginParse();

    console.log('');
    console.log(`CELL ${path}`);
    console.log(`Depth: ${depth}`);
    console.log(`Bits remaining: ${slice.remainingBits}`);
    console.log(`Refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);

    /*
     * The important correction:
     * do NOT use BitString.toString().
     *
     * Instead, inspect every possible 64-bit window
     * directly through a fresh Slice.
     */

    const totalBits = slice.remainingBits;

    for (
        let offset = 0;
        offset + 64 <= totalBits;
        offset++
    ) {
        try {
            const s = cell.beginParse();

            if (offset > 0) {
                s.skip(offset);
            }

            const value = s.loadUintBig(64);

            if (value === TARGET) {
                const msg =
                    `FOUND TARGET uint64: ${path}, ` +
                    `bit offset=${offset}`;

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!'
                );
                console.log(msg);
                console.log(
                    `Value: ${value.toString()}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `Hex: 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
                );
                console.log(
                    '!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!'
                );

                hits.push(msg);
            }
        } catch {
            break;
        }
    }

    /*
     * Also inspect common aligned integer fields.
     */
    const alignedOffsets = [
        0, 8, 16, 24, 32, 40, 48, 56,
        64, 72, 80, 88, 96, 104, 112,
        120, 128, 136, 144, 152, 160
    ];

    for (const offset of alignedOffsets) {
        if (offset + 64 > totalBits) {
            continue;
        }

        try {
            const s = cell.beginParse();
            s.skip(offset);

            const value = s.loadUintBig(64);

            console.log(
                `  uint64 @ ${offset}: ${value.toString()}`
            );

            if (value === TARGET) {
                const msg =
                    `ALIGNED TARGET: ${path}, ` +
                    `bit offset=${offset}`;

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!'
                );
                console.log(msg);
                console.log(
                    `Value: ${value.toString()}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `Hex: 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
                );
                console.log(
                    '!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!'
                );

                hits.push(msg);
            }
        } catch {
            // Not enough bits.
        }
    }

    /*
     * Recursively inspect every reference.
     */
    for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
        scanCell(
            cell.refs[i],
            `${path}.ref[${i}]`,
            hits,
            depth + 1
        );
    }
}

async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 POOL BOC TARGET SEARCH'
    );
    console.log(
        ' TARGET: 7.219004800 ROOSTER'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log('');
    console.log('Pool:', POOL.toString());
    console.log(
        'Target nano:',
        TARGET.toString()
    );
    console.log(
        'Target hex:',
        `0x${TARGET_HEX}`
    );

    const url =
        `${TONCENTER}` +
        `?address=${encodeURIComponent(POOL.toString())}` +
        `&limit=100`;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Requesting Pool transactions...'
    );

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter HTTP ${response.status}: ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    if (!json.ok) {
        throw new Error(
            `Toncenter API error: ${JSON.stringify(json)}`
        );
    }

    const transactions = json.result ?? [];

    console.log(
        'Transactions returned:',
        transactions.length
    );

    const hits: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx = transactions[i];

        console.log('');
        console.log('='.repeat(78));
        console.log(
            `TRANSACTION #${i + 1}`
        );
        console.log(
            'Hash:',
            tx.transaction_id?.hash
        );
        console.log(
            'UTIME:',
            tx.utime
        );
        console.log('='.repeat(78));

        /*
         * IN BODY
         */
        const inBody =
            tx.in_msg?.msg_data?.body;

        if (inBody) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                '--- IN MESSAGE BODY ---'
            );

            try {
                const cell =
                    Cell.fromBase64(inBody);

                scanCell(
                    cell,
                    `tx${i + 1}.in`,
                    hits
                );
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'IN decode error:',
                    String(e)
                );
            }
        }

        /*
         * OUT BODIES
         */
        const outMsgs =
            tx.out_msgs ?? [];

        for (
            let j = 0;
            j < outMsgs.length;
            j++
        ) {
            const body =
                outMsgs[j]?.msg_data?.body;

            if (!body) {
                continue;
            }

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `--- OUT MESSAGE #${j + 1} BODY ---`
            );

            try {
                const cell =
                    Cell.fromBase64(body);

                scanCell(
                    cell,
                    `tx${i + 1}.out${j + 1}`,
                    hits
                );
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'OUT decode error:',
                    String(e)
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(
        'FINAL SEARCH RESULT'
    );
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    console.log(
        `Target: ${TARGET.toString()}`
    );
    console.log(
        `Target hex: 0x${TARGET_HEX}`
    );

    if (hits.length === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'No uint64 target found.'
        );
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Next step: decode the actual STON.fi'
        );
        console.log(
            'v2.2 message schemas and their refs.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            `TARGET HITS: ${hits.length}`
        );

        for (const hit of hits) {
            console.log(hit);
        }
    }
}

export { run };

FILE: scripts\decodePoolToLpTransferV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

const INTERNAL_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x178d4519;

function nanoToTon(value: bigint): string {
    return (Number(value) / 1e9).toFixed(9);
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {

    console.log('='.repeat(78));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL -> LP WALLET TRANSFER DECODER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(78));

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 20,
            archival: true,
        },
    );

    for (const tx of transactions) {

        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION');
        console.log('LT:', lt.toString());

        console.log('');
        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('hex'));

        const outMessages: any = tx.outMessages;

        if (!outMessages || outMessages.size === 0) {
            console.log('No outgoing messages.');
            return;
        }

        let found = false;

        for (const [key, msg] of outMessages) {

            const destination =
                msg?.info?.dest?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE';

            const body = msg.body;

            const slice = body.beginParse();

            let opcode = 0;

            try {
                if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
                    opcode = slice.loadUint(32);
                }
            } catch {
                opcode = 0;
            }

            const opcodeHex =
                '0x' +
                opcode.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');

            if (
                destination !== LP_WALLET.toString() &&
                opcode !== INTERNAL_TRANSFER_OPCODE
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            found = true;

            console.log('');
            console.log('='.repeat(78));
            console.log('POOL -> LP WALLET MESSAGE');
            console.log('='.repeat(78));

            console.log('');
            console.log('Dictionary key:');
            console.log(key);

            console.log('');
            console.log('Source:');
            console.log(
                msg.info?.src?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('Destination:');
            console.log(destination);

            console.log('');
            console.log('TON value:');
            console.log(
                nanoToTon(
                    BigInt(msg.info?.value?.coins ?? 0)
                )
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('Opcode:');
            console.log(opcodeHex);

            console.log('');
            console.log('BODY BOC HEX:');
            console.log(
                body.toBoc().toString('hex')
            );

            // ========================================================
            // DECODE INTERNAL TRANSFER
            // ========================================================

            const s = body.beginParse();

            const op = s.loadUint(32);
            const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
            const amount = s.loadCoins();
            const from = s.loadAddress();
            const response = s.loadAddress();
            const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

            console.log('');
            console.log('-'.repeat(78));
            console.log('DECODED INTERNAL TRANSFER');
            console.log('-'.repeat(78));

            console.log('');
            console.log('1. OPCODE:');
            console.log(
                '0x' +
                op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('2. QUERY ID:');
            console.log(queryId.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('3. AMOUNT RAW:');
            console.log(amount.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('4. AMOUNT / 1e9:');
            console.log(
                (Number(amount) / 1e9).toFixed(9)
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('5. FROM:');
            console.log(
                from?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('6. RESPONSE ADDRESS:');
            console.log(
                response?.toString?.() ?? 'NONE'
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('7. FORWARD TON AMOUNT:');
            console.log(forwardTonAmount.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('8. FORWARD TON:');
            console.log(
                nanoToTon(forwardTonAmount)
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
            console.log(s.remainingBits);

            console.log('');
            console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
            console.log(s.remainingRefs);

            // ========================================================
            // VERIFY DESTINATION
            // ========================================================

            console.log('');
            console.log('-'.repeat(78));
            console.log('ADDRESS CHECK');
            console.log('-'.repeat(78));

            console.log('');
            console.log('Expected LP Wallet:');
            console.log(LP_WALLET.toString());

            console.log('');
            console.log('Actual destination:');
            console.log(destination);

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Destination matches LP Wallet:',
                destination === LP_WALLET.toString()
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('='.repeat(78));
            console.log('DECODE COMPLETE');
            console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
            console.log('='.repeat(78));

            return;
        }

        if (!found) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'No Pool -> LP Wallet internal_transfer found.'
            );
        }

        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Target LT not found:',
        TARGET_LT.toString()
    );
}
